# Changelog

## [Unreleased]
- **Git auto-permission: `-c` wrapper handling, dangerous config blocking, prompt 1.7 + temp-dir + backup cap (2026-08-30)** — `IsHarmfulBashCommand` and the allowlist previously ignored `git -c` wrappers, so `git -c protocol.ext.allow=always ls-remote ext::...` could auto-allow and shell out via `ext::`. Fix splits concerns: the code allowlist (`internal/agent/permissions.go:bashSubcommandAllow`, via `gitSubcommandIndex`) intentionally returns -1 for any `-c` wrapper so `git -c ... status` is *not* auto-allowed by code and falls through to the LLM prompt; `BundledAutoPermissionPromptBody` (bumped `1.5.0` → `1.7.0`) documents that repeated `-c`/`-C`/`--git-dir`/global flags are transparent for read-only subcommands. Harmful detection remains `-c`-aware via `gitSubcommandIndexSkippingC` so `git -c k=v reset --hard` is still blocked, and new `isDangerousGitConfigKey`/`hasDangerousGitConfig` mark wrappers with keys like `protocol.*.allow`, `core.sshCommand`/`pager`/`editor`/`hooksPath`, `filter.*`, `url.*.insteadOf`, `credential.*` as harmful (require human approval). Also flips the OS temp-dir policy from deny to allow (`/tmp`, `/var/tmp`, `$TMPDIR`, `os.TempDir()` and children, any operation) and caps `auto-permission-prompt.md.bak.*` at 5 (`pruneAutoPermissionPromptBackups`, was unbounded — 77 backups observed). New gotchas document the `ext::` bypass and the journal DB pool leak. Tests: `auto_permission_prompt` prune, `permissions` dangerous-config and wrapper cases. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/config/auto_permission_prompt.go`, `internal/config/auto_permission_prompt_test.go`, `docs/gotchas/git-ext-transport-auto-allow-bypass.md`, `docs/gotchas/journal-db-connection-pool-leak.md`)
- **Server CORS late-route fix + web/desktop polish (2026-08-30)** — `Server.WithCORS` wrapped `srv.mux` at startup, so routes registered *after* it (e.g. scheduler's `/api/cron` via `SetScheduler`) escaped the CORS middleware. Removed `apiCORS`/`WithCORS`; `Run` now serves via `Server.Serve(ln)` which applies `corsMiddleware` per-request so late routes are covered. Test `TestServeUsesCORSForLateSchedulerRoutes` pins it. Web: `App.tsx` hides the chat StatusBar when the terminal is focused; `ChatPanel` re-pins to bottom via `ResizeObserver` + rAF when a hidden tab (display:none) becomes visible again (fixes landing at top after a stream finished in background); `fileLinks.tsx` now linkifies `.ocode/uploads/` image paths (with spaces including NBSP/U+202F) without turning bare `shot.png` in prose into a link, and `FileLink` uses the `file-link` hook so colored surfaces (user bubbles, inline code) override the prose link color; `sessionEvents.ts` flush comments expanded, `useTheme.ts` guards `hexToRgb` against non-string and filters malformed candidates before contrast checks and derives a prose link color that clears AA on both muted and page surfaces (prefers `user`/`header`/`accent`), `TerminalPanel.tsx` gains a right-click context menu (copy/paste/duplicate/clear/scroll/search/new terminal) via portal; `MessageBubble`/`AssetsPanel`/`SessionSubTabs` class tweaks keep contrast on colored surfaces. (`internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/server_cors_test.go`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.test.tsx`, `web/src/lib/fileLinks.tsx`, `web/src/lib/fileLinks.test.tsx`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.ts`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.test.ts`, `web/src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `web/src/hooks/useTheme.test.ts`, `web/src/components/Assets/AssetsPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/MessageBubble.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/SessionSubTabs.tsx`, `web/src/index.css`, `web/tailwind.config.js`, `web/dist/index.html`)
- **Changes tab survives session resume — persistent snapshot journal + GC (2026-08-30)** — the changes tab emptied out whenever a session's agent was rebuilt (30-min idle eviction, server restart, TUI relaunch, resume) because snapshot metadata lived only in the in-memory `snapshot.Store` while backup bytes sat unindexed on disk. New per-project journal `project/<slug>/snapshots/snapshots.sqlite` (`internal/snapshot/journal.go`, WAL + busy_timeout, shared per-dir handle via `journalFor`): every `Store.Backup` with a session id appends a row (`session_id, agent_id, tool_call_id, original_path, backup_path, agent_step, created_at`, indexed by `snapshot_session_id_idx`), and `Store.Rehydrate()` replays a session's rows into an EMPTY store on agent rebuild — wired via `Agent.SetSessionID` (server/desktop, `buildAgentSession`) and new `Agent.SetChangesSession` (TUI construction, `/load`, `/new`; kept separate so TUI debug-log entries stay untagged). Rehydrate drops rows whose backup file is gone and never touches a store with live snapshots. GC on first journal open per process: rows older than 30 days (`journalRetention`) are deleted with their backup files (refcount-checked), then orphan backup files older than the cutoff — including the pre-journal backlog of unindexed backups — are removed (`Journal.gc`/`gcOrphans`; the sqlite file + `-wal`/`-shm` are skipped). Journaling is best-effort: an index failure logs and never fails the write; the global store and sessionless stores stay unjournaled. Also caps `auto-permission-prompt.md.bak.*` retention at 5 (`pruneAutoPermissionPromptBackups` in `internal/config/auto_permission_prompt.go` — 77 backups had accumulated). Tests: journal round-trip/rehydrate-empty-only/missing-backup-skip/GC-retention/sessionless-no-journal (`internal/snapshot/journal_test.go`), backup prune cap (`internal/config/auto_permission_prompt_test.go`). Deferred items in TODO.md (bash-only entries, sub-agent attribution, divergence marking, legacy JSON bulk migration). (`internal/snapshot/journal.go`, `internal/snapshot/snapshot.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/config/auto_permission_prompt.go`)
- **Web/desktop streaming text no longer splits around tool blocks (2026-08-30)** — a live assistant sentence could render cut mid-word with its tail pushed *below* the tool bubbles (e.g. "…across TUI, web, and d" → [bash] → [grep] → "esktop."). Cause: `thinking`/`text` SSE deltas are coalesced in a 90 ms buffer (`bufferLiveDelta`/`LIVE_DELTA_FLUSH_MS` in `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.ts`), but `tool_start` and the active variants of `permission_check`/`advisor_checkpoint` dispatched their `LIVE_*` actions immediately — and those reducers *append* a new part to `live[]`, while `LIVE_DELTA` only continues the last part when kinds match. So a text tail still buffered when a tool started flushed after the tool part landed and became a separate text bubble underneath. Fix: `flushLiveDeltas()` is now called before every dispatch that appends a live part (`tool_start`, `permission_check` active, `advisor_checkpoint` active), preserving true stream order; genuinely post-tool text still correctly forms a new part below the tool block. `tool_output`/`tool_result` are unchanged (they mutate an existing tool part by callId, never append), and inactive check events don't flush (they remove a part). The TUI has no equivalent race — its 90 ms cadence is a render tick, not an event buffer. Tests: 4 regression cases in `sessionEvents.test.ts` assert exact `live[]` part ordering (tail merged above tool, post-tool text stays below, both active status-part flushes, inactive no-early-flush). (`web/src/lib/sessionEvents.ts`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.test.ts`)
- **Web/desktop theme mapping is contrast-safe (LCARS gray-on-orange fixed) (2026-08-30)** — terminal palettes assign colors for text-on-background use only, but `applyThemeColors` mapped them 1:1 onto shadcn CSS surface roles, so vivid themes rendered unreadable text on colored surfaces: on `lcars`, user chat bubbles were tan `text` (#F7D28B) on orange `user` (#FF9F1C, ~1.4:1), and sidebar agent cards / the chat-input placeholder were gray `hint` (#8E6E55) on brown `dim` (#6B5A48, ~1.4:1); `pipboy`, `dracula`, `tokyonight` and the light themes had the same class of failure on primary/accent/muted pairs. `web/src/hooks/useTheme.ts` now derives the palette with WCAG checks: `computeThemeVars()` (pure, exported) picks the first AA-passing foreground from theme candidates for `--primary-foreground`/`--accent-foreground`/`--destructive-foreground`/`--secondary-foreground` (black/white only as last resort), and repairs the `--muted`/`--muted-foreground` pair by first nudging the surface toward the page background (capped at 70% so it keeps a tint, never toward `text` which could flip surface polarity) then brightening the foreground toward `hint`→`text`→neutral black/white per page polarity; pairs that already pass AA are left byte-identical, so themes that read well are untouched. `ThemeForm` previews automatically share the logic (it calls `applyThemeColors`). Components that painted text on `bg-accent` now use `text-accent-foreground` (user-bubble inline code, markdown table headers, attachment chips, jump-to-bottom button, TopTabs badge, FileTree/ProfileSwitcher/ReasoningLevelSelector selected rows) and `hover:bg-accent` surfaces gained matching `hover:text-accent-foreground` across 14 files, since the derived accent foreground is no longer the inherited foreground. Tests: `web/src/hooks/useTheme.test.ts` asserts every text/surface pair clears 4.5:1 for all 23 built-in palettes (snapshot fixture `themePalettes.fixture.ts` generated from `internal/theme/theme.go`), plus the LCARS regression and a no-op-when-readable guard; `MessageBubble.test.tsx` pins the user bubble's `bg-primary`+`text-primary-foreground` class pair (and multiline wrapping) so the contrast fix can't be silently reverted. No Go/TUI palette changes — the terminal mapping is unchanged. (`web/src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `web/src/hooks/useTheme.test.ts`, `web/src/hooks/themePalettes.fixture.ts`, `web/src/components/Chat/MessageBubble.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/MessageBubble.test.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatInput.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/{EditorTabBar,UnifiedTabBar,DirectoryBrowser,ContextMenu,ReasoningLevelSelector,TopTabs}.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/{ThemeForm,ProfilesManager}.tsx`, `web/src/components/Files/FileTree.tsx`, `web/src/components/Agents/AgentsPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/ProfileSwitcher.tsx`)
- **Sidebar model is now per chat session (2026-08-30)** — picking a model in the web/desktop sidebar previously wrote the process-global config model (`PUT /api/config/model`) and every status snapshot carried that single value, so changing the model on one chat tab visibly reflected on **all** other sessions. Backend: new `PUT`/`DELETE /api/sessions/{id}/model` (`HandleSetSessionModel`/`HandleClearSessionModel` in `handler_session_state.go`) persist an optional `model` override in the session transcript metadata (validated like the global setter: provider-prefixed ids only) and broadcast a session-tagged status snapshot (`pushSessionStatusSnapshot`; skipped only for the bridged TUI's own session, which keeps owning its feed). A single resolver `effectiveSessionModel(id)` (override → global config default) now drives everything model-related for a session: `HandleSessionStatus` (live TUI value still wins for the bridged session), `publishTurnStatusSnapshot`, `applySessionContext`'s `context_model`, `getOrCreateAgentSession`, both `HandleChat`/`HandleSendMessage` turn paths and their model-change reconcile — so the sidebar no longer just *displays* a per-session model, the session actually *runs* it, including across resume/restart. `POST /api/chat` with an explicit `model` persists the override at session creation (before the first turn) so "new chat with model X" is durable; sessions created without one keep following the global default. Frontend: `CoworkSidebar`'s Model row (and Context gauge) read the active tab's `tuiStatus.main_model` instead of the process-global `ChatState.model`; `ModelDialog` gains a `sessionId` prop — real-session picks go to the session endpoint (with status refetch on failure, no optimistic global write), draft (`new-*`) tabs store the pick in the new per-session `SessionSlice.model` (dispatch `SET_SESSION_MODEL`), `useChat.sendMessage` rides it into the first `api.chat` request, and `REKEY_SESSION` carries it to the real id; `sessionEvents.ts` no longer dispatches the global `SET_MODEL` from status snapshots (the cross-tab leak) — the global value stays the startup config default used only where no session context exists. Desktop ships the same SPA. Tests: `handler_session_model_test.go` (A/B isolation via the status endpoint, persistence across reload, clear→fallback, provider-prefix validation, 404, new-session create-time persistence, no-override sessions follow global flips), `chatStore.test.tsx` (SET_SESSION_MODEL per-session + rekey carry-over), `sessionEvents.test.ts` (status events never touch the global model), `ModelDialog.test.tsx` (session-scoped pick/clear, draft-local pick, global fallback without session context). - **Web/desktop model picker regains Recently Used + ★ Favorites (2026-08-30)** — `ModelDialog` now partitions `GET /api/models` via new `partitionModelSections` (Recently Used → ★ Favorites deduped against recents → provider groups excluding both), mirroring `openModelPicker` in `internal/tui/picker.go`, for every purpose whose TUI picker reuses it (main/small/recap/permission/mask); the star toggle itself is offered only where the TUI's `ctrl+f` handler actually acts (main/permission — model/permission-model/image-model kinds); each toggleable row carries a star (canonical `provider/model` ids only, single-flight via `pendingFavorite`) wired to new `api.setModelFavorite` with optimistic flip + resync from the endpoint's canonical `favorites` list and revert on failure — the web counterpart of the TUI's `ctrl+f`. Backend: registered the previously unrouted `PUT`/`DELETE /api/models/favorite` handlers (`handler_models_favorite.go`), and `ModelInfo.Favorite` now reports raw favorites membership (a model can be both recent and favorite; placement dedupes) so star state matches `config.IsFavorite`. `web/src/api/types.ts` `ModelInfo` gained `favorite`/`recent`. Desktop ships the same SPA. Tests: `handler_models_favorite_test.go` (validation 400s, idempotent add/remove, saved-order stability, mux route resolution), `modelSelection.test.ts` (section ordering, overlap dedupe, provider grouping), and `ModelDialog.test.tsx` (rendered sections/dedupe, star toggle without select/close, sections-without-star for small/recap/mask, sections+star for permission, advisor/ocr keep the flat list). - **Web/desktop chat agent-preview rail starts collapsed (2026-08-30)** — when a sub-agent spawns during a chat session, the AgentPreview rail above the chat input ballooned because every `RunNode` row mounted expanded (full transcript, result, nested children). `RunNode` gains an optional `defaultOpen` prop (default `true`, propagated to nested children) and `AgentPreview` now passes `defaultOpen={false}`, so each spawned agent shows one compact summary row (chevron, status dot, name, model, duration) until the user clicks the row to expand it; the Agents-tab detail view (`AgentsPanel`) keeps the expanded default. Supersedes the 2026-08-06 web-agents-tab spec's "rail toggle out of scope" note per user request. (`web/src/components/Agents/RunNode.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/AgentPreview.tsx`, `web/src/components/Agents/RunNode.test.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/AgentPreview.test.tsx`)
- **Web/desktop can retarget API/SSE traffic to a configurable backend (2026-08-30)** — new `backend_url` key in `ocodeconfig.json` (normalized/validated by `config.NormalizeBackendURL`: only empty = same-origin, `http://localhost[:port]`, `http://127.0.0.1[:port]`, or `https://hub.mercstudio.com`; trailing slash trimmed; userinfo, paths, queries, fragments, non-http localhost schemes, host-subdomain tricks like `http://localhost.evil.com` and malformed ports rejected). Exposed as `GET`/`PUT /api/config/ocode/backend` (`HandleGetBackendConfig`/`HandleSetBackendConfig` in `handler_config.go`, persisted via `config.SaveBackendURL`) and editable from a new **Backend** group in the web/desktop Settings panel (`BackendForm.tsx`: preset select + custom-origin input, shows the currently active base). The SPA routes every API/SSE/upload/WebSocket call through `apiPath()`/new `apiWsPath()` in `client.ts`, which prepend the absolute backend origin when configured; `main.tsx` gates the first render on `initBackendBase()` (same-origin discovery) so the initial fetches and the first `eventBus` connection already use the right origin, falling back to same-origin on failure; `eventBus.restart()` reopens the live SSE stream against the new `apiPath` after a switch (preserving subscriptions/projects) and stale-`EventSource` guards in the open/envelope/error handlers stop a superseded stream from mutating post-restart state. Terminal sockets use `apiWsPath` (ws/wss derived from the backend URL); an explicit comment notes backend switches deliberately do **not** reconnect running PTYs — host-local terminals survive until reload. Server: all `/api/*` responses now carry CORS via a wrapper that is applied as the **outermost handler over the fully-registered mux** (reflecting `Origin` with `Vary: Origin`, allowing `Content-Type/Authorization/X-Requested-With`, exposing `Content-Length/Content-Type`, OPTIONS preflight answered 204 before auth) — safe without an allowlist because auth is bearer-header only with no cookies/credentials; the same origin-reflection was applied to the legacy `corsMiddleware`. Desktop ships the same SPA. Tests: `internal/server/server_cors_test.go` asserts the origin-reflecting CORS headers reach every `/api` response — including method-specific scheduler routes (the ServeMux catch-all-bypass pitfall: the wrapper must be outermost over the fully-registered mux) — and that OPTIONS preflight is answered without auth; no Go unit test for `NormalizeBackendURL` yet (validation exercised manually via the Settings form). (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/server_cors_test.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/components/Settings/BackendForm.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/SettingsPanel.tsx`, `web/src/main.tsx`, `web/src/lib/eventBus.ts`, `web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.tsx`)
- **StatusBar is the single synchronized "working" indicator (2026-08-30)** — `ChatPanel` no longer renders its own "Working…" row above the transcript; the per-session activity display is centralized in the bottom `StatusBar`, mirroring the TUI's single activity row. `runningStatusParts` is now gated solely on the authoritative per-session liveness flag (`isStreaming || turnActive`), so a stale or late TUI `status` snapshot can never resurrect the indicator after `turn_done`/`turn_error`; within a running turn details prefer the RC-bridge snapshot (`⟳ llm`, `@ agent`, `⚙ tool`), then the in-flight live tool (name truncated to 24 chars), then a base `working…` label so silent turn gaps never look idle. Exported as `runningStatusPartsForTests`. Tests: `web/src/components/common/StatusBar.test.ts` pins the liveness gate, detail precedence, and truncation. (`web/src/components/common/StatusBar.tsx`, `web/src/components/common/StatusBar.test.ts`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`)
- **Unified tab bar drag-reorder registers; cowork rail yields to the terminal (2026-08-30)** — the merged chat+terminal tab order in `UnifiedTabBar` moved from a `useMemo` reading `localStorage` to React state seeded from it and re-synced on project/tab-set change: `onDragEnd` now updates state **and** storage, so a dropped pill renders in its new position immediately instead of snapping back (the old write-storage-only handler never re-rendered the memo). The bar is hoisted out of the focus-toggled chat/terminal containers in `App.tsx` so it stays visible whichever kind is focused (each container is `hidden` when the other owns focus, taking any nested bar with it). New pure helper `coworkSidebarVisibility.shouldRenderCoworkSidebar` gates mounting the right Cowork rail to the Sessions view + Chat sub-tab and hides it while the terminal is focused (the separate `coworkOpen` collapsed/expanded width state is preserved across focus switches); `CoworkSidebar` is passed the active tab's per-session model for its Model row. Tests: `web/src/components/Layout/UnifiedTabBar.drag.test.tsx` (jsdom `PointerEvent` polyfill + mocked pill rects to drive a real dnd-kit reorder end-to-end, kept in its own file to isolate the global-state leak), `coworkSidebarVisibility.test.ts` (view/sub-tab/focus matrix). (`web/src/components/Layout/UnifiedTabBar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/UnifiedTabBar.drag.test.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/coworkSidebarVisibility.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/coworkSidebarVisibility.test.ts`, `web/src/App.tsx`)
- **Memory/leak and robustness sweep — server hot paths and web panels (2026-08-30)** — server: `gatherProcessStats` in `emitters.go` takes one process-table snapshot per tick (`processChildrenSnapshot` → ppid→children map reused by `sumProcessTree`/`terminalCommand`/`collectCmdlines`) because gopsutil's `Children()` re-reads the whole table per node on darwin (previously the second-largest allocation source in the server); `internal/tool/search.go` compiles `**` glob patterns once per walk (new `globMatcher` replaces `regexp.MatchString`, which recompiled per visited file) and both glob/grep call `maybeFreeOSMemory()` — a forced `debug.FreeOSMemory()` only when idle heap exceeds 512 MB — so RSS spikes from big tree walks are returned instead of sitting inflated; `HandleFileSearch` reuses the single already-open file handle after the binary sniff (no second `os.Open`; fixes the fd leak on the cap-hit early return), and `HandleFileTree`/`HandleFileSearch` return 400 when the server has no working directory instead of silently anchoring on `.`; file-tree git badges now propagate from children to parent directories so a folder containing only dirty/untracked files still shows a status. web: `AssetsPanel` guards out-of-order preview loads with a generation counter and revokes superseded/stale object URLs (blob leak); `FileTree` aborts the in-flight full-tree fetch on unmount/re-run via `AbortController`; `FileEditor` tracks and disposes Monaco cursor-selection and scroll listeners on unmount; `PermissionDialog` wraps long tokens (`break-words`/`[overflow-wrap:anywhere]`), makes the dialog scroll and widen to `sm:max-w-2xl` so long commands/rules no longer overflow the viewport. Trivial: `internal/tui/files_model.go` gofmt alignment fixes. - **Redaction hardened — env-var/assignment secret detection (2026-08-28)** — `internal/redact` now detects `KEY = value` assignment-style secrets in **both** chat and file-content modes (previously only keyword+entropy heuristics ran in chat mode). A line-anchored `envAssignRe` captures the value (quoted or unquoted, trailing `# comment` excluded), gated by `isEnvSecretName`: high-confidence suffix tokens (`*_SECRET`, `*_KEY`, `*_TOKEN`, `*_PASSWORD`, `*_CREDENTIAL`, …) redact regardless of entropy while weak tokens (`*_ID`) redact only when high-entropy. Aligns tier-1 redaction with the tier-2 scan trigger. (`internal/redact/detect.go`, `internal/redact/redactor.go`, `internal/redact/detect_test.go`, `internal/redact/net_test.go`)
- **Web file tree gains git-status badges + in-file content search (2026-08-28)** — `HandleFileTree` now annotates nodes with git status (`git status --short`, `gitStatusMapForDir`/`annotateFileTreeGitStatus`) so the frontend shows per-file badges (M/A/D/R/?) and gates context-menu actions; new `GET /api/file-search` (`HandleFileSearch`) does whitespace-AND, case-insensitive content search within the anchored project root. `FileTree.tsx` renders `GitBadge`, `gitFlags`, and `highlightKeywords` for results. (`internal/server/handler_files.go`, `web/src/components/Files/FileTree.tsx`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.78 → 0.8.79: redaction env-var assignment detection, web file-tree git-status badges + content search, Groq live picker supplementation, encrypted file/dir workflow (`rekey` + `secretcli` dir support + `RekeyProjectKey`/`PeekIsEncrypted`), and web transcript tool-group rendering.
- **SQLite session storage — per-project index and incremental append (2026-08-28)** — adds `modernc.org/sqlite` as a third session format alongside `.json`/`.ojsonl`: new sessions are created as `.sqlite`, existing legacy sessions migrate on next write (old file deleted), and a shared per-project `index.sqlite` serves listing from one indexed query instead of opening every file. Covers dependency + schema/connection helpers, per-session read/write with `writeSqliteSessionFull`/`appendSqliteSession`/`readSqliteSession`, index upsert/delete/query, write-path/migrate-on-write dispatch, read-path with sqlite-first fallback, listing union + delete, metadata preservation and hardened path handling, and deterministic test coverage. (`go.mod`, `go.sum`, `internal/session/sqlitestore.go`, `internal/session/session.go`, `internal/session/sqlite*`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-28-sqlite-session-storage/`)
- **Encrypted file/dir workflow expanded — bulk jobs, rekey, and web/TUI parity (2026-08-28)** — `ocode secret` now handles files **and** directories, adds `rekey`, confirmation prompts and `SetIO` for suspended-terminal use, and shares cancellable progress-reporting core via new `internal/secretjob` (`Walk`, `Manager`, `secret_progress`/`secret_done`/`secret_error`/`secret_cancelled` bus events). Server exposes `/api/secret/*` (`init`/`scan`/`encrypt`/`decrypt`/`rekey`/`cancel`) with `resolveSecretPath` confinement (symlink-resolved, `fileTreeRootFor`-checked); web `FileTree` adds a context-menu + `SecretActionDialog` (scan count, passphrase + confirmation, progress bar) and `SecurityForm`/`TopTabs` surface; TUI `secret_editor` + `secret_cli_exec` mirror the CLI with the same confirmation flow. OCR diagnostics in `Agent.GetToolDefinitions` now only emit when `config.Ocode.Ocr.Enabled` to avoid per-turn log spam on sub-agent allowlists. - **Version Bump** — 0.8.75 → 0.8.77: security hardening and deterministic regression coverage for plugin lifecycle, permissions, retries, session tabs, terminals, watchdogs, and navigation.

- **Mid-turn reload no longer loses streaming reply (2026-08-28)** — `SessionManager` now buffers `text`/`thinking`/`tool_*` SSE frames while `turnActive` is true (256 KiB cap, head-truncated) and exposes them as `live_frames` in `GET /api/sessions/:id/state`; frontend replays them through the existing bus envelope path with `seq`-based dedup against frames already applied live, and reconstructs permission/question dialogs from the persisted transcript sentinel so a reload mid-turn or mid-question/permission continuation doesn't drop them. `turnActive` is now also set around continuation `Step` calls in `HandleResolvePermission`/`HandleAnswerQuestion`. []` guards. - **Runtime limits apply live and usage queries drop from full-file re-parse to append-only cache (2026-08-28)** — `Agent.maxSteps` is now `atomic.Int64` so a concurrent `SetMaxSteps` (TUI `/model` or `PUT /api/config/ocode/limits`) during `Step` no longer races, and `HandleSetLimitsConfig` fans out via `applyLimitsToLiveSessions`; `buildAgentSession` copies `h.cfg` under `h.mu`; `internal/usage` keeps an append-only `cachedRecords`/`cachedOffset` protected by `queryMu` so `watchEmittersLoop`'s 60 s `Query` no longer re-parses 170 k+ records each tick (~10 GB garbage/100 min). ### Security
- **Plugin Endpoints Require Auth on Non-Loopback Bindings** — `POST /api/plugins` (and related plugin routes) previously accepted unauthenticated requests on any bound interface, letting a remote caller clone an arbitrary Git repo and run `on_install` commands (RCE). Unauthenticated plugin management is now restricted to loopback; non-loopback binds require authentication (`pluginAuthMiddleware` / `isLoopbackBind`). (`internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/handler_plugins.go`)
- **Plugin Removal Rejects Symlink Escapes** — `HandleRemovePlugin` trusted the persisted `cfg.Plugins[name].Dir` and called `os.RemoveAll` without containment checks, allowing a symlinked dir to delete arbitrary paths. Removal now validates the target as a direct child of an approved plugin root via canonical `filepath.EvalSymlinks` resolution (rejecting absolute/traversal/symlink escapes) using the new `ValidateRemovableDirForProject`. (`internal/plugins/loader.go`, `internal/server/handler_plugins.go`, `docs/gotchas/plugin-removal-symlink-escape.md`)
- **Plugin Handlers No Longer Hold the Server Lock Across I/O** — `HandleSetPluginEnabled` held `h.mu` across config persistence and filesystem scans (blocking session polling / agent-run state), and plugin config maps were read after unlock, racing concurrent mutations. Handlers now snapshot config (copying the `Plugins` map via `pluginConfigSnapshot`) under the lock, release it for I/O, then re-acquire to commit. (`internal/server/handler_plugins.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/frontend_stats.go`)
- **Project-Local Plugin Discovery Uses the Session Project Root** — plugin discovery read `os.Getwd()`, so a Finder-launched desktop app or multi-project web server loaded the wrong project's plugins. New `LoadPluginsForProject(name, projectRoot)` / `FindPluginDirForProject(name, projectRoot)` resolve against the session/project root, with backward-compatible `LoadPlugins` / `FindPluginDir` wrappers for the TUI and tests. (`internal/plugins/manager.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/handler_plugins.go`)
- **Plugin Lifecycle Rebuilds Resident Agent Sessions** — install/enable/disable/remove previously left already-built `agentSession` instances with stale plugin tools/instructions. New `refreshAgentSessionsForPluginChange` snapshots session IDs under the lock and rebuilds outside it (skipping active turns, honoring `h.mu` → `agentSession.mu` ordering) so changes take effect on the next turn. (`internal/server/handler_plugins.go`, `internal/server/server.go`)
- **Web Plugin Install Persists `@ref` Pins and Cleanup Is Transactional** — the requested `@ref` was dropped on web install; it is now stored in `PluginConfig`. Installation rolls back (remove cloned dir + `UnregisterMCP` + skip config save) when `RunOnInstall`/`AutoRegisterMCP`/`SavePlugin` fails, and removal now calls `UnregisterMCP` to drop stale MCP entries. (`internal/server/handler_plugins.go`, `internal/plugins/manager.go`)
- **Auto-Permission Prompt Tightened (1.2.0 → 1.4.0)** — the bundled auto-permission prompt no longer blanket-allows `npm publish`, `pnpm dlx`/`npx`, `bunx`/`bun x`, arbitrary executables, or unrestricted `/tmp` writes. It now narrowly allows project-scoped package-manager reads/installs and temp writes only to user-owned `os.TempDir` paths. (`internal/config/auto_permission_prompt.go`)

### Fixed
- **TUI Panic on Concurrent Model Switch + Memory Maintenance (2026-08-29)** — `QueueMemoryMaintenance` only nil-checked `memoryMaintCh` before sending, while an async `/model` switch's background goroutine could call `Shutdown()` (closing that channel) on an agent still referenced as the live `m.agent`; a job completing in that window panicked with `send on closed channel`. `memory_maintenance.go` now guards the channel with a `memoryMaintMu`/`memoryMaintClosing` flag mirroring the existing `docMaintCh` fix (`doc_maintenance.go`, C4: OCSEC:31f59a:2), dropping (not blocking-goroutine-sending) queued requests once shutdown begins. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/memory_maintenance.go`, `internal/agent/memory_maintenance_test.go`)
- **Web Terminals Stay Mounted and Closed Sessions Stop Resurrecting (2026-08-28)** — selecting the Processes tab previously unmounted every `TerminalPanel`, killing server-side PTYs; panels now stay mounted (hidden via CSS) and apply scrollback/font changes in place without tearing down the pty. `cancelLiveDeltas` is invoked before a session reset so buffered SSE deltas can't resurrect a closed session, `ProcessesPanel` row merging now removes entries absent from the latest snapshot (no unbounded memory growth), and rejected queued chat submissions restore the draft instead of silently dropping it. (`web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Terminal/ProcessesPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalTabs.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatInput.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`)
- **Local Chat Models No Longer Allocate an Unbounded KV Cache (2026-08-28)** — every bundled llama.cpp `LaunchArgv` (bonsai-8b-1bit, qwen3.8-4b-distill) omitted `--ctx-size`, so llama-server defaulted to `n_ctx=0` ("loaded from model") and allocated a KV cache sized to the GGUF's full native trained context — 262144 tokens for `qwen3.8-4b-distill`, ~tens of GB of fp16 KV cache on a single `--parallel` slot regardless of actual usage. `LocalModelConfig` gains a `ContextSize` field (default 20000 for newly-registered models via `DefaultLocalChatContextSize`; 0 preserves llama.cpp's own "use model's native context" convention for anyone who wants it uncapped), threaded through `SaveLocalModelConfig` → `agent.StartLocalModelInstance` → `discovery.StartModelInstance`/`spawnLlamaCppChatServer`'s new `{ctx}` LaunchArgv placeholder, and exposed via a new `/localmodel ctx <name> <tokens|0>` subcommand (mirrors `/localmodel limit`). (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/discovery/manifest.go`, `internal/discovery/instances.go`, `internal/agent/local_models.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Parallel Permission Asks No Longer Deadlock or Silently Drop (2026-08-27)** — a single tool-call round can pause with more than one unresolved `PERMISSION_ASK`/question sentinel when parallel dispatch runs several calls needing approval before the pause check. `findPendingSession` and `tailIsPermissionAsk`/`tailIsQuestionAsk` assumed the pending ask was `messages[len-1]`; answering one sentinel then `Step()`-ed on top of still-raw sentinels fed the model a malformed tool-result, which it retried as a brand-new ask (same dialog popping back up). New `trailingToolRunStart` scans the whole trailing tool-result run by `ToolID`, `HandleResolvePermission` persists a single resolution and waits for remaining asks instead of stepping, `HandleChatStream` rejects new turns while permission is pending, `SessionManager.EvictIdle` exempts sessions with a pending ask (with correct `as.mu`/`m.mu` lock ordering) so idle eviction no longer 404s a later resolve, `PendingPermissionAsks` reads under `as.mu`, and web `chatStore` queues superseded permission dialogs (`permissionQueue` stack) and resurfaces them on resolve instead of dropping. (`internal/server/agent_session.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `internal/server/handler_questions.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`, `internal/server/run_states.go`, `internal/server/session_manager.go`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.test.tsx`, `AGENTS.md`)
- **Finder-Launched Desktop No Longer Walks / or $HOME and No Longer Bypasses Path Containment (2026-08-27)** — a Finder/Dock-launched `.app` starts with cwd `/`; the old fallback used `$HOME` (huge tree triggering macOS TCC prompts when the file tree/LSP walker scanned it) and `resolveWithinWorkdir`/`HandleInit` called raw `os.Getwd()` so every absolute path passed the "stays inside workdir" LFI guard when cwd was `/` (and `HandleInit` wrote `AGENTS.md` to `/`). New `desktop.IsUnsafeDesktopRoot`/`ResolveFallbackWorkDir` reuses the most-recent saved project or the small global data dir (`~/.local/share/ocode`) instead, and `handler_info.go`/`handler_open.go` resolve against `h.workDir` with `os.Getwd()` only as fallback. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`, `internal/desktop/boot.go`, `internal/server/handler_info.go`, `internal/server/handler_open.go`, `internal/server/handler_open_test.go`, `AGENTS.md`)
- **Search Ignore Rules Honor the Session Project Root and Unscoped Grep Is Bounded (2026-08-27)** — `NewIgnoreMatcher` read `.gitignore`/`.ignore` from the process cwd, so a `/`-cwd desktop shell silently missed the project's `.gitignore` and walked `node_modules`/`dist`/`vendor` unfiltered. It now loads from the resolved search root (`WithWorkDir` project root). `GrepTool` also caps unscoped (no `path`/`include`) repo-wide searches at `maxUnscopedFiles=5000`, skips `vendor`/`dist`/`build`/`.next`/`target` when unscoped, reuses a single 1 MB scan buffer instead of allocating per file, and truncates pervasive-match output via `truncateOutput`. (`internal/tool/ignore.go`, `internal/tool/search.go`, `internal/tool/search_test.go`)
- **Local-Model Slot-Lock Reclamation No Longer Races Live Holders (2026-08-26)** — the stale-slot reaper treated an old lock mtime as proof of abandonment and deleted the slot path outright, but its Stat → Remove window could race `slotReleasingBody`'s mtime touch ticker: deleting a live holder's lock let a contender claim an extra concurrent slot and break `max_parallel`. Staleness is now only a hint — reclamation additionally requires winning a non-blocking advisory lock on a per-slot `.guard` file (new `filelock.TryLock`, the non-blocking sibling of `WithFileLock`), which live holders keep for their whole lease, so a slot is only removed after proving it has no owner; acquirers also take the guard immediately after exclusive-create so two contenders can never both believe they own the same fresh slot. (`internal/agent/local_model_limiter.go`, `internal/agent/local_model_limiter_test.go`, `internal/filelock/filelock.go`, `docs/gotchas/local-model-limiter-stale-slot-race.md`)
- **Slash-Only Sessions Get No Command-Text Titles (2026-08-26)** — auto-titling seeded from the first non-empty user message regardless of content, so a session opened by running `/model`, `/compact`, etc. got titled after literal command text on every surface: server title generation (`firstAndLastMessageTexts` / `lastUserAndLastAssistantTexts`), headless persistence (`saveJSON` / `saveOjsonl`) and Claude-JSONL import, the TUI sidebar fallback, and the web `CoworkSidebar`. User messages starting with `/` are now excluded everywhere, mirroring the TUI's `isCommandHistoryMessage` filter. Because slash-only sessions then have no display title at all, the sidebar header renders whenever any message exists — reserving one row so the "✦ gen" button stays visible and clickable over an empty title — while "Untitled" remains only for genuinely empty sessions. (`internal/server/title_gen.go`, `internal/session/session.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`)
- **Long LLM Streams No Longer Die at 5 Minutes with an Unretryable Timeout (2026-08-26)** — `llmHTTPClient` carried a blanket 5-minute `http.Client.Timeout`, which covers the *streamed response body*, so any generation streaming longer than the cap was killed mid-read with `net/http: request canceled (Client.Timeout or context cancellation while reading body)` — and because partial deltas had already been rendered, Chat's anti-duplication gate refused to retry (`failed after 1 attempt(s)`), hard-failing long reasoning-model turns. The blanket cap is gone: pre-stream phases are bounded by transport-level timeouts (`ResponseHeaderTimeout`=5m, TLS handshake), a new `idleAbortTransport` watchdog aborts only genuinely silent streams (no bytes for 3m) with a typed **retryable** `streamStalledError`, and healthy-but-slow streams now run unbounded. Local-model slot locks are refreshed by a touch ticker while a body is open (staleness bound re-anchored to the touch cadence instead of the removed total cap), so cross-process slot stealing can't hit legitimately long local generations. User-initiated cancellation still propagates unchanged; the no-retry-after-deltas invariant is preserved and pinned by test. (`internal/agent/llm_stream_idle.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/local_model_limiter.go`, `internal/agent/llm_stream_idle_test.go`, `internal/agent/local_model_limiter_test.go`, `internal/agent/agent_test.go`)
- **Desktop Search Tools Anchor on the Session Project Root (2026-08-26)** — `glob`/`grep`/`list` resolved an empty/relative `path` against the process cwd; a desktop shell launched from Finder runs with cwd `/` (walking the entire disk), and the multi-project web server walked the wrong tree. New `resolveSearchRoot` anchors searches on the session's `WithWorkDir` root: empty means the project root, relative paths join onto it, and absolute paths are validated via `Clean`+`EvalSymlinks`+prefix check (extra allowed roots and temp dirs also accepted) so a model cannot escape the project via `/` or `../../`; results are re-relativized through `searchDisplayPath` so they stay resolvable by the file tools. Also fixes glob's truncated-match count (previously reported `len(matches)+1` after slicing). With no workDir in ctx (tests, TUI direct calls) legacy cwd-relative behavior is preserved. (`internal/tool/search.go`, `internal/tool/search_test.go`)
- **MLX Local Models Fail Fast Instead of Hanging 15 Minutes (2026-08-26)** — two defects behind the "auto-permission consult stuck ~16m" desktop hang: (1) a Finder-launched `ocode.app` inherits launchd's bare PATH, so every bare `"python3"` in the MLX backend resolved to the Xcode CLT stub (3.9.6, no `mlx_lm`) — new `internal/discovery/python_env.go` asks the user's login shell for its real PATH (`$SHELL -ilc`, cached via `sync.Once`, marker-delimited to survive MOTD noise) and resolves an absolute `python3` binary substituted into `argv[0]` at all four spawn sites (Go resolves a bare argv[0] via the *calling* process's PATH before `cmd.Env` is ever consulted); (2) `waitForChatHealth` polled only the HTTP endpoint with no process-liveness visibility, so a dead-on-arrival spawn burned the full 15-minute poll budget — the `spawn` callback now returns a `ChatLiveness` check (backed by `tool.Process.SnapshotStatus()`/registry output) and the health loop fails within ~1s of process death, surfacing exit code plus the last 2000 bytes of process output. Regression test asserts the fast-fail completes in under 2s. (`internal/discovery/python_env.go`, `internal/discovery/instances.go`, `internal/discovery/instances_test.go`, `internal/discovery/localserver.go`, `internal/discovery/manifest.go`, `internal/agent/local_models.go`)
- **Web Chat No Longer Renders Blank After Mid-Turn Reload (2026-08-26)** — the SSE `MERGE_SNAPSHOT` guard keyed on live-part presence, but after a page reload the mirror reconnects before history fetch resolves, so deltas landed on a virgin slice first and dropping the snapshot left the reloaded chat blank until `turn_done`; conversely a slice with committed messages must never regress to the pre-turn page. The merge now suppresses snapshot replacement only when `turnActive && messages.length > 0` (committed transcript holds newer state), otherwise applying the disk page while preserving the live buffer during an active turn. (`web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.test.tsx`)
- **Web Sidebar Context Gauge Pre-initialized from Config (2026-08-25)** — `GET /api/config/model` now returns `context_max_tokens` via `agent.ModelWindow(model)` so the web sidebar can initialize its Context gauge before any session snapshot exists. Web `api.getConfigModel` widens to `{model, context_max_tokens?}` and `CoworkSidebar`/`ProjectSidebar` consume it on mount; `isDesktopShell` is extracted to a shared `web/src/lib/desktopShell.ts` so keyboard shortcuts and sidebar agree on shell detection. (`internal/server/handler_config.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/ProjectSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/lib/desktopShell.ts`, `web/src/hooks/useKeyboard.ts`)
- **Web/Desktop Sidebar Context Gauge Tracks Every Turn (2026-08-24)** — the right sidebar's Context section (and bottom-bar token gauge) went stale or blank during use. Three defects, all fixed server-side: (1) session-tagged `status` SSE broadcasts carried snapshots **without** the `context_*` fields — they are `omitempty`, so the web client's wholesale `SET_TUI_STATUS` replace zeroed the gauge; this hit every headless web/desktop session right after its *first* turn, when auto-title generation broadcast a bare config-derived snapshot. (2) Nothing refreshed context usage after subsequent turns — the transcript estimate (`chars/4`) was only fetched on tab activation or bus reconnect. (3) In `/rc` co-location mode the TUI-pushed snapshots never set `context_current_tokens` at all, so every live TUI status push zeroed current tokens while keeping max/model. Context computation is now centralized in `Handler.applySessionContext` (transcript estimate; live bridge values win for the bridged session) and applied by every session-tagged snapshot source: the per-session status endpoint, the title-generation broadcast, and a new `publishTurnStatusSnapshot` fired right after each headless turn completes (normal path and permission-resolve continuations; no-op when a TUI bridge owns the status feed). The TUI snapshot builder now reports the last call's provider-reported input tokens as `ContextCurrentTokens`, giving real usage instead of an estimate for bridged sessions. (`internal/server/handler_session_state.go`, `internal/server/title_gen.go`, `internal/server/agent_session.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/server/handler_session_state_test.go`, `internal/server/title_gen_test.go`)
- **Advisor Plan Checkpoint No Longer Freezes the Turn After a Write Tool Call (2026-08-23)** — with `advisor.checkpoints` at its default `["plan","done"]`, the first write-class call of a turn (`apply_patch`, `edit`, `write`, …) rendered in the transcript and then the loop went silent for minutes: `advisorPlanCheckpoint` fired *before* execution and blocked on a second model (a `claude -p` subprocess runs up to 5 minutes), emitting no `OnMessage`/`OnDelta`, and then **deferred the whole batch** — so the model had to regenerate the entire tool call, a full `apply_patch` diff included, after the review. Three changes: (1) the checkpoint now runs *after* the batch executes and injects the review as a user message, so the write is generated once and corrected in place if the advisor objects (deferral and `deferredWriteIntent` are gone); (2) a new `Agent.OnAdvisorCheckpoint(kind, running)` callback brackets the call — the TUI renders `◆ advisor plan checkpoint — reviewing…` and rewrites it with the elapsed time on completion, and the server broadcasts an `advisor_checkpoint` SSE frame the web client shows as a live status part; (3) the call is bound to the agent's stop channel via a new `AdvisorTool.ExecuteCtx`, so Escape cancels an in-flight advisor (subprocess killed, sub-agent loop cancelled) instead of parking the loop on it — which also makes *model-invoked* `advisor` tool calls cancellable, since `executeToolCallWithContext` now takes the `ContextualTool` branch for them. (`internal/agent/advisor_checkpoint.go`, `internal/agent/advisor_tool.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/advisor_checkpoint_test.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`, `internal/server/event_bus.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.ts`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.test.ts`, `README.md`)
- **Duplicate-Key Tool Arguments Skip Instead of Ending the Turn (2026-08-23)** — duplicate top-level JSON keys in tool-call arguments (`{"path":"a.py","path":"b.md"}`) were fatal at the OpenAI stream parser, which killed the whole turn; the error matched nothing in `isRetryableLLMClientError`, so the loop stopped dead and the model never learned what was wrong. The check now runs at the dispatch site (`handleToolCallWithContext`): the call is skipped, never executes, and the conflict comes back as an ordinary tool result naming the key and both values (`arguments set "path" twice, to "a.py" and then to "b.md". Re-issue this call once…`), still paired by `tool_call_id`. Moving it off the parser also widened coverage to the Anthropic path, whose `input_json_delta` assembly can produce the same duplicates and was never checked. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/agent_test.go`, `internal/agent/client_test.go`)
- **Web/Desktop Permission Dialog Parity + Persistent Out-of-Scope Path Roots (2026-08-24)** — unified out-of-scope path classification (`IsOutOfScopePathRequest`/`OutOfScopePathRoot` in `internal/agent/permission_paths.go`) shared by TUI and `POST /api/permissions/resolve`. `permission_resolved` SSE now broadcasts before the approved tool executes (so the dialog auto-closes), and the 4-choice dialog (`allow`/`deny`/`always_rule`/`always_tool` + confirm step) is available on both surfaces with correct guards (`AlwaysRuleChoiceAvailable`/`AlwaysToolChoiceAvailable`/`IsHarmfulRequest`). "Always" on an out-of-scope path persists the path's root to `extra_allowed_paths` rather than a bash/tool rule; `webfetch` domain "always" grants remain session-scoped and are not persisted. Includes expanded TUI/web permission resolvers, updated `CoworkSidebar`/`PermissionsForm`/`PermissionDialog`, and new `permission_paths_test`/resolver E2E fixtures. (`internal/agent/permission_paths.go`, `internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `web/src/components/Chat/PermissionDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/PermissionsForm.tsx`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve_test.go`, `internal/server/zz_e2e_perm_test.go`, `internal/server/zz_repro_perm_test.go`)
- **Headless Model Switching Rejects Bare Provider-Less IDs (2026-08-24)** — `PUT /api/config/model` now rejects a model id with no `provider/model` (or `provider:model`) separator before persisting it as `last_model`; a bare id (e.g. `gpt-4o-mini`) cannot be resolved back to a provider by `agent.NewClient` and made every later start/resume bootstrap fail with a misleading "no API key" refusal. Also fixes live application of `PUT /api/config/small-model` and `PUT /api/config/permission-model` to bridged TUI agents and all live headless sessions (atomic overrides on `Agent` — `SetSmallModelRuntimeEnabled`/`SetSmallModelRuntimeModel`/`SetAutoPermissionModel`), so toggles take effect immediately instead of after restart and `TUIStatus` reflects the live value. (`internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/small_model.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/handler_tui_status.go`, `web/src/api/types.ts`)
- **Tier-2 Redaction Scanner Follows the Live Local-Model Port** — the tier-2 LLM scanner's `base_url` for a `local/*` (`/localmodel`-managed) model was taken from the persisted config value (e.g. `http://localhost:11459/v1`) or a static `AssignChatPort` computation, which goes stale when the running server was spawned under a different registered-id set or simply isn't up yet — so the scanner pointed at the wrong/dead port and couldn't connect (and, under `fail_mode=block`, blocked sends). `buildLLMScanner` (TUI) and `Handler.buildRedactionScanner` (server) now resolve the endpoint from the instance manager's live state: `discovery.LocalModelBaseURL` prefers the in-process instance record, then probes the deterministic `AssignChatPort` for a server started by another ocode process, and only falls back to the deterministic port when nothing is live (so the scanner is still constructed). A new `model.resolveLiveScanner` re-resolves the live port at every scan, returning nil (tier-2 skipped, regex tier-1 still applies) when no local server answers, so a not-yet-started model never blocks sends. `/mask status` now shows the resolved live endpoint for `local/*` models. The persisted `security.redaction.base_url` is now ignored for `local/*` (the port is instance-manager-owned, not user-configurable). (`internal/discovery/instances.go`, `internal/tui/redaction.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`)
- **Background Bash No Longer Hangs on Timeout (Process-Group Kill)** — when the exec timeout or caller cancellation fired before the command exited, `exec.CommandContext`'s default `Cancel` only killed the direct child; a grandchild that inherited the stdout/stderr pipes (e.g. a pipeline stage) kept them open and blocked `pumpWg.Wait()` forever even after the child was dead. `ExecuteStreamCtx` now kills the whole process group (`killProcessGroup`), returns the output captured so far immediately, and finalizes the process record on its own goroutine instead of blocking the call. (`internal/tool/exec.go`)
- **Bash Change-Recorder No Longer Stalls on Huge workDir** — `StatBashRecorder.Pre`/`Post` walk the entire workDir on every bash call; when a session has no real project root bound (e.g. the desktop fallback workDir resolves to the user's home or the filesystem root) or points at a slow/network tree, the walk could block every bash call for minutes with no cancellation available. A 2s `walkBudget` now aborts the walk early (best-effort fingerprint), and roots that are the home dir or filesystem root are skipped entirely via `unsafeWalkRoot`. (`internal/changes/bash.go`)
- **Failed Turns No Longer Erase Themselves (2026-08-21)** — a mid-turn LLM error (dropped stream, auth failure between rounds) wiped the whole turn: `Agent.Step` returned `nil, err`, discarding assistant text/tool results already streamed and executed, and the server error paths returned without `saveSession`. Reopening showed only the user message. `Step` now returns `newMsgs, err` (both mid-loop and summarize paths) and `commitPartialTranscript` persists/mirrors the partial transcript before `turn_error`; permission/question resolve continuations use the same path. TUI still discards `resp` on error (web/desktop scope only). (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/step_partial_test.go`, `internal/server/agent_session.go`, `internal/server/agent_session_partial_test.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `internal/server/handler_questions.go`)
- **Embed Server Reclaims Stale 11457 (2026-08-21)** — `EnsureLocalServer` no longer fail-opens when a FOREIGN-model/wedged server squats the shared port 11457. Central pid-recorded `embed.start.lock` (machine-global, not project-scoped; `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` + owner pid; instant reclaim when holder pid dead instead of burning the full wait window, 10m mtime stale fallback for pre-pid locks) serializes spawns; contender waits with per-second holder-pid liveness check (`waitForEmbedHealthTracked`) for fast takeover when the holder crashed (live-holder lock is never broken); `reapStrayEmbedServer` re-probes before kill and identifies ocode servers by ANY manifest token OR cache-artifact path (`<cache>/local-*` + `mlx_embed_server.py`) plus `--port 11457` + `listeningPIDs` ownership, so wrong-model squats are reclaimed, while an unidentifiable holder is reported, never killed (`UserBaseURL` mismatch still hard-errors). Chat `*.start.lock` now uses the same central pidlock with conditional release (only removes own pid). LSP daemon startup remains project-root scoped via `broker.StartOnce` flock (kernel-released on crash). (`internal/discovery/pidlock.go`, `internal/discovery/localserver.go`, `internal/discovery/instance_reap.go`, `internal/discovery/instances.go`, `internal/lsp/broker/metadata.go`, `TODO.md`)
- **Surprise Startup Model Eliminated (2026-08-21)** — `config.Load()` no longer falls back to `recent[0]` from shared `model.json` when `last_model` is unset; that file churns from every tool/instance sharing it and launches silently adopted whatever won the last write race. Now `env > last_model > config model`; no `last_model` → agentless until user picks. `SaveRecentModel`/`readWriteModelState` now run under `lockModelState()` (`O_EXCL` lockfile, 10s stale-steal, 5s deadline, PID-suffixed tmp). (`internal/config/config.go`, `internal/config/state.go`, `internal/config/config_test.go`)
- **Web Editor Typing Regressions (2026-08-21)** — `FileEditor.tsx` is now uncontrolled (`defaultValue` + `lastEmittedRef` guard): external sync effect only applies genuine disk/draft/reload content, preserving selection via `pushEditOperations`; `cursorSmoothCaretAnimation: "off"` (was `"on"` — caret trailed text 30–60ms); Monaco selection listeners wired inside `onMount` (empty-deps effect read null before async mount); duplicate tabs claimed via `openFileIdsRef` before await fetch. (`web/src/components/Files/FileEditor.tsx`, `web/src/hooks/useEditorTabs.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/EditorTabBar.tsx`)
- **Background Bash Commands Self-Terminate on Force-Kill (2026-08-26)** — `run_in_background: true` bash commands now go through `WrapWithParentMonitor` before `StartBackground`, so they self-terminate (polling `kill -0` on ocode's PID) within ~0.5s even if ocode is killed with SIGKILL and never runs its own `supervisor.Shutdown`/`TerminateAll` cleanup. `StartBackgroundDisplay` decouples the displayed command text from the actual wrapper command so `bash_output`/`kill_shell` listings still show the caller's original text. (`internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/tool/process.go`, `internal/tool/exec_test.go`, `docs/gotchas/background-bash-orphan-on-force-kill.md`)
- **Foreground Bash Commands Get Parent-Death Protection Before Start (2026-08-26)** — foreground commands are now wrapped with `WrapWithParentMonitor` before `cmd.Start()` in `BashTool.ExecuteStreamCtx`, so a command promoted to background mid-flight retains parent-death protection without changing its streaming pipes, timeout/cancellation path, registry record, supervisor tracking, or process-group ownership. (`internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/tool/exec_test.go`, `docs/gotchas/foreground-bash-parent-death-protection.md`)
- **Process-Group Hijack via TIOCSPGRP Fixed with Setsid (2026-08-26)** — `setProcGroup` changed from `Setpgid` to `Setsid` so spawned children are new session leaders with no controlling terminal; a local-model server child was confirmed doing `open("/dev/tty")` + `TIOCSPGRP` against the real terminal, knocking ocode's own process group out of the foreground and corrupting the shell session. `Setsid` makes this class of hijack structurally impossible. (`internal/tool/proc_unix.go`, `docs/gotchas/local-model-tty-hijack.md`)
- **Desktop Binary Registers Provider Plugins + Handles LSP Daemon (2026-08-26)** — the desktop binary now blank-imports `internal/plugin/codex` and `internal/plugin/grok` so OAuth-backed OpenAI requests work (without them, requests fall through to the API-key endpoint and ChatGPT tokens fail with `missing_scope`). Also handles the hidden `lsp-daemon` subcommand (re-exec'd by `internal/lsp/manager.go`'s `spawnDaemonProcess`) so the child runs the daemon instead of opening a second full desktop window. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`)
- **Agent Replacement Race Prevention (2026-08-26)** — `/model` and `/new` now retire the current agent asynchronously while installing a fresh one; user input (prompts, `!` shell commands, slash commands) entered during replacement is queued instead of dispatching against the retiring agent. Epoch-tagged stream events prevent stale events from mutating the new session, and generation guards on `modelSwitchDoneMsg`/`sessionResetDoneMsg` stop superseded completions from clobbering a newer replacement. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`, `docs/gotchas/agent-replacement-input-queuing.md`)
- **Web Streaming CPU Cost Reduced via Memoization + Delta Coalescing (2026-08-26)** — `MessageBubble` and `ToolBlock` are now wrapped in `React.memo` so committed bubbles are not re-rendered on every live delta. Live thinking/text deltas are buffered and flushed every 90ms (matching the TUI's coalescing cadence) instead of dispatching one Redux action per SSE frame, halving in-flight CPU with no perceptible animation loss. (`web/src/components/Chat/MessageBubble.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/TurnParts.tsx`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.ts`, `web/src/lib/sessionEvents.test.ts`)

### Added
- **AIHubMix Live Model Discovery (2026-08-26)** — models AIHubMix serves that the models.dev catalog hasn't indexed (e.g. `ox-alpha`) were invisible in the model picker. New `fetchAIHubMixLiveModels` queries the provider's OpenAI-compatible `/models` endpoint (5-minute TTL cache; returns nil silently on missing key, network failure, or unparseable body so callers degrade to the snapshot) and merges the live IDs into both the all-provider list and the `aihubmix` provider list; `PreloadAIHubMixModels` kicks off the fetch at server boot and TUI init so the picker is populated without blocking. Also makes `bestPricedEntry` deterministic by sorting provider keys before iteration — Go randomizes map order, so multi-provider models like `gpt-4o` previously flipped between slightly different provider pricings run to run. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/agent/models_registry_aihubmix_test.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **doc_write / doc_deprecate Snapshot + Undo Support (2026-08-26)** — knowledge-bundle writes bypassed the changes tab entirely: they never appeared in the file list and `undo_file_change` couldn't restore them. Both tools are now context-aware (`ExecuteCtx`): before writing they back up the target doc plus the auto-maintained `log.md` and `index.md` under one `tool_call_id` — a single undo restores the trio together — and register the writes only after store success; new `knowledge.Store.BundleRoot()` exposes the bundle root, and `isValidDocPathForSnapshot` mirrors `validateDocPath` so invalid paths (traversal, absolute, reserved files) never leave orphan snapshots for calls the store will reject. (`internal/agent/doc_tools.go`, `internal/agent/doc_tools_snapshot_test.go`, `internal/knowledge/store.go`)
- **Web/Desktop Slash-Command Parity via Shared commandctx Builders (2026-08-26)** — prompt builders for `/learn`, `/doc-sync`, `/mem update`, `/docs init|status|cleanup`, and `/paths` moved out of `internal/tui` into a new shared package `internal/commandctx` so TUI handlers and server endpoints emit byte-identical output; the web client gained a full slash-command dispatcher (`web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts`) whose prompt-style commands route through the normal chat pipeline exactly like the TUI. New authed endpoints: `GET /api/paths`, `GET /api/memory/status`, `GET|POST /api/docs/{status,init,update,cleanup}`, `POST /api/auth/connect` (`/connect`), `GET|PUT /api/config/ocode/autocontinue`, `POST /api/permissions/bash-rule` (`/ban`; persists and applies to every live agent), plus `PUT /api/config/ocode/paths` now syncs the process-global tool allowlist at runtime via new `tool.SetPersistentExtraAllowedPaths`. Also: eager LSP `WarmUp` mirrored from the TUI so the web/desktop sidebar lists language servers immediately, session-title display in the sidebar mirroring the TUI's `sidebarDisplayTitle`, bare `/model` opening the model dialog, and a title-regeneration endpoint `POST /api/sessions/{id}/title/generate` (regenerates from the latest task — last user + last assistant message — with an in-flight 409 claim, ~20s timeout, status broadcast). (`internal/commandctx/*`, `internal/server/handler_slash_parity.go`, `internal/server/handler_commandctx.go`, `internal/server/title_gen.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/tool/file.go`, `web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`)
- **TUI Local-Model Multi-Select Picker (2026-08-26)** — `/localmodel add` with no argument opens a multi-select catalog picker (Space toggles, `a` selects all) that spawns models sequentially inside one `tea.Cmd`, aggregating results through `localModelBulkActionMsg` so per-model failures are isolated and each line still lands in the transcript; `/localmodel enable` multi-selects disabled registered models the same way. HuggingFace tokens are shape-validated before spawn attempts. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/picker.go`, `internal/tui/localmodel_multi_test.go`, `scripts/capture-desktop-hang.sh`)
- **Frontend Memory Observability Ring (2026-08-25)** — bounded `frontendStatsRing` (cap 240, ~2h at 30s interval) behind `POST /api/debug/frontend-stats` + `GET /api/debug/frontend-stats` that stores renderer-reported `terminal_count`/`terminal_lines`, `session_count`/`message_count`/`message_bytes`, and `dom_node_count` to attribute the isolated WebContent 3.27–3.43 GB RSS leak (Go backend steady 147–163 MB, TODO.md 2026-08-25). Web side adds `FrontendMemoryReporter` mounted in `App.tsx`, `terminalRegistry` (`registerTerminal`/`unregisterTerminal` hooked in `TerminalPanel.tsx`), and `web/src/lib/debug/*` with tests; desktop sets the window title to "ocode — Waiting for processes to gracefully shut down…" during graceful shutdown. Version bump `0.8.72` → `0.8.73`. (`internal/server/frontend_stats.go`, `internal/server/frontend_stats_test.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `web/src/lib/debug/frontendMemoryReporter.tsx`, `web/src/lib/debug/terminalRegistry.ts`, `web/src/lib/debug/terminalRegistry.test.ts`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.tsx`, `cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`, `internal/version/version.go`, `TODO.md`)
- **Scheduler Run History + Every-Interval Floor (2026-08-24)** — persistent per-job run records (`RunRecord`/`RunLogEntry` with `started_at`/`finished_at`/`duration_ms`/`status`/`input`/`output`/`error` + datetime-stamped `logs`) appended to `runs.jsonl` by `Dispatcher.OnJob` (filed before the outbox deliver step) and queryable via `GET /api/cron/{id}/runs?limit&offset`; web `CronPanel` now shows a `CronHistoryPanel` with paginated expandable rows, formatted durations, and per-run log rendering. Also enforces a 30 s floor on `every` schedules (`minEveryMs`) so a misconfigured `every_ms` (e.g. seconds instead of ms) cannot fire every few seconds forever. (`internal/scheduler/runs.go`, `internal/scheduler/dispatch.go`, `internal/scheduler/types.go`, `internal/scheduler/scheduler.go`, `internal/server/scheduler.go`, `internal/server/scheduler_runner.go`, `web/src/components/Cron/CronHistoryPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Cron/CronPanel.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`)
- **Editor Help & Shortcuts (2026-08-24)** — the file preview editor header now shows a `?` (HelpCircle) button that opens a dialog with how-to docs and keyboard shortcuts: app shortcuts (save, file picker, command palette, close tab) and Monaco editor shortcuts (find/replace, multi-cursor, move/copy line, etc., plus `F1` for the full command palette). (`web/src/components/Files/EditorHelpDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Files/FileEditor.tsx`)
- **AIHubMix Provider** — added the `aihubmix` provider (OpenAI-compatible `https://aihubmix.com/v1`, e.g. `aihubmix/gpt-4o-mini`) to the provider registry (`internal/auth/providers.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`) and the README/SETUP provider lists.
- **Auto-Permission Judge Model Picker (2026-08-23)** — `GET/PUT /api/config/permission-model` (`HandleGetPermissionModel`/`HandleSetPermissionModel`) read/write the auto-permission LLM judge model and its enabled gate independently (pointer-typed partial update: either `{model}` or `{enabled}` alone; `"auto"` normalizes to empty = fall back to the small model), persisting via `config.SavePermissionModel`/`SaveAutoPermissionEnabled` and preferring the live TUI snapshot when a bridge is attached; `TUIStatus` carries the new `permission_model` field through the headless build and bridge merge paths; web `CoworkSidebar` gains a working auto-permission toggle plus a clickable permission-model row, `ModelDialog` a `permission` purpose, and `PermissionsForm` a model field persisted through the dedicated setter on Save. Both this endpoint and the sibling small-model setter now apply changes to LIVE agents at runtime (atomic overrides on `Agent` — `SetAutoPermissionModel`/`SetSmallModelRuntime*`), so a bridged TUI agent — which holds its own config snapshot loaded at startup — enforces and *displays* the new state immediately instead of after a restart; TUI-side picks push through to the same override so last-writer-wins holds across surfaces. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/server/handler_tui_status.go`, `internal/server/tui_status.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/ModelDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/PermissionsForm.tsx`)
- **TUI Auto-Continue on Step-Limited Turns** — when a turn ends because it hit the step limit (reply cut off mid-task), an optional judge model (`Ocode.AutoContinueModel`) decides whether to resume; if enabled and the judge (or, when no judge is configured, the step-limit signal alone) says the reply was interrupted, ocode auto-fires a continuation. A chain counter (`autoContinueCount`, cap `autoContinueMaxChain` = 4) prevents runaway loops, and a sidebar toggle plus `OnAutoContinueJudge`/`AutoContinueJudgeAsync` (agent) and an RC-bridge judge hook wire it into both the TUI and headless/server paths. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/tui/autocontinue_test.go`)

(`internal/server/tui_status.go`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`)

- **Mid-Turn Message Injection Queue** — `Agent.injectQueue` with `EnqueueInjection`/`DrainPendingInjections`/`HasPendingInjections` spliced at the next tool-call boundary inside `Step()` so a follow-up user message sent while a turn is running lands mid-turn instead of queuing a whole new turn behind it; server `tryEnqueueInjection` + `flushStrandedInjections` with an immediate `user_message` SSE broadcast for the injected message, `SessionManager.IsTurnActive` guard to avoid tearing down an active turn, and TUI `handleChatKeys`/`streamEvent` handling that renders injected messages live and deduplicates the queued input. (`internal/agent/discovery_opt.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_opt_test.go`)

(`internal/server/handler_shutdown.go`, `internal/server/terminal_kill_*.go`, `internal/server/agent_session_profile_test.go`, `internal/server/terminal_kill_test.go`, `cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/desktop/boot.go`)
- **Profile Debug Toggle** — `GET/PUT /api/config/ocode/profile-debug` (`HandleGet/SetProfileDebugConfig`), `config.SaveProfileDebug`, per-window `emitProfileDebugForWindow` diagnostics (active profile, display name, model, override/credential counts, baseURL), `ProfileDebugForm` settings panel toggle, and `debuglog.KindProfile`. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/server/handler_profiles.go`, `internal/debuglog/debuglog.go`, `web/src/components/Settings/ProfileDebugForm.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/SettingsPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/ProfileSwitcher.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/ProfilesManager.tsx`)

### Changed
- **Profile-Aware LLM Client** — `NewClientWithProfile(cfg, model, profile)` layers per-profile sidecar credentials (`auth.profiles.json` / `auth.GetProfileCredential`), OAuth tokens, and base-URL overrides on top of config/base credentials (still behind `OPENCODE_AUTH_TOKEN`); `reconcileProfileAgent` rebuilds the session agent on profile switch without restarting the app; `NewClient` now delegates via `auth.ActiveProfile()`. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/auth/providers.go`, `internal/server/agent_session.go`, `internal/server/handler_profiles.go`, `web/src/components/Terminal/terminalPersistence.ts`)
- **Stable Per-Window WindowId** — desktop passes a stable `windowId=main` query param in the webview URL (including `OCODE_DESKTOP_DEV_URL` handling) so the SPA `sendMessage`/`chat` helpers prefer the URL `windowId` over the ephemeral `sessionStorage` random id, surviving webview reloads; new `authedFetch` helper injects token + base path for all `/api` calls. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `internal/desktop/boot.go`, `skills/ocode-desktop/SKILL.md`)
- **LSP Manager Ready Emission** — `emitReady` now fires `ServerStartedEvent{Phase:"ready"}` on every `ClientForExt` return path (cached, broker-attached, and isolated-stdio) so the sidebar indexing indicator always clears; previously only the isolated path emitted `ready`, leaving broker/cached paths stuck on "indexing…". (`internal/lsp/manager.go`)
- **Terminal + Web UI Parity** — `terminalPersistence` now keys per-profile, `TerminalPanel`/`LogPanel`/`ChatInput`/`ProfileSwitcher` tweaks for profile switching, and rebuilt `web/dist/index.html`. (`web/src/components/Terminal/terminalPersistence.ts`, `web/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Logs/LogPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatInput.tsx`, `web/dist/index.html`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.72 → 0.8.73: frontend memory observability ring + sidebar Context gauge preinit (previous commit), web/desktop slash-command parity via shared `commandctx` builders (+ title regeneration endpoint + runtime paths sync), TUI local-model multi-select picker, desktop search-root anchoring for `glob`/`grep`/`list`, MLX python3 login-shell PATH resolution + spawn liveness fail-fast, web chat merge-snapshot reload guard, `scripts/capture-desktop-hang.sh`. (`internal/version/version.go`, `scripts/capture-desktop-hang.sh`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.71 → 0.8.72: scheduler run history (persistent `runs.jsonl` + `GET /api/cron/{id}/runs` + `CronHistoryPanel`), web/desktop permission parity (out-of-scope path roots + session-scoped webfetch + dialog auto-close), headless model validation (reject bare `model` ids) + live propagation of `small-model`/`permission-model` to bridged agents, `every` floor (30 s), AIHubMix provider, wails `v3.0.0-beta.12` / `go-git` `v5.19.2` / `x/crypto` `v0.53.0` / `x/net` `v0.56.0` / `x/sys` `v0.46.0` bumps. (`internal/version/version.go`, `go.mod`, `go.sum`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.69 → 0.8.71: duplicate-key tool-call arguments skip at dispatch instead of ending the turn; auto-permission judge model picker (REST endpoints + web sidebar/settings UI). (`internal/version/version.go`)

### Changed
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.56 → 0.8.62: `--effort` CLI flag, project rename/reorder/group, terminal font/shell config, resizable sidebar, reasoning level selector, context-aware orphan recovery, per-project LSP managers, desktop app icon improvements, cross-compilation Dockerfile, terminal processes REST endpoint + immediate SSE publish, WebGL terminal renderer. (`internal/version/version.go`)

### Added
- **Shared LSP Broker** — cross-process language-server reuse via a loopback-TCP broker (`internal/lsp/broker/`): length-prefixed framed JSON protocol, authenticated handshake, reference-counted canonical document state, and compatibility-based sharing (incompatible clients fall back to isolated local servers). Enabled automatically for `gopls`; disable with `lsp_shared: false`. The `Manager`/`Client` APIs are unchanged — `ClientForExt` now attempts broker discovery + compatibility handshake before the local stdio path. (`internal/lsp/broker/`, `internal/lsp/manager.go`, `internal/lsp/client.go`, `internal/lsp/daemon.go`)
- **Desktop Per-Window Sparse Profiles** — seamless multi-key/config switching on desktop without touching the upstream auth contract. Base `ocodeconfig.json` plus a sparse `profiles:{name: delta}` overlay, a sidecar `~/.local/share/ocode/auth.profiles.json` (0600), and per-window `window-state.json` active profile. CLI parity via `OCODE_PROFILE=work ocode` / `ocode --profile work` (and desktop `OCODE_PROFILE` env); server APIs `GET/POST /api/profiles`, `DELETE`/`POST rename /api/profiles/{name}`, `GET/PUT /api/window/{id}/activeProfile` backed by an in-memory `windowProfiles` cache; web header profile pill (`ProfileSwitcher`) and a `Settings → Profiles` manager (`ProfilesManager`) with create/rename/delete + active/overridden indicators. (`internal/config/profile_*.go`, `internal/auth/profile_store.go`, `internal/server/handler_profiles.go`, `web/src/components/ProfileSwitcher.tsx`, `web/src/components/Settings/ProfilesManager.tsx`)
- **OrcaRouter Provider** — added the `orcarouter` provider (OpenAI-compatible `https://api.orcarouter.ai/v1`, e.g. `orcarouter/auto`) to the provider registry and the README/SETUP provider lists. (`internal/auth/providers.go`)
- **`--effort` CLI Flag** — new `-effort`/`--effort` argument accepts `off`, `low`, `med`, `high`, `xhigh`, or `max` to set the reasoning effort (thinking budget) level at launch. Validated against `ThinkingBudgetForLabel` and propagated to the agent's thinking budget config. (`main.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Project Rename / Reorder / Group** — the project store gains `Rename()`, `Reorder()`, and `SetGroup()` methods plus a new `GroupStore` for named project groups with collapsible state. Projects now carry `Order` (manual sort position, lower = higher) and `Group` fields; `List()` sorts by `Order` ascending with unassigned projects at the end. New API endpoints: `POST /api/projects/rename`, `POST /api/projects/reorder`, `POST /api/projects/group`. (`internal/projects/projects.go`, `internal/server/handler_projects.go`)
- **Terminal Font and Shell Configuration** — three new config fields: `terminal_font_family` (override the terminal's font stack), `terminal_font_size` (override font size in px, default 13, clamped 8–32), and `terminal_shell` (override which shell binary the interactive terminal starts; empty means use `$SHELL`/`%COMSPEC%`). `AvailableShells()` reads `/etc/shells` on Unix or probes PATH for common shells on Windows. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/config/terminal_shell.go`)
- **Resizable Sidebar** — new `useResizableSidebar` hook provides drag-to-resize with localStorage persistence (min 160px, max 500px, default 240px). (`web/src/hooks/useResizableSidebar.ts`)
- **Reasoning Level Selector** — new `ReasoningLevelSelector` web component renders a compact dropdown in the sidebar to cycle through reasoning effort levels (off → low → med → high → xhigh → max), persisting via `api.setThinkingBudget()`. (`web/src/components/Layout/ReasoningLevelSelector.tsx`)
- **Context Menu Component** — new `ContextMenu` component for right-click menus in the web UI. (`web/src/components/Layout/ContextMenu.tsx`)
- **Editor Tab Persistence** — new `editorTabsPersistence` module saves open editor tabs to localStorage per session. (`web/src/components/Files/editorTabsPersistence.ts`)
- **Undo/Redo Last Change on Agent** — `UndoLastChange()` and `RedoLastChange()` methods on `Agent` provide session-scoped undo/redo via the snapshot store, replacing the legacy process-wide `snapshot.Undo()`. (`internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **Terminal Process Registry** — `terminalRegistry` tracks the PID of every open terminal pty for per-terminal CPU/memory reporting via the new `terminalProcsEmitter` SSE stream. (`internal/server/terminal_registry.go`, `internal/server/emitters.go`)
