Requests to a locally-managed chat model server now route through `localConcurrencyTransport`, a cross-process counting semaphore (lock files under `<cache-dir>/locks/`, 100ms poll, stale-after `llmRequestTimeout+1m` for crashed holders) keyed by model id — the deterministic port assignment means every ocode process talks to the same physical server, so a per-process semaphore would undercount. `max_parallel` is read fresh from disk per request via new `config.LocalModelConfigFor`. (`internal/agent/local_model_limiter.go`, `internal/agent/local_model_limiter_test.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Local Model Start Serialized Within and Across Processes** — `StartModelInstance` now takes a per-model in-process mutex (`lockForStart`) AND a cross-process `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` start-lock file (`acquireChatStartLock`, stale-after 6 min so a crashed holder's lock is reclaimed, same primitive as the config lock and limiter slots), so two ocode processes racing to auto-start the same enabled model — or a relaunched process racing a still-starting prior one — can no longer both spawn a competing server on the same deterministic port. The lock loser and the spawn-failure path re-probe and adopt the winner via the shared `waitForChatHealth` poll; new `OwnsModelInstance` distinguishes spawned-vs-adopted so `/localmodel limit` won't silently no-op a stop on a server owned by another process. MLX chat spawns are forced offline (`HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`): mlx_lm.server hits the HF Hub API on every launch even when cached and fails loudly (404/401) for repos that aren't publicly reachable. (`internal/discovery/instances.go`, `internal/tui/command_test.go`)
- **TUI Local-Model Warm-Up Refactor** — `warmLocalModelIfNeededCmd` split into `localModelNeedsWarm` (pure decision with an injectable `probeLocalModelInstance` for tests) + `startLocalModelCmd(modelID, switchModel)`. A terminal-failure `InstanceStopped` record no longer counts as "already running", fixing a deadlock where every retry skipped warm-up and left the model permanently unusable until process restart. New warm-only `warmLocalModelCmd` starts a server for non-main-model roles (e.g. the auto-permission model) without touching the active model (`modelSwitchWarmedMsg.switchModel`). `localModelUsage` const is shared between the command table and in-command help, with a `help` subcommand; sidebar model toggles render single-line via `clampSidebarModelName`/`renderSidebarModelToggle`. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`, `internal/tui/command_test.go`, `internal/tui/perm_model_sidebar_test.go`)
- **Local Models Excluded from the Model Picker** — Enabled `/localmodel` ids are no longer unioned into the chat model picker lists (`openModelPicker`/`loadFullModelPickerCmd`/`buildFullModelPickerItems`), consistent with the instance-manager design's non-goal: `/localmodel` stays start/stop/query, and selection is by typing `/model local/<id>` (which warms the server). (`internal/tui/picker.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **`.ojsonl` Session Shrink Rewritten Instead of Erroring** — When a save's message count is smaller than the persisted count (e.g. `/compact` spliced out old messages), `saveOjsonl` now rewrites the whole file (`rewriteOjsonlFull`: temp file + rename, preserving the header `CreatedAt`/title) instead of failing with "persisted count N exceeds provided count M" and leaving stale content on disk that a later resume would load. (`internal/session/ojsonl.go`, `internal/session/session.go`, `internal/session/session_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.36 → 0.8.39. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Conduct Skill Re-Tuned Post-GA (deepseek-v4-flash)** — Re-benchmarked deepseek-v4-flash on the full 49-question conduct corpus after its preview→GA release; `tuned_version` bumped 0 → 1. Stack score 83.9% → 79.3%: safety (0.34) remains the weakest tag, hallucination (0.62), simplicity (0.67), and error-handling (0.73) newly surface below threshold, while surgical-changes improved to 0.86 and its digest section was dropped. Scorecard, answer transcript, and the derived + kaizen skill digests regenerated. (`docs/okf/conduct/scores/deepseek-v4-flash.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/answers/deepseek-v4-flash.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/derived/conduct.deepseek-v4-flash.SKILL.md`, `skills/kaizen/conduct-tuning-deepseek-v4-flash/SKILL.md`)
- **Stream-Done Compaction Barrier Test** — Added `TestStreamDoneCompactionIsABarrier` to lock in that a `streamDoneMsg` compaction is a barrier: queued input and pending job messages stay deferred and recap is not started until `compactFinishedMsg` resumes the session. (`internal/tui/compact_anchored_repro_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.33 → 0.8.35. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Subagent Concurrency Limiter + `/agents` Command** — Task-tool subagent dispatches now draw from a single shared, resizable concurrency pool (`AgentRunRegistry` + `agentConcurrencyLimiter`) instead of each registry enforcing its own independent cap, so the true number of concurrently active agents respects one session-wide limit. New `max_concurrent_agents` config (default 2, `0` = unlimited) and `/agents [status|limit <n>]` command to inspect running/queued subagents and change the live limit. Dispatches beyond the limit register as a new `RunQueued` status and wait for a slot; queued runs are cancelled cleanly on session shutdown (`/new`, agent replacement, `task_cancel`) via stop-channel-aware `Acquire`. Synchronous nested `task` dispatches hand their slot back to the shared pool while blocked (`pauseOwnSlotForNestedCall`) so a low limit can't self-deadlock against a dispatch chain's own ancestors. Server `RunState.Ended` now treats only terminal statuses as ended, so queued runs aren't reported finished to desktop/web watchers. `/agents status` stays instant while streaming; `/agents limit` queues like other persistent state mutations. (`internal/agent/agent_runs.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/run_states.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Permission Dialog Pauses Job-Result Injection** — While a permission dialog is up, incoming job-result messages are buffered as pending (`pendingJobMsgs`) instead of being spliced into the transcript, and queue draining is blocked, so approval state can't be clobbered by concurrent stream output. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Agent Detail Card Row-Shift Cleanup** — Removed the post-render row offset previously applied to run/process/expand regions in the agent detail card, keeping hit-testing coordinates aligned with the rendered card. (`internal/tui/detail_view.go`)
- **`rebuildAgentClient` Rewires Live-Agent Callbacks** — Rebuilding the agent after `/connect` (OAuth finish or API-key entry) now re-applies the same live wiring as `installAgent`: `wireCompactCallbacks` (OnCompact/OnCompactStart/OnRecap/OnUsage/OnSideUsage), `SetSupervisor`, max-steps/memory/doc-prompt config, and the RC bridge agent pointer. Previously the rebuilt agent had nil callbacks, so the next auto-compaction's result was silently dropped, `pendingCompactUIIdx` never cleared, and both compaction and input submission stayed queued for the rest of the session. (`internal/tui/connect.go`, `internal/tui/rebuild_agent_test.go`)
- **Compaction Decisions Logged Durably** — `MaybeCompactAsync` now logs why it declined to compact ("below threshold: ~N tokens used, window=…, limit=…" or "auto-compaction disabled in config"), and the TUI mirrors all `COMPACT` debug entries to `<data-dir>/logs/compact.log` (2MB cap with single `.1` rotation) via the new `debuglog.MirrorKindToFile`. The in-memory log holds only the last 500 entries, which previously made "auto compact never ran" undiagnosable after the fact. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/compact_decline_log_test.go`, `internal/debuglog/mirror.go`, `internal/debuglog/mirror_test.go`, `internal/debuglog/debuglog.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Async Sync Login with Inline Auth Code Display** — Replaced blocking `client.Login()` with a two-phase device-code flow (`StartDevice` returns immediately with an auth code; `PollDevice` runs in the background via `tea.Tick`). The auth code and verification URL appear in the TUI transcript immediately, and approval polling status is updated in real-time. Removed old Google OAuth `/login`; renamed `/sync-login`→`/login` and `/sync-logout`→`/logout` (with `/sync-logout` retained as an alias). (`internal/tui/command_sync_login.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tui/command_sync_login_test.go`, `internal/tui/command_test.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/server.go`)
- **Default `sed` Ban** — `sed` is now banned by default (`PermissionDeny`) in `NewPermissionManager`, matching the `/ban` help text `(default: sed)`. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/permissions_test.go`)
- **Changes Tab Eager Diff Caching** — Extracted `ensureDiffCached` helper; diff is now pre-cached on navigation keys (j/k/g/G) for instant display, not just on enter. Fallback text changed from `<diff for ...>` to `(no changes to show)`. (`internal/tui/changes_model.go`)
- **AST Test Uses Isolated Temp Dir** — `TestAstTool_ReferencesByName` creates temp `.go` files and an LSP manager instead of relying on the project's real source tree. (`internal/tool/ast_test.go`)
- **Gemini Image Gen URL Fix** — Gemini API endpoint corrected from `/v1/models/...` to `/v1beta/models/...`. (`internal/tool/imagegen_test.go`)
- **`load_skill` Registered as Builtin** — Added `load_skill` to the expected builtin tools list. (`internal/tool/tool_test.go`)
- **Sync Default URL Set to Localhost for Dev** — `DefaultBaseURL` changed from `https://kakiit.com` to `http://localhost:3201`. (`internal/sync/sync.go`)
- **Git Commit Push Prompt Consolidated** — Merged separate "Stage updated docs" and "Commit and push" steps into a single "Commit and push" step in the `/git-commit-push` prompt. (`internal/commands/parse_check_test.go`)
- **Account Sync / Config Sync** — New `internal/sync/` and `internal/hook/` packages implementing device-code auth (`/login`, `/logout`) and encrypted, deferred, auto-merging config sync of `ocodeconfig.json` and `auth.json` between machines. Hooks at both config-save choke points (`auth.OnCredentialsSaved`, `config.OnConfigSaved`) debounce background push. Server registers sync API routes (`POST /api/sync/login/start`, `POST /api/sync/login/poll`, `GET /api/sync/status`, `POST /api/sync/logout`) and `Handler` manages sync client lifecycle. Web frontend gains `SyncStatusWidget` in `TopTabs` with API client methods and TypeScript types. (`internal/sync/*`, `internal/hook/*`, `internal/auth/store.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/tui/command_sync_login.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/SyncStatusWidget.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/TopTabs.tsx`)
- **Advisor User-Facing Improvements** — Checkpoint prompts (plan/done) now include "the executor cannot answer follow-up questions" to prevent the advisor from requesting more info the caller can't supply. The advisor tool prompt adds a new "WHEN THE REQUEST ITSELF IS INSUFFICIENT" section telling the advisor to say what's missing rather than guess. Claude Code advisor prompt also updated to handle vague requests. (`internal/agent/advisor_checkpoint.go`, `internal/agent/advisor_tool.go`)
- **Advisor Model Picker Updated** — Claude Sonnet 5 and Opus 5 added to the Claude Code (Read-Only CLI) section of the advisor model picker. (`internal/tui/picker.go`)
- **`HandleApprovedToolCall` Signature Updated** — Now accepts a `toolCallID` string parameter, passed through from TUI approval and server permission resolution, enabling undo tracking for approved tool calls. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.32 → 0.8.33. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Minor Refactors** — `alwaysVisibleDotfiles` and `fileSearchResult` moved from function-local to package-level scope in TUI model. `authPath()` exported as `AuthPath()` for use by `internal/sync`. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/auth/store.go`)
- **Editor Context Clears on Tab Switch** — The web editor context chip and outgoing `@path` attachment now clear when the active tab leaves the editor, so stale file references no longer leak into chat after switching to non-editor tabs. (`web/src/hooks/useEditorTabs.ts`)
- **Undo Registry Cleans Up Reverted Writes** — Successful `undo_file_change` now unregisters the reverted cross-agent write record from the active write registry, so an older undo is no longer blocked by a newer change that has already been rolled back. (`internal/snapshot/snapshot.go`, `internal/snapshot/snapshot_test.go`)
- **Desktop Window Quits on Close** — The ocode desktop app now terminates the process when the window is closed, even when a system tray is registered, via a `WindowClosing` → `app.Quit()` event handler. Without this, the system tray keeps the process (and its in-process server) alive after the window closes — surprising on macOS where closing the last window normally terminates a non-tray app. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`)
- **MCP Tool Loading Refactored for Caching** — `LoadMCPToolsForConfig` extracted as a stateless function from `agent.LoadMCPTools`, enabling process-wide caching. `LoadExternalToolsWithMCP` replaced with `LoadExternalTools` + `AddMCPTools`/`AddMCPErrors` at all session creation sites. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`)
- **Scheduler Update API (Backend)** — New `JobPatch` struct and `UpdateJob` method for partial job updates on the scheduler engine, supporting `Enabled`, `Name`, `Schedule`, and all `Payload` fields as optional patches. New `PATCH /api/cron/{id}` REST endpoint with `cronUpdateRequest` type and `toSchedule()` helper. The `update` handler reads the existing job, applies only the provided fields, and returns the merged result. Route registered as `PATCH /api/cron/{id}` in `attachScheduler`. Includes `TestUpdateJob` covering enable/disable, name/payload edit, schedule change with `NextRunAtMs` recomputation, invalid-schedule rejection, and not-found errors. (`internal/scheduler/scheduler.go`, `internal/scheduler/scheduler_test.go`, `internal/server/scheduler.go`, `internal/server/scheduler_update_http_test.go`)
- **Web Cron UI** — New `CronPanel` React component (`web/src/components/Cron/CronPanel`) with full CRUD: list, add, enable/disable, edit schedule/payload, and delete jobs. New "Cron" tab in `TopTabs` with `CalendarClock` icon. API client (`web/src/api/client.ts`) adds `listCronJobs`, `getCronJob`, `addCronJob`, `updateCronJob`, `deleteCronJob`, `getCronOutbox`, `drainCronOutbox`, `getCronTargets`, `setCronTarget`. TypeScript types (`web/src/api/types.ts`) cover `CronJob`, `CronSchedule`, `CronPayload`, `CronJobState`, `CronDelivery`, and request/response interfaces. Both `App.tsx` (standalone) and `SessionPage.tsx` (session mode) render the panel. (`web/src/components/Cron/*`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/TopTabs.tsx`)
- **Per-Session Changes Tab (TUI)** — New `changesModel` and `internal/changes/` package implementing a dedicated tab listing every file added or edited by the current chat session (main agent + sub-agents), with per-file unified diffs and undo (file-level and block-level). The file list derives from the snapshot store (`internal/snapshot`) and a pre/post-stat bash detection hook rather than git. Sub-agent writes are captured via the shared snapshot store. Tab registered as the last tab; opens with `.` shortcut. (`internal/changes/*`, `internal/tui/changes_model.go`, `internal/tui/changes_model_test.go`, `internal/tui/tabs.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/snapshot/snapshot.go`, `internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `docs/changes-tab.md`, `AGENTS.md`)
- **Per-Session Changes Tab (Web)** — New "Changes" tab in the web frontend (`TopTabs` with `History` icon), `ChangesPanel` React component (`web/src/components/Changes/`), API client methods (`listChanges`, `getChangeDiff`, `undoChangeFile`, `undoChangeBlock`), TypeScript types (`FileChange`, `FileChangeStatus`, `ChangeAuthor`, `ChangeDiff`), and backend route shims in `internal/server/server.go` with handler implementation (`internal/server/handler_changes.go`). Wired into both `App.tsx` (standalone) and `SessionPage.tsx` (session mode). (`web/src/components/Changes/*`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/TopTabs.tsx`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/handler_changes.go`, `internal/server/handler_changes_test.go`)
- **Bash Deny-List Slash Command** — New `/ban` command lists banned bash command prefixes, seeds `sed` as a default deny rule, supports multi-word prefixes like `grep -n`, supports add/remove updates by persisting the underlying bash prefix permission rules, and `/ban clear` opens a confirmation dialog before wiping all bans. (`internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Advisor Checkpoints (plan/done)** — New `advisor.checkpoints` config field (default `["plan", "done"]`, set `[]` to disable) makes the agent loop enforce advisor review at two points: the "plan" checkpoint defers the first write-tool batch until the advisor has vetted the proposed changes, and the "done" checkpoint verifies the completion claim before the turn ends. Only fires when the advisor is enabled and the checkpoint is listed. (`internal/agent/advisor_checkpoint.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **NoteBus OnAppend + TUI Real-Time Note Streaming** — The shared notes bus gains a `SetOnAppend` callback, wired through the agent's `OnNoteBusEntry` hook, so note/touch entries from parallel agent groups appear as transient messages in the TUI transcript in real-time. Non-blocking drop on backpressure. (`internal/notebus/notebus.go`, `internal/agent/group_bus.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Multi-File Touch Paths** — `extractTouchFilePaths` now handles `multi_file_edit` by collecting all file paths from the `edits[]` array, so the note bus records a touch for every modified file instead of only the first. (`internal/agent/group_bus_handoff.go`)
- **Write Tool Classification Expanded** — `isWriteTool` now includes `replace_lines`, `multiedit`, `multi_file_edit`, `delete`, and `format` alongside `write`, `edit`, and `apply_patch`. (`internal/agent/group_bus_handoff.go`)
- **`SaveAdvisorModel` Targeted Reset** — Clearing the advisor model (empty string) now only resets `Provider` and `Model` fields, not `Enabled` or `Checkpoints`, so unrelated policy state is not silently restored when using the model-picker command. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Unified Queue Replaces Three Separate Queues** — `queuedCommands`, `queuedInputs`, and `queuedCompactInputs` are consolidated into a single `queuedItems []queuedItem` slice typed by `queueItemKind` (`queueItemInput` / `queueItemCommand` / `queueItemCompactInput`). Drain/render/recall dispatch by type while preserving insertion order across all previously-separate queues. This eliminates duplicated concatenation-and-drain logic in `agentStreamDoneMsg`/`compactFinishedMsg` handlers and fixes an edge case where the `Up` arrow (recall-last-input) could only pop from one queue at a time. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/command_test.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **AGENTS.md Queue Documentation** — Updated the queuing section to reflect the `queuedItems` unified queue and its `pendingCompactInputs` → `queuedItems` migration. (`AGENTS.md`)
- **Kaizen Review Skill Updates** — `skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md` now uses relative `docs/okf` paths, adopts a hostile external-reviewer audit stance with verbatim evidence requirements, adds a materiality threshold for recording patterns, sources conduct tags from `docs/okf/conduct/meta.yaml` instead of a remembered list, reorganizes around a procedure-by-reference pattern, and adds "UPDATE skill" as a fix-type option. (`skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md`)
- **Kaizen Review Skill Expands Audit Scope** — `skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md` now audits context-efficiency/accuracy patterns (whole-file reads, redundant searches, skipped docs, premature action) alongside tool-call failures, adds a Step 1c tooling-gap analysis (NEW tool / UPDATE tool / NEW skill recommendations), and routes verdicts to new `context-efficiency` / `context-accuracy` Kaizen conduct tags. (`skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md`)
- **`isSensitivePath` Allows Common `.env.*` Templates** — `.env.example`, `.env.sample`, `.env.template`, and `.env.dist` are now excluded from sensitive-path detection so they are not flagged as secret-bearing files. Only `.env` and its production/local variants (`.env.local`, `.env.production`, etc.) remain sensitive. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`)
- **`isAllowedDevicePath` Centralized** — `isWithinAllowedScope` and redirection-path checks in `decideSingleCommand` now delegate to a common `isAllowedDevicePath` helper covering `/dev/null`, `/dev/stdin`, `/dev/stdout`, `/dev/stderr`, and `/dev/tty`. This extends allowed-scope coverage to all five device paths (previously the redirection check handled three) and adds `/dev/tty` to the redirection allow list. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`)
- **Scheduled Jobs / Cron Engine** — New `internal/scheduler` package: a persistent, disk-backed cron engine (JSON store, ≤60s poll legs, jitter, 7-day recurring expiry, one-shot auto-delete, panic isolation, mtime-based external reload) supporting `at` / `every` / `cron` (5-field via `robfig/cron/v3` + IANA `tz`). A host-injected `OnJobFunc` dispatches each firing through `agent.Step` into a persistent `cron:<id>` session (capped retention), with per-job permission mode (default `normal`). Wired into the long-lived hosts: `serve`/`web` via `Server.SetScheduler` + `schedulerSetup()`, and `cmd/ocode-desktop` via `desktop.AttachScheduler`; the TUI only authors/manages jobs through the shared store. REST surface: `GET/POST /api/cron`, `GET/DELETE /api/cron/{id}`, `GET /api/cron/outbox?drain=true&limit=N`, `GET/POST /api/cron/targets`. New LLM `cron` tool (`add`/`list`/`remove`/`describe`, registered via an `any` resolver to avoid a tool↔scheduler import cycle) and TUI `/cron` slash command. Delivery fans out to both the Telegram bot (`srv.AttachTelegramBot` auto-registers on `/session`) and the live TUI (`RCBridge.CronDeliveryCh`). Concept doc at `docs/scheduled-jobs.md`. (`internal/scheduler/*`, `internal/tool/cron.go`, `internal/tui/command_cron.go`, `internal/server/scheduler*.go`, `internal/desktop/scheduler.go`, `internal/telegram/bot.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `main.go`, `docs/index.md`, `docs/scheduled-jobs.md`)
- **Undo Support for Destructive Bash Commands** — `BashTool` now snapshots the target file(s) of a narrow whitelist of destructive commands (`rm`, `mv`, `cp`, `sed -i`, `truncate`) before execution via the context snapshot store, so `undo_file_change` can revert them — the same mechanism the Edit/Write tools use. Compound commands (pipes, chains, redirection, substitution) are never misclassified as backed-up. (`internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/tool/bash_backup.go`, `internal/snapshot`)
- **Permission Interpreter Tolerates String-vs-Array** — `interpreterEffects.UnmarshalJSON` now accepts a bare JSON string in place of a one-element array for every effect field (`reads`/`writes`/`deletes`/`network`/`subprocesses`/`db_destructive`/`unknown`), so small local models that emit a single path as a string no longer fail the whole permission-interpretation response. (`internal/agent/permission_interpreter.go`)
- **Undo Window Widened + Configurable** — `undo_file_change`'s expiry window default is raised from 2 → 10 agent steps and is now configurable via `ocode.undo_max_age_delta` in `ocodeconfig.json` (0/unset → default 10). `UndoTool` reads the value through its new `Config` field; the tool description reports the active window. (`internal/tool/undo.go`, `internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/tool/undo_test.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Sidebar "✦ gen" Title Button — Blink + Re-click Guard** — While a manual title regeneration is in flight the button now blinks in place (alternating faint/underline on the 400ms tick) and a second click is suppressed until it completes, so a rapid re-click reads as "already running" instead of looking like a dropped click. `regenerateTitle()` re-arms the dot tick while `titleRegenerating` is set. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Tool Result Preview Sizing** — The TUI tool-output preview window is raised from 12 → 20 lines (`toolOutputPreviewLines`), collapsible boxes now show a ▸/▾ toggle only when content exceeds the preview limit, and the web `ToolBlock` auto-collapses when output exceeds 50 lines and shows a "· N lines" count so large outputs don't dominate the transcript. (`internal/tui/tool_render.go`, `internal/tui/tool_output_test.go`, `internal/tui/detail_view_test.go`, `web/src/components/Chat/TurnParts.tsx`)
- **Image Generation Timeout Raised + Configurable** — The `imagegen` tool's per-request timeout is raised from a hardcoded 10 minutes to a built-in default of 15 minutes, and is now configurable: the persisted `ImageGenConfig.Timeout` field (seconds, `ocodeconfig.json`) sets a global default, and the new per-call `timeout` tool argument (seconds) overrides it. `/image timeout <seconds>` sets and persists the config value; `/image status` shows the active timeout. (`internal/tool/imagegen.go`, `internal/config/imagegen_config.go`, `internal/tui/imagegen_cmd.go`)
- **Kaizen Review Skill — Session-Log Scanning** — `skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md` Step 1 now dispatches a subagent to mechanically scan the on-disk session log for tool failures, rather than relying on in-context memory of the compacted transcript. Includes session-file resolution, structured grep for failure markers, and verbatim-match reporting. (`skills/kaizen-review/SKILL.md`)
- **OpenAI Responses Tool-Pair Reconciliation Refactored** — The inline `function_call`/`function_call_output` pairing logic in `chatOpenAIResponses` is extracted into `reconcileOpenAIResponsesToolPairs()`, which now enforces bidirectional 1:1 pairing: both missing outputs (auto-filled) and orphaned outputs (output with no matching call, dropped) are handled, preventing 400 errors from both mismatch directions. (`internal/agent/client.go`)
- **Headless Runs Disable Discovery by Default** — `internal/runcli/run.go` now sets `cfg.Ocode.Discovery.Enabled = false` for headless runs, skipping the cold-start blocking summarize/embed pass since there is no TUI to show discovery state or warm the corpus. Discovery-disabled mode fails open — all skills/tools attach regardless. (`internal/runcli/run.go`)
- **`alwaysVisibleDotfiles` for ctrl+p File Search** — The TUI file search palette now permits a small set of commonly-edited config files (`.gitignore`, `.editorconfig`, `.gitattributes`, `.env.example`) to appear in results even when hidden-file filtering is active, so users can reach them via ctrl+p without toggling hidden files. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Permission `.path_pattern` Suffix Support** — `isOutOfScopePathRequest` and `outOfScopePathRoot` now recognise the `.path_pattern` rule suffix alongside `.out_of_scope`, extending out-of-scope path resolution to path-pattern permission rules. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **`.gitignore` Log-File Exclusion** — `/*.log` added to root `.gitignore` so generated log files at the project root are not accidentally committed. (`.gitignore`)
- **ListBox Word-Wrap Mode** — The shared `ListBox` component (`internal/tui/component_listbox.go`) gains a `wrap` mode: items can word-wrap across multiple physical lines instead of being truncated to one, with a materialized `lineMap` built in `Layout()` so `Render` and `HitTest` always agree on click targets regardless of wrapped geometry. Enabled on the changes tab file list (`internal/tui/changes_model.go`) and the git-model changes section (`internal/tui/git_model.go`, two sites). Includes `TestListBoxWrapHitTest` verifying that clicks on any physical line of a wrapped item resolve to the correct item index. (`internal/tui/component_listbox.go`, `internal/tui/component_listbox_test.go`, `internal/tui/changes_model.go`, `internal/tui/git_model.go`)
- **Shell Compound Commands Bypass Out-of-Scope Detection** — `firstOutOfScopePath` in `internal/agent/permissions.go` now returns early (empty string) when the command is a shell compound (`a && b`, `a || b`, `a; b`, pipelines), preventing the scoping check from flagging a path from a later sub-command as out-of-scope when the first sub-command's path is unrelated. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`)
- **Agent-Run Collapse State Survives Session Tab Switches** — The web `chatStore` now tracks collapsed agent-run rows per session (`collapsedRunIds` on each `SessionSlice`, toggled by a new `TOGGLE_RUN_COLLAPSED` action), so rows collapsed in the agent preview rail / Agents panel stay collapsed when switching session tabs or projects instead of resetting to expanded on every `RunNode` remount. (`web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`)

### Added
- **In-Batch Task DAG (`id` / `depends_on`)** — A parallel batch of `task` calls that declares `id` and/or `depends_on` is now scheduled as a dependency graph instead of the legacy WaitGroup fan-out (`dagHasDependencies` is the gate; omitting both fields preserves the flat path byte-identically). The scheduler validates the graph (unknown id, duplicate id, or cycle → hard error and no node in the affected component runs; a `depends_on` naming a `run_in_background` node is rejected at validation), walks it wave-by-wave, never acquires a concurrency slot until every predecessor has resolved (the load-bearing invariant that would otherwise deadlock a 3-node chain under `max_concurrent_agents=2`), prepends each predecessor's final result to the dependent's `context`, scopes failure propagation to a node's transitive successors (independent nodes still run), and respects cancellation. Only subagent dispatches participate — `isDAGEligibleCall` keeps `agent_status`/`bash_output`/`kill_shell`, which share the `id` parameter name, off the scheduler. (`internal/agent/task_dag.go`, `internal/agent/task_dag_test.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`)
- **Task Output Contracts (`expected_output`)** — A `task` dispatch may carry an optional `expected_output`: a short natural-language description of the shape/content the result must have (e.g. "the full list of affected files, one path per line"). When set — or when the agent definition carries an `expected_output:` frontmatter — the sub-agent's final result is verified against the contract before being returned, and retried once in place when it does not match. Verification is a single small-model call (`internal/agent/prompts/task_verifier.txt`) that checks **shape, not truth** — a result claiming completion satisfies a contract it never fulfilled, so the verdict is presented as a shape check, never "verified correct". Verdicts (satisfied / deficiency / check-failed) are surfaced in `agent_status`/`task_status`, the TUI agent strip and detail view, and the web Agents tab via a new DTO `contract` field with a "contract ✗" badge on failed runs. No built-in agent declares a default contract — verification is opt-in per dispatch. (`internal/agent/task_contract.go`, `internal/agent/task_contract_test.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/agent/agent_loader.go`, `internal/agent/prompts/task_verifier.txt`, `internal/server/handler_runs.go`, `internal/tui/detail_view.go`, `web/src/components/Agents/RunNode.tsx`)
- **Persistent Per-Session Todo Plan** — `todowrite`/`todoread`/`todo_update` are now backed by a durable, user-visible, git-diffable markdown file at `.ocode/todo/<session-id>.md` (gitignored) instead of ephemeral in-memory state: revisioned (`# Todo (revision N)`), optimistically concurrent (every mutation must cite the revision returned by `todoread`; a stale revision is rejected with the current content), and written atomically (temp file + fsync + rename) under a cross-process advisory lock (`internal/filelock`, the shared flock/LockFileEx helper the knowledge bundle lock now delegates to) that spans the whole read → verify revision → apply → write sequence so two processes can never both write revision N+1. The main agent is the only writer — subagents get `todoread` only — and a user-role `[ocode:todo]` tail re-anchors the agent on the plan without busting the prompt cache (it rides the uncached suffix, coalescing with the current turn). Targeted `todo_update` ops (`set_status`, `edit_text`, `append`, `insert_after`) replace whole-plan rewrites, and a destructive full replacement — a `todowrite` dropping completed items — is rejected outright, pointing the model at `todo_update`. (`internal/tool/todo_store.go`, `internal/agent/todo_inject.go`, `internal/filelock/` (new), `internal/tool/todo_test.go`)
- **Multi-Session Tabs in the Web/Desktop UI** — The web/desktop frontend now keeps a per-project set of open session tabs: every project has a "New session" tab (auto-ensured, idempotent via `ENSURE_NEW_TAB`/`openNewSessionTab`), opening a new or existing session opens a tab instead of replacing the current one, and switching projects in the left sidebar swaps to that project's own tab set and restores its active tab. Tabs persist across app restarts in versioned `localStorage` (`ocode.ui.tabs.v1`, debounced writes, temp `new-*` tabs never persisted, corrupt/absent state tolerated). A deep link (`/session/:id` — including the desktop `-session <id>` flag) opens the session as a tab on the Home view beside the New tab; `SessionPage` is now a thin redirect into that flow. Tab labels are replaced by each session's real title once available and ellipsized (`max-w-28 truncate`): the title is fetched with a lightweight `limit:1` session detail on tab open, and live updates arrive via a new status-only SSE subscription (`/api/chat/messages?events=status`) that the Home view keeps open (previously it only polled `/api/tui-status` once at mount). (`web/src/stores/projectStore.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/OpenSessionBar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/SessionDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/SessionTabSync.tsx` (new), `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`)
- **Headless Session-Title Generation** — The web/desktop server (no TUI attached) now generates a session title after the first assistant turn of an untitled session, mirroring the TUI's `maybeGenerateTitle`: a one-shot background `agent.GenerateTitleAsync` call, persisted via `session.Save` and broadcast as a `status` SSE event carrying `session_id` + `session_title` so connected browsers update tab labels live. A per-session in-flight guard (`titleGenState` + `tryClaimTitleGen`) prevents double generation, and the path is a strict no-op when an RC bridge is attached (TUI-owned sessions keep the TUI's titles). (`internal/server/title_gen.go` (new), `internal/server/title_gen_test.go` (new), `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`)
- **SSE Event Allowlist on the Session Mirror** — `GET /api/chat/messages` now accepts `?events=status,turn_done` to forward only matching events (client-side, the initial history frame is filtered too). Lets the web tab bar subscribe to status updates without receiving every message/tool payload on the bus. (`internal/server/handler_sse.go`)
- **Desktop `-session <id>` Flag** — `ocode-desktop` accepts `-session <id>`/`--session <id>` (erroring on a missing value) and opens the webview at `/session/<id>`, which the SPA converts into a session tab beside the New tab. Works with both the embedded SPA and `OCODE_DESKTOP_DEV_URL`. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`)
- **Cross-Project `.ojsonl` Session Lookup** — `session.Load` now searches every project's session store for a bare timestamp session id when it isn't found in the current project's directory (the `.ojsonl` path previously only supported the in-project fallback, so desktop sessions launched from a different cwd resumed empty). (`internal/session/session.go`)
- **Changes Tab Scrollable Diff Preview + Open-in-Editor** — The changes tab's right-hand diff pane is now a scrollable viewport: `ctrl+d`/`pgdown` and `ctrl+u`/`pgup` scroll half a page, navigation between files jumps the preview to the top, and `ctrl+e` opens the selected file in the configured editor (shared tmux-aware opener when wired, `$EDITOR`/`vi` fallback otherwise; binary files open with the OS default). (`internal/tui/changes_model.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Hidden Agents Dispatchable Only From Their Own Plugin** — Task-tool dispatch of a hidden agent (e.g. the orchestrator pipeline's internal planner/explorer/developer/validator subagents) now requires the caller to be defined in the same source directory as the target; the main LLM or any agent outside the plugin gets an "internal-only" error instead of invoking it. (`internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/agent/subagent_hidden_scope_test.go`)
- **`list_processes` Tool** — New parallel tool that lists still-running background processes started with `bash(run_in_background)`, sorted by start time (oldest first). Displays each process's ID, status, start time, and command. The `ProcInfo` snapshot now includes a `StartedAt` field. (`internal/tool/process_tools.go`, `internal/tool/process.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/permissions.go`)
- **Subdirectory Instruction Injection (Claude Code Parity)** — When a tool call touches a file in a subdirectory, the agent now lazily scans that directory's ancestors (up to, not including, the project root) for `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, or `OCODE.md` and injects their content into the volatile prompt tail on the next model iteration. Each directory is scanned at most once per session (`dirMDSeen` guard). Root-level docs are already part of the always-on system prompt via `LoadContext` and are skipped. `trackDirMDTouch` maps tool names to their path argument key (`read`→`path`, `multiedit`→`file_path`, etc.) and runs after every successful tool call; `injectDirMDTail` drains the pending blocks into the user-role `[ocode:discovery]` tail so the cached system prefix is unchanged. (`internal/agent/dir_docs.go`, `internal/agent/dir_docs_test.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`)

### Fixed
- **Git Diff Returns Empty Array for Clean Repos** — `HandleGitDiff` now uses `make([]GitDiffFile, 0)` instead of `var files []GitDiffFile`, returning JSON `[]` instead of `null` so the frontend can safely call `.find()` on the response. New test asserts the `[]` response shape. (`internal/server/handler_git.go`, `internal/server/handler_git_test.go`)
- **Advisor Permission-Ask Wiring** — The advisor sub-agent now receives the main agent's `subAgentPermAsker` callback, so out-of-scope tool calls surface a real Y/N prompt to the user instead of returning the inert `SentinelPermissionAsk` string which the advisor model couldn't act on. (`internal/agent/advisor_tool.go`)
- **GitPanel Null Safety** — The `files.find()` call in GitPanel now uses `(files ?? [])` to handle clean repos where the API returns `null` (legacy behavior), preventing a runtime crash when no diff files exist. (`web/src/components/Git/GitPanel.tsx`)
- **Pending-Compact Input Race Window** — Three guard clauses in `handleChatKeys` (line 4956 shell-command path, line 4976 text-input path) and `handleCommand` (line 6854 slash-command path) now check `len(m.pendingCompactUIIdx) > 0` alongside `m.compacting`, preventing commands and chat input from falling through to `askAgent` during the race window between the synchronous `pendingCompactUIIdx` set and the async `compacting` flip via `compactStartedMsg`. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **`SaveAdvisorModel` Targeted Reset** — Clearing the advisor model no longer resets `Enabled` or `Checkpoints` to defaults. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig_test.go`)
- **Git Files-List Render Receiver** — `renderFileList` is now a pointer-receiver method so scroll-state mutations (clamp) take effect; the prior value-receiver copy left list-box scroll/clamp out of sync. (`internal/tui/git_model.go`)
- **TUI Git Files-List Height During Commit** — `handleMouseAction` now accounts for the commit-input rows (`gitModel.commitInput`) when sizing the files list box while committing, matching `Resize()`; clamps to a minimum of 1 row to avoid a zero-height list. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Telegram Cron Error Message** — Removed a duplicated `owner` field from the cron error payload body. (`internal/telegram/bot.go`)
- **Sidebar Scroll-Clamp Header Height** — `clampSidebarScroll` previously assumed a 1-row sidebar header (`lipgloss.Height("◆ ")`), so when a wrapped session title occupied 2-3 rows the scroll viewport math was wrong and could overlap or under-count the header. It now uses `sidebarHeaderHeight()`, which mirrors the wrap performed in `renderSidebar`, keeping layout, the selectable-line map, and the scroll clamp in agreement. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Empty ToolID Filter in `removeIncompleteToolRequests`** — Tool-result messages with an empty `ToolID` are now dropped during session load, preventing them from reaching the provider as `function_call_output` items with `call_id:""`, which the OpenAI Responses API rejects with a 400. (`internal/session/session.go`)

### Added
- **Process-Wide MCP Tool Cache** — New `internal/server/mcpCache` with `warm()`/`wait()` eliminates per-session MCP enumeration by running connect/handshake/ListTools once per process via a background goroutine started at handler construction. Every session creation site (`HandleChat`, `HandleSendMessage`, `getOrCreateAgentSession`, `HandleChatStream`) uses the cached result. (`internal/server/mcp_cache.go`, `internal/server/mcp_cache_test.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`)
- **File Save API Endpoint** — New `PUT /api/files/content` route with `HandleSaveFileContent` handler, path-traversal containment via symlink resolution + `O_NOFOLLOW` write, and test coverage for basic write, missing path, path escape, and `..` escape. (`internal/server/handler_files.go`, `internal/server/handler_files_test.go`, `internal/server/server.go`)
- **Web: Editor Context Chip** — New `EditorContextChip` component rendered above the chat input showing the active file path and selection range. The ref (`@path#Lx-Ly`) is prepended to outgoing messages. Selection tracking via Monaco's `onDidChangeCursorSelection` reports non-collapsed ranges to `SessionPage` state. (`web/src/components/Chat/EditorContextChip.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatInput.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`)
- **Web: Inline Diff Decorations in FileEditor** — Monaco editor decorations highlight modified lines from the session's change diff: green background for additions, yellow for modifications, and collapsed view zones with copy-to-clipboard for deletions. Powered by the new `parseDiffPatch` utility and the existing `api.getChangeDiff` endpoint. (`web/src/components/Files/FileEditor.tsx`, `web/src/lib/parseDiffPatch.ts`, `web/src/index.css`)
- **`TestEnterDuringPendingCompactApplyIsQueued`** — Reproduces the streaming→compacting race window: `pendingCompactUIIdx` is set synchronously when `MaybeCompactAsync` starts a compaction goroutine, but `m.compacting` only flips true later when `compactStartedMsg` is processed by `Update`. Validates that input submitted in that gap is queued as `queueItemCompactInput` instead of falling through to `askAgent`. (`internal/tui/compact_anchored_repro_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.21 → 0.8.27. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.27 → 0.8.29. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Conduct Corpus Expansion (surgical-05, context-01)** — Two new conduct questions added to `docs/okf/conduct/questions.md` and `questions.yaml`: `conduct-surgical-05` tests bulk-edit scope-check discipline (`replace_all` overmatch), and `conduct-context-01` tests consult-the-loaded-reference discipline (repeated CLI syntax errors despite the skill being loaded). Conduct prompt updated from 45→47 total questions. (`docs/okf/conduct/questions.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/questions.yaml`, `docs/okf/_prompts/conduct.md`)
- **DeepSeek V4 Flash Derived Skill Expanded** — The `conduct-tuning-deepseek-v4-flash` SKILL.md (both `docs/okf/conduct/derived/` and `skills/kaizen/`) gains two new sections: "Bulk edits (`replace_all`) need a scope check" and "Consult the loaded reference every call". Description updated to mention both new correctness disciplines. (`docs/okf/conduct/derived/conduct.deepseek-v4-flash.SKILL.md`, `skills/kaizen/conduct-tuning-deepseek-v4-flash/SKILL.md`)
- **HOW-TO-EVALUATE.md SKILL.md Authoring Rules** — Added rules forbidding eval bookkeeping and observed-transcript narration in derived SKILL.md files, clarifying that real observed transcript content belongs in standalone sections, not mixed with tag-gated corrective sections. (`docs/okf/HOW-TO-EVALUATE.md`)
- **DeepSeek V4 Flash Incremental Spot-Check** — Two-question incremental spot-check results (`surgical-05`, `context-01`) added to spot-check log and scorecard. (`docs/okf/conduct/answers/deepseek-v4-flash.spotcheck.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/scores/deepseek-v4-flash.md`)
- **`load_skill` Tool Alias** — New `load_skill` tool registered as an explicit alias for `skill` (models like Claude Code guess `load_skill` instead of `skill`; this prevents the unregistered-tool hallucination guard from rejecting valid skill-loading calls). Registered in all agent/subagent tool lists (`debug`, `docs`, `explore`, `scout`, `context`), added to the auto-allow and read-only permission lists, and wired into `InitBuiltinTools` via the new `SkillAliasTool` type. (`internal/tool/misc.go`, `internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/agent/agent_registry.go`, `internal/agent/registry.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/agent/permissions.go`)
- **`TestAdvisorCheckpointsRoundTrip`** — Tests that the `advisor.checkpoints` config field round-trips through save/load correctly: omitted key → default `["plan","done"]`, explicit `[]` → disabled, explicit list → preserved. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig_test.go`)
- **`TestVersionMatchesChangelog`** — Cross-checks that `version.Version` matches the version bump line in `CHANGES.md` `[Unreleased]` section. (`internal/version/version_test.go`)
- **`TestSetOnAppend_*`** — Tests for `Bus.SetOnAppend`: fires once per append with finalized Seq/TS, nil clears the callback, and concurrent appends preserve seq order. (`internal/notebus/notebus_test.go`)
- **`TestWriteTouches_MultiFileEdit`** — Extends the write-touches test to verify `multi_file_edit` produces a touch for each path in `edits[]`. (`internal/agent/write_touches_test.go`)
- **`TestCheckpointPlanDefersFirstWriteBatch`** / **`TestCheckpointDoneReviewsCompletion`** — Tests for the advisor checkpoint state machine: plan defers the first write batch until advisor approval, done injects a review before the final answer on non-trivial turns. (`internal/agent/advisor_checkpoint_test.go`)
- **`context-efficiency` / `context-accuracy` Kaizen Tags** — Two new conduct tags in `docs/okf/conduct/meta.yaml` classify context-efficiency (waste/speed) and context-accuracy (gap/misuse) behavioral patterns for the Kaizen evaluation framework. (`docs/okf/conduct/meta.yaml`)
- **`TestIsSensitivePath_AllowsEnvTemplates`** — New unit test covering the regression that `.env.example`/`.env.sample`/`.env.template`/`.env.dist` are non-sensitive while `.env`/`.env.local`/`.env.production` remain sensitive. (`internal/agent/permissions_test.go`)
- **Scheduled Jobs / Cron Dispatch** — New `internal/scheduler` package plus host/tool/TUI wiring (see the `### Changed` scheduler entry for the full description and file list). Headline capabilities: `at`/`every`/`cron` schedules, persistent per-project JSON store, headless `agent.Step` dispatch with persistent `cron:<id>` sessions, per-job permission modes, multi-sink delivery (Telegram + live TUI), and a `/cron` slash command + `cron` LLM tool. A `NANOBOT.md` capability-comparison note is added (and now lists scheduled jobs as a covered gap). (`docs/scheduled-jobs.md`, `NANOBOT.md`)
- **DeepSeek System Prompt** — Added `internal/agent/prompts/deepseek.txt` and registered it in `provider_prompts.go` so DeepSeek models receive a tailored system prompt. (`internal/agent/prompts/deepseek.txt`, `internal/agent/provider_prompts.go`, `internal/agent/prompt.go`)
- **Image Generation Cost Estimation** — `ImageGenTool` now estimates and surfaces an image-generation cost. A new `SuccessNoticeSeparator` constant (`internal/tool/tool.go`) lets `Execute()` attach a leading display-only notice (split from the canonical result). `ImageGenTool` computes the estimated cost from a per-provider/model/size/quality price table (`imgPerImagePriceUSD`), appends an `Image generation cost: $…` notice behind the separator, and logs a structured event to `imagegen_costs.jsonl` under the global data dir (via `log`, keeping the TUI alt-screen safe). `Agent.Step` (`internal/agent/agent.go`) splits the notice into `Message.Notice` for the `imagegen` tool so it renders in the transcript but is never sent to the LLM. (`internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/tool/imagegen.go`, `internal/tool/imagegen_test.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **Image Generation (`imagegen` Tool + `/image` Command)** — New `ImageGenTool` generates images via Gemini (default), OpenAI, Novita, DeepInfra, or any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, resolved from provider config + env/api-key. Configured through a new `ImageGen`/`imagegen` block in `ocodeconfig.json` (`ImageGenConfig` with `default_provider`, `default_model`, `timeout`, per-provider overrides) and a `/image [status|enable|disable|model [provider/model]|timeout <seconds>]` slash command. The TUI status struct and web `TUIStatus` type expose `image_gen_enabled`/`image_gen_provider`/`image_gen_model`; the tool is registered in `InitBuiltinTools` with an allow permission default. (`internal/tool/imagegen.go`, `internal/tool/imagegen_test.go`, `internal/config/imagegen_config.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/tui/imagegen_cmd.go`, `internal/tui/imagegen_cmd_test.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/server/tui_status.go`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `skills/ocode-tools/SKILL.md`)
- **Sidebar "✦ gen" Title Button** — A clickable button at the right edge of the sidebar title row regenerates the session title from the latest task; hover underlines it while a drag still selects/copies the title text. A `titleRegenerating` flag tracks the in-flight manual regeneration state. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/title_gen_button_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.19 → 0.8.20. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Agents Tab in TUI** — New dedicated `agents` tab (between chat and files) shows a scrollable card-based list of active and recent agent runs with live status updates, elapsed time, model label, sub-agent summary, token usage, and click-to-open drill-in to the run transcript. The viewport auto-refreshes on the 400ms tick while any run is still running. (`internal/tui/tabs.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/detail_view.go`, `internal/tui/detail_view_test.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **Markdown Rendering in File Preview** — The file explorer preview panel now renders `.md` files with proper headings, bold, links, and tables via `renderMarkdown()` instead of raw chroma-highlighted source; `previewRawLines` tracks ANSI-stripped rendered text so selection, in-file search, and mouse hit-testing stay aligned with what is drawn on screen. (`internal/tui/files_model.go`, `internal/tui/files_search.go`)
- **`stripSliceANSI` Utility** — Batch ANSI-stripping for rendered line arrays, keeping a plain-text mirror of styled output so selection coordinates and search offset computation stay aligned. (`internal/tui/selection.go`)
- **opencode-zen "-free" Tier Suffix Matching** — `modelMatchesTuned` now strips a trailing `-free` suffix (opencode-zen free-tier convention) so `opencode/deepseek-v4-flash-free` matches Kaizen skills tuned for `deepseek-v4-flash`. Only the literal `-free` token is removed, never everything after the last hyphen. (`internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/skill/loader_kaizen_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.18 → 0.8.19. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Novita AI Reasoning Effort + Tencent Hy3 Thinking Support** — Novita AI now accepts the OpenAI-style `reasoning_effort` param (`providerSupportsReasoningEffort` adds `novita-ai`), `ModelSupportsThinking` recognizes `tencent`/`hy3`, and the OpenAI/WebSocket/HTTP request paths gate `reasoning_effort` on `ModelSupportsThinking` for Novita so non-reasoning Novita models never receive a param they would reject. Novita's live model list is also consulted as the authoritative reasoning signal for `novita-ai` models absent from the models.dev registry. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/provider_prompts_test.go`)
- **Kaizen Directive Digest Force-Injection** — A per-model tuning `SKILL.md` may carry a compact `<!-- kaizen:digest -->` block; `skill.KaizenDigestBlock` collects admitted digests and `LoadContext` force-injects them into the base prompt as authoritative rules (unconditional, keyed on model+root for prefix-cache stability, exactly `""` for a non-matching model). Fixes overconfident models that see the advertised skill but never load its body. (`internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/agent/context.go`, `internal/agent/context_test.go`)
- **`/effort` Slash Command** — New `/effort [off|low|med|high]` command shows or sets the reasoning effort (thinking budget) level. (`internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **Local Embedding Default → bge-m3; llama.cpp b9777 + LFM2.5 via llama.cpp** — `DefaultLocalModelID` now returns `local/bge-m3` on all platforms (its wide cosine band clears the attach floor that LFM2.5's compressed band could not). llama.cpp manifests bumped b9747→b9777 (adds the `lfm2-bidir` arch, PR #24913); LFM2.5-Embedding moved from MLX to llama.cpp with LiquidAI's official Q4_K_M GGUF (CLS pooling from GGUF metadata). (`internal/discovery/manifest.go`, `internal/discovery/download.go`)
- **OpenAI Codex "Responses Lite" Support (GPT-5.6 family)** — For OAuth+OpenAI models on the `gpt-5.6` family (sol/terra/luna), the Codex backend serves them only on its "responses lite" path. The requests builder now sends tools and instructions as `developer` items at the head of `input` (with the top-level fields emptied), forces `reasoning.context=all_turns`, sends a `prompt_cache_key` (session id or a process-stable random key) for cache scoping, and sets the `x-openai-internal-codex-responses-lite: true` header. The Codex plugin switches its originator/User-Agent to `codex_cli_rs` for lite models so the backend resolves them. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/plugin/codex/allowlist.go`, `internal/plugin/codex/codex.go`, `internal/plugin/codex/headers_test.go`)
- **Kaizen Skills Delivered Under Discovery** — New `skill.KaizenSkillsForModel(root, activeModel)` returns ONLY the admitted per-model tuned skills; `discoveryDocs()` now appends them to the always-visible names-index so a gated tuning skill is always advertised regardless of embedding rank (the full SKILL.md body still loads on demand via the skill tool), and the discovery fail-open path now uses `BuildCatalogForModel`. (`internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_glue.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_glue_test.go`, `internal/skill/kaizen_deliver_test.go`)
- **Discovery Corpus Warm Single-Flight** — A cold local embedder (MLX/llama.cpp) can take seconds to load, so the background corpus warm is now single-flight (one in-flight at a time) bounded by a 20s timeout; a hot cache stays a microsecond hash-check early-return. (`internal/agent/discovery_glue.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_warm_test.go`)
- **Local Embed Server Reports `ready` on Adopt** — `EnsureLocalServer` now calls `setStatus("ready")` when it adopts a user-provided or shared cross-process embed server. (`internal/discovery/localserver.go`, `internal/discovery/localserver_adopt_test.go`)
- **Skill Command User-Instruction Wrapper** — Slash-command and skill invocations now pass user-supplied arguments inside a `<user instruction>` block instead of concatenating them onto the prompt. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.15 → 0.8.16. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Kaizen Skill Enforcement Hook** — The skill-catalog filter now gates derived ("Kaizen") skills by active model ID and stack detection. `internal/skill/loader.go` parses Kaizen frontmatter (`tuned_for`, `stack`) and admits a Kaizen skill only when the provider-stripped model ID matches AND its stack is detected (or is universal). The default `LoadSkills()`/`BuildCatalog()` exclude all Kaizen skills; `LoadSkill(name)` still resolves them by exact name. Wired into `LoadContext` (now takes `activeModel, root` threaded from `prompt.go` and `tui/model.go`) via `BuildCatalogForModel`. (`internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/skill/loader_kaizen_test.go`, `internal/agent/context.go`, `internal/agent/prompt.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **8 New OKF Evaluation Stacks** — Full evaluation stacks for csharp, dotnet, elixir, nestjs, php, python, ror, ruby, vbnet with questions (`.md` + `.yaml`), meta, answers for tencent/hy3, and scorecards. Elixir includes a with-skill validation set and the first real derived skill (`skills/kaizen/elixir-tuning-tencent-hy3/SKILL.md`). (`docs/okf/{csharp,dotnet,elixir,nestjs,php,python,ror,ruby,vbnet}/`)
- **Conduct With-Skill Validation** — With-skill answers and scores for tencent/hy3 on the conduct corpus, demonstrating the Kaizen improvement loop. (`docs/okf/conduct/answers/tencent__hy3.with-skill.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/scores/tencent__hy3.with-skill.md`)
- **Derived Skill Sync Script** — New `docs/okf/_tools/sync-derived-skills.py` copies derived skills from `docs/okf/*/derived/*.SKILL.md` into `skills/kaizen/<name>/SKILL.md` (idempotent, prunes stale dirs). (`docs/okf/_tools/sync-derived-skills.py`)
- **Two Embedded Kaizen Skills** — Derived skills for conduct-tuning and elixir-tuning for tencent/hy3, embedded in `skills/kaizen/` under the `//go:embed all:skills` tree. (`skills/kaizen/conduct-tuning-tencent-hy3/SKILL.md`, `skills/kaizen/elixir-tuning-tencent-hy3/SKILL.md`)
- **stackdetect Expansion** — New stack detection entries for elixir, csharp, dotnet, vbnet, nestjs, php, python, ruby, and ror. (`internal/stackdetect/stackdetect.go`, `internal/stackdetect/stackdetect_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.13 → 0.8.15. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Dialog Height Restore Regression Test** — New test for TUI dialog height restoration on short terminals. (`internal/tui/dialog_height_restore_repro_test.go`)
- **Git Diff Double-Click Jumps to Source Line** — Double-clicking a line in the git panel's diff pane now opens the file in the configured editor at the corresponding source line. The diff gutter now renders the actual source line number (not the viewport row) for added/context lines, computed by parsing `@@` hunk headers; removed lines map to the next surviving line and headers/notices show a blank gutter. A new `editorOpenerAtLine` path (external + tmux modes) reuses `editorArgsWithLine` so VS Code (`-g file:line`), Vim/Neovim (`+line`), Helix (`file:line`), and nano/emacs (`+line`) all jump correctly. Single-click + drag still selects diff text; double-click only fires for the single-file `Changes` section (commit/stash full-diffs span multiple files and fall back to selection). (`internal/tui/git_model.go`, `internal/tui/editor_mode.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/git_diff_linemap_test.go`)
- **Telegram Bot Companion (`ocode-telegram`)** — New `cmd/ocode-telegram` binary and `internal/telegram` package (bot, client, SSE) let you drive any running ocode instance with `/rc` enabled straight from Telegram. The bot discovers instances via the new `internal/rc` registry (per-instance heartbeat JSON files under the global data dir) and relays messages to the selected instance's web/RC server, streaming the agent's reply back. No public ingress required — only Telegram is external. (`cmd/ocode-telegram/main.go`, `internal/telegram/bot.go`, `internal/telegram/client.go`, `internal/telegram/sse.go`, `docs/telegram-bot.md`)
- **Grok (xAI) Subscription Provider** — New `grok` provider (env `XAI_API_KEY`, base URL `api.x.ai/v1`) authenticates against grok.com's OpenAI-compatible backend using an x.com cookie-based SSO bearer token. `internal/auth/grok_subscription.go` exchanges the `auth_token`+`ct0` cookies for a short-lived SSO token and automatically refreshes it; `internal/plugin/grok` supplies request params/headers (originator, User-Agent, session id). The `/connect` dialog gains a Grok cookie-collection stage, and the `Credential` struct carries `CookieAuthToken`/`CookieCt0`. (`internal/auth/grok_subscription.go`, `internal/auth/plugin_hooks.go`, `internal/plugin/grok/grok.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/auth/providers.go`, `internal/auth/store.go`, `internal/tui/connect.go`)
- **Remote Control (`/rc`) Expansion & Server Bridge** — `/rc` instances now publish a discovery entry (heartbeat + TTL pruning) via the `internal/rc` registry; the web server gains an RC bridge (`rc_bridge.go`) plus `handler_rc_resolve.go` so external clients (web UI, Telegram bot) can resolve permission prompts and answer multi-select questions on behalf of a bridged TUI session. `allAgents()` includes the RC agent so permission/YOLO toggles and question answers reach `/rc` instances. New shared wire types `QuestionAnswer`/`QuestionAnswerSet` preserve every selected option across surfaces. (`internal/rc/registry.go`, `internal/server/rc_bridge.go`, `internal/server/handler_rc_resolve.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions.go`, `internal/server/handler_questions.go`, `internal/tool/misc.go`)
- **Non-Blocking Model Registry Lookups** — `AllProviderModelsCached()` consults the embedded snapshot plus already-populated live caches, never blocking on the network inside HTTP handlers or the TUI main loop; LM Studio/Requesty/Novita live fetches are gated to the async refresh path with registry-snapshot fallback. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`)
- **Model-Aware Skill Slash Commands** — `refreshCustomCommands` is now scoped to `workDir` + active model so Kaizen (`tuned_for`) skills are admitted only when the active model matches; switching model or workDir refreshes the slash popup. `modelMatchesTuned` strips OpenRouter-style `:free`/`:nitro` variant suffixes for correct Kaizen matching. (`internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/skill/loader.go`)
- **File-Edit Undo Hint** — Write/Edit/ReplaceLines/Delete/MultiEdit/MultiFileEdit tool results now append an `undo_file_change` reminder with the tool call ID so the agent can revert an edit in one step. (`internal/tool/file.go`)
- **Patch Tool Result Rendering** — New `internal/tui/tool_render_patch.go` renders patch-style tool outputs in the TUI transcript. (`internal/tui/tool_render_patch.go`, `internal/tui/tool_render.go`)
- **Conduct Corpus Expansion (surgical-06, surgical-07)** — Two new conduct questions added to `docs/okf/conduct/questions.md` and `questions.yaml`: `conduct-surgical-06` tests naming-convention adherence (grep+match analog, no unbacked new convention), and `conduct-surgical-07` tests comment-WHY discipline (default to no comments; only for non-obvious WHY, not restating WHAT). Conduct prompt updated from 47→49 total questions. (`docs/okf/conduct/questions.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/questions.yaml`, `docs/okf/_prompts/conduct.md`)
- **DeepSeek V4 Flash Incremental Spot-Check (surgical-06, surgical-07)** — Two-question incremental spot-check results for the new surgical questions added to spot-check log. Baseline (no digest): 4.0/4; with digest (production build): 3.0/4 (surgical-06 control wobble -1.0, surgical-07 stable at 2.0). Analysis concludes both are control wobbles within noise tolerance, not a directive to extend the digest. (`docs/okf/conduct/answers/deepseek-v4-flash.spotcheck.md`)

### Fixed
- **TUI: Changes Tab Files Not Populated After Connect** — `changesModel.Update` had a `handleKey` path that called `refreshFiles()` only in `View()` (which has a value receiver, so the refreshed copy was discarded after rendering). The model `Update()` persists between messages, so `handleKey` now also calls `m = m.refreshFiles()` to ensure the file list survives across frames. (`internal/tui/changes_model.go`)
- **TUI: Agent Rebuild Leaves Changes Tab Registry Dead** — `rebuildAgentClient()` creates a new agent but did not rewire the changes tab's registry accessor; after reconnect the Changes tab continued reading the old (or nil) agent's registry. `rebuildAgentClient()` now calls `m.changes = m.changes.withRegistry(m.agent.Changes)`. (`internal/tui/connect.go`)
- **TUI: Stale Activity Indicators After Escape** — When the user presses Escape to cancel streaming, the activity row continued showing stale "working" indicators (⟳ LLM, active tools, running agents) for 1–2 frames until `streamDoneMsg` landed. `handleEscKey` now clears `m.lastActivity` immediately after `CancelAll()`. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **TUI: Missing Status Icon for Cancelled Agents** — `detail_view.go::statusIcon` returned empty string for `RunCancelled` state instead of a distinct icon. Now renders `⊘` with the hint style. (`internal/tui/detail_view.go`)
- **TUI Composer Arrow-Key Navigation Restored** — `newComposerInput` was unbinding the textarea's `CharacterBackward`/`LinePrevious` with an empty `key.NewBinding()` (which matches nothing), silently killing left/up arrow navigation in the chat input. They are now rebound to keep the arrow key while dropping only the `ctrl+b`/`ctrl+p` chord, so the global background-bash / file-search shortcuts still fire and the arrows still move the cursor. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **LSP `Close()` No Longer Hangs Forever** — `Client.Close()` blocked on `<-c.exited` with no timeout; a child process stuck in uninterruptible I/O ignores SIGKILL until the kernel unblocks it, which would hang `Close()` (and every synchronous caller) forever. The wait is now bounded by a 3s `select` that returns an error instead of blocking indefinitely. (`internal/lsp/client.go`)
- **TUI Quit No Longer Blocks on Cleanup** — Several quit paths (`cleanupRequestMsg`, the modal `q` handler, `exitButtonForClick`, and the `ctrl+c` handlers in `handleChatKeys`/`handleTabCtrlC`) called `m.cleanupCurrentSession()` directly. Because the update handler runs on the same goroutine that reads SIGINT, a cleanup step blocked on I/O (e.g. a stuck LSP child) would hang the very signal meant to quit. Cleanup now runs post-`Run()` via `tui.go`'s deferred `cleanupProgramModel`, off the update goroutine; `runExitCmd` likewise returns `tea.Quit` without calling cleanup. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`)
- **`libDirForBinary` Avoids Cross-Version ABI Mismatch** — After the b9747→b9777 bump `binDir` can hold multiple version dirs; the spawn library path is now the launched binary's own parent dir instead of a directory scan that could pair the new binary with old `.dylib`/`.so` files. (`internal/discovery/localserver.go`)
- **Discovery Cache Busts on Embedding-Pipeline Format Change** — `Cache` now stores a `version` field; a load whose format version differs (bumped to v2 for the MLX→llama.cpp backend swap and bge-m3 GGUF) is discarded and re-embedded rather than reusing incompatible vectors under the same model id + dim. (`internal/discovery/cache.go`, `internal/discovery/cache_test.go`)
- **TUI: Mouse Text Selection Uses Content-Relative Columns** — Text selection in the transcript, input area, and status bar used the absolute mouse X column instead of the padded content column, so the highlighted/copied range started at the panel border and drifted off the real text. New `transcriptContentLeftX` / `inputContentLeftX` / `statusContentLeftX` constants convert mouse coordinates into content-relative columns for `selectionState`, mouse-motion drag updates, and hover link probing; click handlers now also guard that the press lands inside the content region (not the border/padding). (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **TUI: Permission Dialog No Longer Corrupts the Chat Height on Short Terminals** — The permission dialog body cap (`permDialogMaxBodyLines`, max of a fixed floor and 40% of terminal height) ignored the rest of the bottom chrome, so on short terminals (or with a long command / the always-allow confirm step) the total frame exceeded the terminal height; every over-tall frame scrolled the real terminal one row, and the corruption survived accept/deny, compounded with each popup, and was only repaired by a window resize — perceived as "the chat tab keeps shrinking after every permission request". `syncPermViewport` now rebudgets the dialog body so the chrome plus at least one transcript row always fits, and `View()` gained a last-resort hard clamp that truncates any frame taller than the terminal (over-tall frames are what scroll and permanently corrupt the layout). The slash-command popup had the same class of bug: long command descriptions wrapped inside its border (8 items could render 20+ rows) while the popup opens without a `layout()` pass; item rows are now truncated to the popup width. Regression tests: `TestPermDialogNeverExceedsShortTerminal`, `TestRenderContentNeverTallerThanTerminal`. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/slash_popup.go`, `internal/tui/perm_height_short_terminal_repro_test.go`)
- **OcodeConfig Concurrent-Write Race Fixed** — `SaveOcodePermissions` (and other setters) previously did a bare load-modify-write that could silently drop another session's concurrent change; they now run under a cross-process advisory lock (`lockOcodeConfig`/`withOcodeConfigLock`) with stale-lock stealing after 10s. `RefreshLocked` serializes per-provider credential refreshes so concurrent goroutines reuse a shared fresh result. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/auth/providers.go`)

### Added
- **Scheduler Update HTTP Test** — New `internal/server/scheduler_update_http_test.go` covering the `PATCH /api/cron/{id}` endpoint: enable/disable, edit name/payload/schedule, invalid JSON rejection, not-found handling, and schedule validation. (`internal/server/scheduler_update_http_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.29 → 0.8.30. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **OKF Conduct Corpus Added (Universal, 39 Q&A)** — New `docs/okf/conduct/` corpus (universal, not language-specific) with `questions.md`/`questions.yaml` (39 Q&A) and `meta.yaml`; per-tag threshold raised to n>=4 per tag (39 → 45 Q&A). `TODO.md` records universal-corpus handling in the enforcement hook. (`docs/okf/conduct/meta.yaml`, `docs/okf/conduct/questions.md`, `docs/okf/conduct/questions.yaml`, `docs/okf/README.md`, `TODO.md`)
- **OKF golang Scorecard Added** — New `docs/okf/golang/scores/novita-ai__tencent__hy3.md` scores `novita-ai/tencent/hy3` on the golang corpus: 100% weighted stack score, all tags at/above the 0.75 threshold, so no corrective skill was derived. (`docs/okf/golang/scores/novita-ai__tencent__hy3.md`)
- **TUI: Ctrl/Cmd-Click URL Opens Browser Directly** — Plain clicks on a transcript URL still open the confirm-before-launch dialog (per clickable-URL safety rules), but Ctrl-click (or Cmd-click on macOS) now bypasses the confirmation and launches the browser immediately, returning the open command on mouse release. New `TestURLModClickOpensDirectly` covers both modifiers. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/url_link_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.12 → 0.8.13. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Orchestrator Pipeline Hardening: "Distrust the Executor"** — The orchestrator pipeline now centers on independent verification: Step 0 captures a git baseline before any work; the planner's success criteria must be directly traceable to the original goal; the developer agent is forbidden from shipping silent stubs (`panic("not implemented")`, `// TODO`, empty bodies) and must be radically honest in "What Was NOT Done"; the validator adversarialy reads actual files and git diffs (not the developer's report), detects stubs/partial implementations, and checks goal alignment (scope drift or silent de-scoping is a failure). The orchestrator itself runs an executor-verification gate between develop and validate: it compares real git status against the developer's claimed file list (trust failure on mismatch), greps for stubs, runs compile+vet, and optionally runs targeted tests before calling the validator. Validation alone is no longer sufficient — a Goal Alignment Gate (Step 5) follows, re-reading the ORIGINAL goal verbatim and requiring every substantive part to be addressed. (`internal/agent/orchestrator.md`, `internal/agent/orchestrator-validator.md`, `internal/agent/orchestrator-developer.md`, `internal/agent/orchestrator-planner.md`)
- **Desktop Embedded Assets Bundling** — New `bundle-desktop-assets` Makefile target copies bundled skills/plugins/model-configs next to the desktop main package at build time; `Makefile desktop` and `install-desktop` depend on it. The produced content is gitignored; a bare `go build ./cmd/ocode-desktop` still compiles against a committed `.gitkeep`. The desktop entry point (`main.go`) now embeds the assets via `go:embed`, registers them with `skill.SetBundledFS`, `bundled.SetEmbeddedSkills/SetEmbeddedPlugins`, and `agent.SetBundledModelConfigFS`, and calls `bundled.EnsureExtracted()` so embedded skills/plugins are available even without a disk copy. (`Makefile`, `.gitignore`, `cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`, `internal/bundled/`)
- **MLX Embedding Server Integration** — New `internal/discovery/mlx_server_embed.go` and `mlx_server_embed_test.go` implement local embedding via Apple MLX (`.gguf` models). `EnsureLocalServer` is now model-aware: it probes for the requested `modelID` at each adoption point (user base URL, cross-process port, in-process slot) and rejects a server that serves the wrong model. New `ManifestForModel(modelID)` and `ExpectedServeID()` return the manifest for a specific model and its expected model ID, supporting multiple embedding backends (llamacpp, mlx). (`internal/discovery/localserver.go`, `internal/discovery/manifest.go`, `internal/discovery/mlx_server_embed.go`, `internal/discovery/mlx_server_embed_test.go`, `internal/discovery/manifest_test.go`)
- **Bundled Plugin Agents Load Order** — `agent_loader.go` now loads embedded (bundled) plugin agents first, so disk-based global/project agents override them by name via last-wins semantics. The load loop no longer skips plugin agents when `enabled` is nil, so the normal startup path loads them unfiltered. New `internal/bundled/` package provides embedded skill/plugin/model-config extraction. (`internal/agent/agent_loader.go`, `internal/agent/agent_loader_test.go`, `internal/plugins/loader.go`, `internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/bundled/`)
- **Web Frontend: CoworkSidebar Rework** — Major rework of the CoworkSidebar component (588-line diff) with expanded functionality. (`web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`)
- **Web Frontend: ChatPanel Enhancements** — Significant ChatPanel rework (209-line diff) with improvements to streaming, rendering, and user interaction. (`web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`)
- **Web Frontend: New Components** — Added ChatSearchBar, QuestionDialog, and PluginsPanel components. (`web/src/components/Chat/ChatSearchBar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/QuestionDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/PluginsPanel.tsx`)
- **Web Frontend: commands.ts Expansion** — Major expansion of slash command definitions (508-line diff) with new commands and improved auto-complete. (`web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts`)
- **Server Question & Permission Bridge** — New `POST /api/questions` endpoint for headless question answering; `POST /api/permissions/resolve` endpoint for permission resolution (approve/deny) with SSE `permission`/`permission_resolved` events. Both work in headless serve mode; `/rc` bridge mode returns 409. (`internal/server/handler_questions.go`, `internal/server/handler_questions_test.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve.go`, `internal/server/handler_permissions_resolve_test.go`, `internal/server/handler.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/hooks/useChat.ts`)
- **Server Refactoring** — Handler and server layer refactored for maintainability and new endpoint support. (`internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/handler_theme.go`, `internal/server/tui_status.go`)
- **`/goal` Slash Command** — Added `/goal` to the instant commands list in AGENTS.md. (`AGENTS.md`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.11 → 0.8.12. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Project-Scoped File-Edit Snapshots** — Backup files now live in the global, project-scoped data dir (`GlobalDataDir()/project/{slug}/snapshots`) instead of the repo-relative `.opencode/snapshots`, so `/cd` to another project never lands backups in the wrong place and cross-project state stays isolated. `Agent.SetWorkDir` re-points the snapshot store and clears markdown-discovery state on `/cd`; `snapshot.NewStore(agentID, baseDir)` takes a base dir and `SetBaseDir` re-points it. Each snapshot records its `BaseDir` provenance so undo/redo stays pinned to the owning project, and the backup filename includes the agent ID to avoid collisions. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/snapshot/snapshot.go`)
- **Project Slug/Roots Moved to `paths`** — `ProjectRoot`, `ProjectSlug`, and the memoized `gitToplevel` helper moved from `internal/session` into `internal/paths` so callers that cannot import `session` (import cycle) can still compute the per-project slug; `session` now delegates to `paths`. (`internal/paths/paths.go`, `internal/session/session.go`)
- **Markdown Discovery Excludes Active OKF Bundle Docs** — `walkMarkdownFiles` skips files inside an active knowledge bundle's `docs/` directory (already owned by the knowledge system) to avoid a redundant "path — summary" TOC and wasted summarization calls; the markdown summary cache moved to the project-scoped global `md-summaries.json`. `AGENTS.md` documents the exclusion. (`internal/agent/md_discovery.go`, `AGENTS.md`)
- **Novita Live Model Preloading** — New `PreloadNovitaModels` / `NovitaModelsLoaded` populate Novita's live model cache in the background so the sidebar's "context used / max context" readout reflects real Novita context sizes without waiting on first use. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`)
