- **OpenRouter Live Model Resolution** — New `PreloadOpenRouterModels` / `OpenRouterModelsLoaded` (plus `fetchOpenRouterLiveModels` / `openRouterLiveModelEntry`) populate a live OpenRouter model cache from the public OpenRouter API so `ModelWindow` / `ModelCost` / `ModelSupportsVision` resolve metadata for `openrouter/...` models that models.dev omits or names differently (e.g. `openrouter/tencent/hy3:free`, which models.dev lists as `tencent/hy3-free` or not at all). The cache is consulted in `modelEntryFor` for both `openrouter/`-prefixed and bare ids, and supplements the model picker in `allProviderModelsFromRegistry` / `providerModelsFromRegistry`. The TUI preloads it at startup and re-renders on `openRouterReadyMsg` when the active model is OpenRouter. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **OpenRouter Picker Fetch No Longer Blocks the TUI** — `AllProviderModels` / `ProviderModels` keep refreshing live sources over the network (used by the async model-picker loader and the server), but the synchronous main-loop callers (model-picker refresh on favorite toggle / cache refresh, and `/models` slash autocomplete) now go through `AllProviderModelsCached`, which only reads an already-fresh live cache (`openRouterCacheFresh`) and never blocks on a network re-fetch. Without this, a cache miss after the 30s TTL froze the TUI for up to 15s because OpenRouter has no API-key gate (unlike Novita). Regression-tested by `TestAllProviderModelsCachedSkipsStaleOpenRouter`. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/tui/picker.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`)
- **OCR Timeouts Raised to 600s** — Local vision backends (`openaiCompat`, `paddle`) now allow up to 10 minutes per request to cover cold model load plus large-image inference. (`internal/ocr/openai_compat.go`, `internal/ocr/paddle.go`)
- **TUI URL Links & Listener Hardening** — Chat transcript URLs become clickable `buildURLLinkRegions` regions that confirm before launching `openBrowser`; sub-agent permission and activity listeners are now context-cancellable (`listenSubAgentPerm`, `armSubAgentPermListener`, `listenActivity`, `armActivityListener`) instead of blocking forever on cancelled agents; `silenceCmdOutput` captures subprocess stderr; path links expand `~`; and `waitForNovitaReady` gates the model picker on the Novita cache. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/files_model.go`, `internal/tui/pathlink.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.8 → 0.8.11. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Sub-Agent Goroutine Leak Fix** — New `Agent.shutdownTransient()` tears down the per-agent memory + doc maintenance-worker goroutines for both synchronous and background sub-agent dispatches, preventing one leaked Agent + goroutine pair per `knowledge_lookup` / `task` call. `memoryMaintShutdown` now closes the channel and waits (bounded) for the worker to exit; the background `task` path calls it via `defer`. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/agent/memory_maintenance.go`)
- **Compact Token-Aware Tail Retention** — New `keep_recent_tokens` compact config (`KeepRecentTokens`) bounds the retained "recent" suffix by token estimate instead of message count, with CJK-aware token counting (`charsPerTokenFor`, `splitCJK`, `isCJK`) and a new `findTokenTail` cutoff finder. (`internal/agent/compact.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Knowledge Sub-Agent Discovery Skip** — The `context` knowledge sub-agent sets `skipDiscovery`, bypassing the repo-wide markdown summarization pass and embedder warm-up to cut `knowledge_lookup` latency. (`internal/agent/discovery_glue.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **MCP Tool Loading Refactor** — `LoadExternalTools` renamed to `LoadExternalToolsWithMCP`; new `LoadMCPTools`, `AddMCPTools`, `AddMCPErrors` helpers split MCP loading from external-tool wiring. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/cli/goal.go`, `internal/runcli/run.go`)
- **doc_search Inline Bodies** — `DocSearchTool` accepts `get_top` to return the full body of the top matches inline (backward-compatible default 0); the `context` sub-agent prompt now issues a single `doc_search` with `get_top: 3` to avoid a separate `doc_get` round-trip. (`internal/agent/doc_tools.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`, `docs/knowledge-bundle.md`)
- **AgentRun.ModelLabel Safety** — Returns "" when the sub-agent client is nil (still being initialized), avoiding a nil deref. (`internal/agent/agent_runs.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.6 → 0.8.7. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Agent Coding-Standard Guardrail: Production `.env` Files** — `AGENTS.md` now forbids blindly overwriting production or remote `.env` files (`.env`, `.env.production`, `.env.local`, and environment-specific variants) unless the user explicitly requests it, to avoid leaking credentials or breaking deployments. (`AGENTS.md`)
- **Skill-as-Command Dispatch** — The TUI now treats `/<skill-name>` as a command that activates any installed skill: `/help` lists available skills, and entering a skill name loads its SKILL.md content as a skill-run prompt (extra words become context). Resolution uses `m.workDir` so skills installed under a `/cd` target are matched. New `LoadSkillsForRoot`, `InvalidateSkillCache`, a TTL-bounded skill-load cache in `internal/skill/loader.go`, and a case-insensitive `findSkillByName` matcher; the dispatch branch lives in `handleCommand`. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/skill/loader.go`, `internal/tui/command_test.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **Streaming `!` Shell Output** — `!` shell commands now stream combined stdout/stderr into the transcript live via a new `shellChunkMsg` and `runStreamingShell`; an in-flight `shellStreamCmd` field queues concurrent `!` commands while one is active instead of running them in parallel. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Live LLM `bash` Tool Streaming** — When the LLM invokes the `bash` tool, its combined stdout/stderr now streams into the transcript live (matching the `!` shell experience) instead of appearing only when the command finishes. New optional `tool.StreamingTool` interface (`ExecuteStream(args, emit)`); `BashTool` implements it and pumps pipe bytes to an `emit` callback, while `Agent` gains an `OnToolOutput(toolCallID, chunk)` callback plumbed through the existing `deltaCh` stream pipeline. The TUI renders chunks via `appendShellOutput` and replaces the provisional content with the canonical (redacted/truncated) result on completion; foreground and background `run_in_background`/move-to-background paths are preserved. (`internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Fix: Streamed `bash` Result Preserved at Any Size** — Large streamed tool output was being clobbered on completion: the live chunks (full output) were replaced by the LLM-side truncated copy (`TruncateToolResult`, cap 12000), so the chunked result appeared "not applied" once output exceeded that size, and fast/large commands dropped live chunks on `deltaCh` backpressure. Fix: the agent now carries the full text in a new `Message.DisplayContent` (used for transcript/UI display) while `Content` stays truncated for the LLM prompt; the finalize handler prefers `DisplayContent`; the bash tool skips its 30000 hard cap when streaming; and `OnToolOutput` now blocks (backpressuring the bash pipe) instead of dropping chunks. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tool/exec.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Bundled Skill Library Expansion** — Added ~58 new skills covering Google Workspace (`gws-*`), PDF tooling (`pdf`), context compression (`compress`, `caveman*`), browser automation (`agent-browser`, `htrcli`), web QA/testing (`webapp-qa`, `webapp-testing`), framework migration (`nextjs-to-tanstack`), and skill authoring (`skill-creator`). (`skills/`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.5 → 0.8.6. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **Novita AI Provider** — New `novita-ai` LLM provider with live model listing via the v3 API, automatic pricing conversion (from Novita's 1/10000th-of-$ per M tokens to USD per M tokens), context size and modality detection, and reasoning/function-calling feature flags. Live-fetched models supplement the static `models.dev` registry with a 30-second TTL cache and graceful fallback to snapshot data. Provider authentication registered via `NOVITA_API_KEY` env var or stored credentials. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/auth/providers.go`, `internal/agent/novita_test.go`)
- **Knowledge Bundle Search Word-Level AND Matching** — `Store.Search()` now tokenizes multi-word queries into individual tokens and requires ALL tokens to appear somewhere in the document (title, description, or body). Per-field scoring uses cumulative token counts (title weight 3, description weight 2, body weight 1). Single-token queries retain the fast-path substring check. Updated `doc_search` tool description and `docs/knowledge-bundle.md`. (`internal/knowledge/store.go`, `internal/agent/doc_tools.go`, `docs/knowledge-bundle.md`)
- **Agent.ChangedFiles() Method** — New public method exposing the agent's snapshot store changed files, enabling the TUI sidebar and other consumers to see file changes made by sub-agents that share the parent's snapshot store. (`internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.2 → 0.8.5. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **`/context` Command Knowledge Bundle & Memory Sections** — Added "Knowledge Bundle" and "Memory" sections to `/context` output, showing active/inactive/disabled status for OKF docs and layered memory with file paths and token estimates. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **`/context` Command Discovery Section Restructure** — Restructured Discovery section with corpus index breakdown (Skills in index, MCP tools in index, Project docs in index, Docs pending summarization) separated from volatile tail (attached skills/MCP/docs). Added efficiency metrics (gross/net context saved). Renamed "Subtotal" to "Base subtotal". Tools subtitle varies by discovery state. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **`/context` Command Test Coverage** — New `TestContextCommandDisabledStates`, `TestContextCommandKnowledgeBundleInactive`, `TestContextCommandMemoryEnabled`. Updated existing tests for new section labels and corpus info. (`internal/tui/command_test.go`)
- **Knowledge Bundle Architecture Documentation** — New `docs/knowledge-bundle.md` documenting the OKF v0.1 knowledge bundle system: bundle detection, scanning, frontmatter parsing, doc search, .okfignore exclusion, and store CRUD with edge cases. (`docs/knowledge-bundle.md`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.8.1 → 0.8.2. (`internal/version/version.go`)
- **OCR LM Studio Model Resolution** — `/ocr model` now accepts and round-trips `lmstudio/<model>` selections, with the OCR picker labeling LM Studio-backed models accordingly and the OCR executor stripping the provider prefix before calling the OpenAI-compatible endpoint. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/ocr/openai_compat.go`, `internal/ocr/backend.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **LSP Language Coverage Expansion** — Extended `serversByExt` from 4 languages (Go, Rust, Python, TypeScript/JS) to 20+: Dart/Flutter (`dart language-server`), PHP (`intelephense`), Java (`jdtls`), C# (`csharp-ls`), Ruby (`solargraph`), C/C++ (`clangd`), Lua (`lua-language-server`), Kotlin (`kotlin-language-server`), Swift (`sourcekit-lsp`), Scala (`metals`), Elixir (`elixir-ls`), Zig (`zls`), Haskell (`haskell-language-server-wrapper`), OCaml (`ocamllsp`), Terraform (`terraform-ls`), YAML (`yaml-language-server`), JSON (`vscode-json-language-server`), and Bash (`bash-language-server`). Each has a matching `installHints` entry surfaced when the binary is missing from PATH. All new entries are best-effort stdio invocations (only gopls is validated end-to-end — see TODO.md). (`internal/lsp/manager.go`, `TODO.md`)
- **Image/Vision Support in Agent Tool Results** — Agent now embeds images from `read` tool calls into vision-capable model messages via the new `handleToolCallWithImages` pipeline. New `ImageResultTool` interface on `ReadTool` (`ExecuteImage`) provides raw pixel bytes. `NewImageFromBytes` encodes them with optional downscaling (`MaxImageDim` config, default 2048). `ModelSupportsVision()` maps provider/models. OpenAI client injects pending image blocks as a synthetic `user` message after tool-result runs; Anthropic client renders image blocks natively. Non-vision models receive the existing textual description stub unchanged. Locked in by `read_image_test.go`. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/images.go`, `internal/agent/models_registry.go`, `internal/agent/read_image_test.go`, `internal/tool/file.go`, `internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **OCR Tool** — New built-in `ocr` tool that runs Tesseract (via `go-tesseract` or the `tesseract` binary) to extract text from image files. Returns structured text with confidence estimates. Gated by `tool.ocr` permission in config. (`internal/tool/ocr.go`)
- **Monaco Editor Integration (Web UI)** — In-browser Monaco-based file editor with syntax highlighting, multiple language support, and a dedicated settings panel. New `FileEditor` component, `MonacoSettingsPanel` for editor preferences, `web/src/lib/monaco-setup.ts` for language registration and theme setup. Server-side handler at `handler_monaco.go` for file read/write proxying. (`web/src/components/Files/FileEditor.tsx`, `web/src/components/Files/MonacoSettingsPanel.tsx`, `web/src/lib/monaco-setup.ts`, `internal/server/handler_monaco.go`, `internal/monaco/`)
- **Web UI Slash Commands Centralization** — All 13 slash commands now defined in a single shared `web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts` with `COMMANDS` array, async `dispatchCommand()` dispatcher, and `CommandResult` effects (messages, session switch, download). ChatInput, SlashCommandMenu, and CommandPalette all import from the shared list. Backend API endpoints added for `/btw`, config mask toggle, `/compact`, `/recap`, and `/share` commands. (`web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts`, `web/src/components/Chat/ChatInput.tsx`, `web/src/components/Chat/SlashCommandMenu.tsx`, `web/src/components/common/CommandPalette.tsx`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`)
- **Server API Expansion** — New REST endpoints for config management (`GET/PUT /api/config/mask*`), project operations (`GET/POST /api/projects`, `DELETE /api/projects/:id`), Monaco editor file handling, enhanced SSE event emission, and run-states polling for desktop integration. (`internal/server/handler.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/server/handler_projects.go`, `internal/server/handler_monaco.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse.go`, `internal/server/handler_sse_test.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/server/tui_status.go`, `internal/server/handler_files.go`)
- **Desktop App DevTools Menu Item** — New "Open DevTools" entry in the system tray (and View menu) for debugging the frontend in non-production builds. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/main.go`)
- **Web UI CoworkSidebar & Layout Improvements** — Enhanced parallel agent monitoring sidebar with agent state indicators, run grouping, and collapse/expand. ProjectSidebar improved with search and reorder. TopTabs refined with active-state indicators and overflow handling. FileTree refactored for performance and maintainability. SessionPage gains project-scoped session routing. (`web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/ProjectSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/TopTabs.tsx`, `web/src/components/Files/FileTree.tsx`, `web/src/pages/SessionPage.tsx`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`)
- **Recap Model Simplification** — `recapClient()` now uses the configured recap model whenever it is set, removing the `RecapModelEnabled` gate. The `enabled` flag now only controls whether auto-recap runs after each response; manual `/recap` and recap model usage always use the configured model. (`internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **URL Link Display Improvements** — Enhanced `urllink.go` with better URL detection patterns, protocol-relative URL support, and improved rendering of inline URLs in the TUI transcript. New test coverage for edge cases. (`internal/tui/urllink.go`, `internal/tui/urllink_test.go`)
- **Orchestrator Pipeline Enhancements** — Improved compile-failure reporting in HALT state, updated pipeline test cases for coverage of edge conditions. (`internal/orchestrator/pipeline.go`, `internal/orchestrator/pipeline_test.go`)
- **Projects Package Updates** — `ListForDir()` helper for path-based storage directory resolution, enabling project-scoped session listing from the API. (`internal/projects/projects.go`)
- **Extra Allowed Paths Update** — Added `~/.claude` to project-scoped extra allowed paths. (`.ocode/settings.json`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.7.0 → 0.7.2.
- **Multi-Backend OCR System** — Extracted OCR into a pluggable multi-backend architecture with `openai-compat` (LM Studio, vLLM) and `paddle` (PaddleOCR) backends. New `internal/ocr` package with `Backend` interface, `OcrConfig` (backend type, per-backend settings), and backend registry via `Get()`/`List()`. Config migrated from flat `ocr_model`/`ocr_enabled` fields to a structured `ocr` object with automatic legacy field migration. New `SaveOcrConfig()` API. OCR tool (`internal/tool/ocr.go`) delegates to the configured backend, removing inline HTTP logic. TUI OCR model picker fetches models from all registered backends and supports `backend/model` syntax. Web UI updated with `OcrConfig`/`OcrModelsResponse` types, new `/api/config/ocr` and `/api/ocr/models` REST endpoints, and backend-aware `/ocr` command. (`internal/ocr/`, `internal/tool/ocr.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/server/handler_config.go`, `internal/server/server.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/picker.go`, `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/components/Chat/commands.ts`, `web/src/components/Layout/CoworkSidebar.tsx`, `web/src/stores/chatStore.tsx`)
- **Vision-Model Safety for Text-Only Providers** — Non-vision models (e.g. DeepSeek) now receive a descriptive text stub instead of a base64 `image_url` block when an image is attached, preventing API 400 errors from providers that reject the `image_url` variant. Mirrors the read tool's text-only degradation pattern. Applies to both `chat/completions` and `responses` API paths. Unlocked by `TestConvertToOpenAIMessages_NonVisionUserImageStubbed`. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/read_image_test.go`)
- **Enhanced Vision Model Name Detection** — `ocrKeywordMatch()` expanded with broader model name keywords (moondream, florence, cogvlm, pixtral, paligemma, minicpm, internvl, llava, clip, phi, gemma, qwen) and `vl`/`vlm` pattern suffix matching for better OCR model discovery. (`internal/agent/models_registry.go`)
- **SSE Broadcast After Compaction** — The `/compact` API endpoint now broadcasts the compacted message snapshot via SSE, so connected web UI mirrors immediately reflect the reduced context instead of showing stale pre-compaction messages. (`internal/server/handler.go`)
- **Gemini Interactions API Support** — Native Google provider (`chatGoogle`) speaks the Gemini Interactions API (`v1beta/interactions`) with full SSE streaming (`interaction.created`, `step.start`, `step.delta`, `step.stop`, `interaction.completed` event parsing). Thought signatures round-tripped via new `Message.Signature` and `ToolCall.Signature` fields. Tool calling uses flat function format, thinking budget maps to `thinking_level` in generation_config. Provider routing dispatches `google`/`google-vertex` to the new handler. (`internal/agent/client.go`, `internal/agent/google_toolcall_repro_test.go`)
- **OCR LM Studio Native API Routing** — OpenAI-compat OCR backend detects LM Studio base URLs and routes to the native `/api/v1/chat` endpoint with `system_prompt` + `input` items, avoiding Jinja template failures on vision OCR models. Auth token resolution via new `auth.ResolveOpenAICompatKey` falls back to stored LM Studio credentials when no explicit API key is configured. (`internal/ocr/openai_compat.go`, `internal/ocr/authkey.go`, `internal/auth/store.go`)
- **InitBuiltinTools Extraction (Tool System Refactor)** — Extracted `InitBuiltinTools()` in `internal/tool/tool.go` as the single source of truth for built-in tool registration, eliminating the 9-tool divergence between `LoadBuiltins()` and the TUI's `getInitialTools()`. Both now call `InitBuiltinTools()`. New regression tests enforce name parity. (`internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/tool/tool_test.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **OCR Tool Test Coverage** — Comprehensive test suite for the OCR tool covering openai-compat, LM Studio native, and paddle backends with mock HTTP and filesystem isolation. (`internal/tool/ocr_test.go`, `internal/ocr/openai_compat_test.go`)
- **Web UI Session Management Overhaul** — `SessionTabs` replaced with a filterable dialog-based session picker (`SessionDialog.tsx`) plus a compact open-session row (`OpenSessionBar.tsx`). TopTabs restored to original without session trigger. Sessions remain multi-tab; open tabs visible as compact row below TopTabs. (`web/src/components/Layout/SessionDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/OpenSessionBar.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/SessionTabs.tsx` (deleted), `web/src/components/Layout/TopTabs.tsx`, `web/src/App.tsx`)
- **Web UI ChatPanel Empty-State Fix** — ChatPanel now shows "Start a conversation" placeholder when no session is active instead of a blank panel. (`web/src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx`)
- **Web UI FileTree Default Expansion Depth** — Default tree expansion depth reduced from 2 to 1 for better UX on large projects. (`web/src/components/Files/FileTree.tsx`)
- **Skill Documentation** — New `ocode-desktop` and `ocode-web` skill files documenting the desktop shell and web frontend architectures. (`skills/ocode-desktop/SKILL.md`, `skills/ocode-web/SKILL.md`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.7.3 → 0.7.5.
- **Bash File-Read Redaction Expansion** — `bashFileReadCmds` now includes `grep`, `awk`, and `sed` so `grep pattern .env` and `awk -F= '{print $1}' .env` are caught by the tier-2 redaction scan (previously they bypassed it). `bashSensitiveFilePath` now handles shell pipes correctly: only the first segment before `|` is inspected (so `grep pattern .env | head -20` still detects the sensitive read), and escaped `\|` inside grep/awk/sed patterns is normalized to a space so the parser doesn't mistake a regex alternation for a shell pipe. Compound commands (`&&`, `||`, `;`) are still bailed on. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/agent/redaction_test.go`)
- **Redaction Gate Coverage** — `sensitivePrefixRe` adds `OPENROUTER_` and `DEEPINFRA_` to the concrete-prefix list and `_KEY` to the generic-suffix list, so `OPENROUTER_KEY=...`, `DEEPINFRA_KEY=...`, `SECRETMAN_KEY=...` and similar env-var names now trigger a tier-2 LLM scan in lenient mode without relying on QuickScan value-pattern matches. (`internal/redact/gate.go`, `internal/redact/gate_test.go`)
- **Paste Routing to Modal Text Inputs** — `tea.PasteMsg` now reaches the active modal's filter input instead of falling through to the chat composer. Five new branches in `Update`: picker filter (debounced via `pickerFilterApplyMsg`), ctrl+P file search, ctrl+F chat find bar, detail-view find bar, ctrl+` log search. For the session picker, the first paste/keypress also triggers `loadAllSessions` so the filter can match the full session set; the typed character is now kept (not dropped) on the first keystroke, matching the paste path. Plain-string filter inputs (`fileSearchInput`, `logSearch`, `pickerFilterPending`) normalise paste content via the new `pasteForFilter` helper: newlines/tabs become spaces, and the result is capped at 256 runes to prevent panel overflow. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/paste_routing_test.go`)
- **Immutable Dependency Root Expansion** — System-level language installation directories are now protected as immutable read roots, preventing accidental writes to language runtime installs. Go standard library paths (GOROOT, /usr/local/go, /usr/lib/go), Ruby gem paths (GEM_HOME, GEM_PATH, ~/.gem), PHP Composer cache (COMPOSER_HOME, ~/.cache/composer, ~/.composer), Java JDK/JRE paths (JAVA_HOME, /usr/lib/jvm, /Library/Java/JavaVirtualMachines), and Python site-packages (system, user, pyenv, Homebrew) are all covered. Environment variables are respected where set. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/permission_immutable_root_test.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.7.5 → 0.7.6.
- **OKF Knowledge Bundle (Core)** — New `internal/knowledge/` package implementing the OKF v0.1 knowledge bundle: recursive bundle scanning with YAML frontmatter round-trip, index/log auto-generation with cross-instance file locking (flock), and full CRUD store (`Search`, `Get`, `Write`, `Deprecate`). Bundle detection via `.okfversion` marker in `docs/`. Backed by comprehensive tests. (`internal/knowledge/bundle.go`, `internal/knowledge/doc.go`, `internal/knowledge/index.go`, `internal/knowledge/lock.go`, `internal/knowledge/store.go`, `internal/knowledge/*_test.go`)
- **Context Sub-Agent & Doc Tools** — New `context` sub-agent as the sole automated writer of the knowledge bundle, injected with `doc_search`, `doc_get`, `doc_write`, `doc_deprecate` tools for reading, writing, and deprecating bundle documents. Background doc maintenance worker handles triage (decides if durable knowledge was produced) and execution (dispatches the context agent) with drain-on-shutdown lifecycle. (`internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/agent/doc_tools.go`, `internal/agent/doc_maintenance.go`)
- **`knowledge_lookup` Tool** — New built-in tool for semantic retrieval from the knowledge bundle. Dispatches the `context` sub-agent to answer why/decision/playbook questions, soft-failing with a `/docs init` hint when the bundle is inactive. (`internal/agent/knowledge_lookup.go`)
- **`task_cancel` Tool** — New built-in tool that cooperatively cancels background sub-agent runs (by run ID) at the next step boundary. Ownership enforced by dispatcher identity. (`internal/agent/task_cancel.go`, `internal/agent/task_cancel_test.go`)
- **Agent Run Registry Enhancements** — Registry now auto-prunes completed runs to the newest 30. Token usage display added per-run and aggregate. `enumNames` filter exposes only visible agents. (`internal/agent/agent_runs.go`, `internal/agent/registry.go`, `internal/agent/subagent.go`)
- **TUI `/docs` Commands** — New `/docs on|off` toggle for doc-first development prompt (master switch for the knowledge system), `/docs status` for bundle state, `/docs init` to bootstrap the bundle, `/docs update` for maintenance pass, `/docs cleanup` to list/delete deprecated docs. All dispatched from `handleDocsCmd`. (`internal/tui/docs_knowledge.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`)
- **TUI Agent Detail Knowledge Tab** — New knowledge section in the agent detail view showing knowledge bundle activity. (`internal/tui/detail_view.go`)
- **AGENTS.md Documentation** — Comprehensive knowledge system documentation added covering bundle structure, context/explore agent roles, doc tools, the `knowledge_lookup` tool, `/docs` subcommands, and the maintenance worker lifecycle.
- **Stale Docs Cleanup** — Removed ~80 stale `docs/superpowers/plans/` and `docs/superpowers/specs/` files superseded by completed feature implementations. Existing OKF knowledge docs (`docs/plugins.md`, `docs/zed.md`, `docs/acp-zed-spec.md`, `docs/title-generation-analysis.md`) retained and updated.
- **Version Bump** — 0.7.6 → 0.8.0.
- **Loopback Netcat Auto-Allow** — `nc`/`ncat` connections to loopback hosts (`127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`, `localhost`) are now auto-allowed instead of being flagged as a harmful exfiltration risk, since they stay on-host and cannot leak data off-machine. Non-loopback `nc` (e.g. `nc evil.com 443`) still always requires human approval. New `netcatHostIsLoopback` / `isLoopbackHost` / `isAllDigits` / `isLoopbackNetcat` helpers drive both the harmful-check bypass and the early `PermissionAllow` in `decideSingleCommand`, with regression tests covering IP, `-w` timeout, `localhost`, IPv6, and `< file` redirect variants. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/permissions_test.go`)

### Fixed
- **LLM Stream Errors Excluded from Prompt** — When an LLM call fails (server error, no-response, network failure), the failure is now rendered in the transcript but marked `skipLLM` so it is no longer folded back into the prompt on the next turn or retry as a fake assistant turn, which corrupted the conversation. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Streamed Tool Output Ordering** — Live-bash-streaming chunks that were still buffered in `deltaCh`/`pendingStreamDeltas` when the canonical tool result arrived are now dropped instead of appended, preventing a duplicated trailing line in both the transcript and the LLM prompt. The final tool message is marked `streamFinalized`; `appendShellOutput` ignores any subsequent chunk for a finalized message. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Sub-Agent File Changes Visible in Sidebar** — Sub-agents now share the parent agent's snapshot store so file writes performed by sub-agents (e.g., the `context` agent writing knowledge docs) appear in the TUI sidebar's Files section. Previously each sub-agent created an isolated store that the sidebar never read from. (`internal/agent/subagent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Unknown Tool Hallucination Guard** — `isToolAllowed()` now returns `false` for tool names not present in `a.tools`, preventing confusing "tool not found" errors from model-hallucinated tool names. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **OCR OpenAI-Compat Timeout Increased** — Timeout raised from 60s to 5 minutes to accommodate slow local vision models (cold model load ~16s, warm inference ~36s for large images like phone-photo receipts). (`internal/ocr/openai_compat.go`)
- **monaco-editor npm Audit Fix** — Downgraded `monaco-editor` from `^0.55.1` to `^0.53.0` to resolve 24 moderate-severity `dompurify` CVEs. The dev-range 0.54+ bundled `dompurify@3.2.7` and `marked@14.0.0` with known XSS vulnerabilities; 0.53.0 uses `@types/trusted-types` instead and has no transitive vulns. Updated `monaco-setup.ts` type annotations for API compatibility with the older stable release, and rebuilt the production bundle. (`web/package.json`, `web/src/lib/monaco-setup.ts`)
- **Heredoc Classification Skips Leading Comment Lines** — Fixed `extractHeredocs()` to properly handle commands starting with a `#` comment line before the first heredoc operator. Previously the comment was treated as the header line, causing empty heredoc bodies to be silently discarded. (`internal/agent/permissions.go`, `internal/agent/permission_interpreter_test.go`)
- **Compaction Race Condition with Pending UI Index** — Fixed compaction result being silently discarded when a second compaction is triggered before the first one's result is applied. Added `pendingCompactUIIdx` gating in `askAgent()` and `streamDoneMsg` preflight to prevent in-flight mappings from being overwritten. Deferred background job completions (`pendingJobMsgs`) during compaction to avoid corrupting the message splice. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Question Dialog Submit Button Click Support** — Added mouse-click handling for the "Submit" button in the question dialog, enabling mouse-driven answer confirmation in the TUI in addition to the existing keyboard-driven path. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Data Race in File Tree Scroll Handler** — Fixed a data race in the file tree horizontal scroll handling where a dereference guard was inadvertently placed outside the proper scope. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Off-By-One Pagination in DocSearch** — `DocSearchTool` treated `page` as 1-based but passed it directly to `store.Search()`, whose `paginate()` used 0-based arithmetic (`start := page * pageSize`). Page 1 computed `start = 20`, skipping the first page entirely. Fixed by converting to 0-based before calling `Search()`. (`internal/agent/doc_tools.go`, `internal/knowledge/store.go`)
- **EOF Frontmatter Parsing Edge Case** — `extractFrontmatter()` failed to detect the closing `---` delimiter when it appeared at EOF without a trailing newline (e.g., `---\ntitle: test\n---` at EOF), incorrectly marking such documents as non-conforming. Added fallback checks for `\n---` and `\r\n---` at EOF. (`internal/knowledge/doc.go`)
- **`.okfignore` Directory Path Matching** — `excludedDir()` only matched against the directory's base name, so patterns like `legacy/archived/` never matched. Moved `relPath` computation before the directory check and updated `excludedDir()` to match `.okfignore` patterns against both the basename and the relative path, matching `excludedFile()` behavior. (`internal/knowledge/bundle.go`)

- **Projects Support** — New project management system enabling users to organize and persist session collections per workspace. New `internal/projects/` package with project CRUD operations, `GET/POST /api/projects`, `DELETE /api/projects/:id`, project listing and details endpoints. Frontend `ProjectStore` (React context + hooks) manages project state, new `ProjectSidebar` component for navigation with pinned/unpinned project organization. Locked in by integration tests. (`internal/projects/`, `internal/server/handler_projects.go`, `web/src/stores/projectStore.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/ProjectSidebar.tsx`)
- **Shadcn UI Component Library** — Ported essential UI components from shadcn/ui (React + TypeScript + Tailwind) for consistency with web design system: Badge, Input, ScrollArea, Tabs, Tooltip. Each component includes Radix primitives as peer dependencies and can be styled independently. Enables faster future feature development with tested, accessible primitives. (`web/src/components/ui/badge.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/input.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/scroll-area.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/tabs.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/tooltip.tsx`)
- **Layout Reorganization** — New `SessionTabs` component centralizes session tab management, replacing inline tab logic in main App. `ProjectSidebar` and `SessionTabs` split layout concerns, making navigation more modular and testable. (`web/src/components/Layout/SessionTabs.tsx`, `web/src/components/Layout/ProjectSidebar.tsx`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.16 → 0.7.0.
- **Desktop App (ocode-desktop)** — Native cross-platform desktop shell: a Wails v3 (pinned `v3.0.0-alpha2.111`) webview window over an in-process ocode server on a random loopback port with a per-launch auth token, reusing the web UI and `/api/*` contract unchanged. System tray (show/quit), dock badge with running-agent count (macOS/Windows), finished/failed-run notifications when unfocused with click-to-focus, native error dialog on boot failure, `OCODE_DESKTOP_DEV_URL` dev override. New shared SPA embed `web/embed.go` (`web.FS()`), exported `Handler.RunStates()`/`Server.RunStates()` cross-session run snapshot, pure-Go `internal/desktop` boot helper + poll-and-diff watcher (`Diff` keyed by session+run ID). Build via `make desktop` / `make desktop-app` (macOS bundle through `scripts/bundle-macos.sh`). Locked in by `web/embed_test.go`, `internal/server/run_states_test.go`, `internal/desktop/boot_test.go`, `internal/desktop/watch_test.go`. (`cmd/ocode-desktop/`, `internal/desktop/`, `internal/server/run_states.go`, `web/embed.go`, `Makefile`, `scripts/bundle-macos.sh`)
- **Redaction Awareness in Discovery Context** — When the session redactor is active, `injectDiscoveryContext` now appends a system message explaining that `[[OCSEC:...]]` tokens are redacted secrets (not placeholder text). New `redactionAwarenessPrompt` constant and conditional injection gated by `redactionEnabled` and `redactionRegistry`. Locked in by `TestInjectDiscoveryContextRedactionAwareness`. (`internal/agent/discovery_glue.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_glue_test.go`)
- **Recap Pending Tasks** — `runRecap()` now includes a numbered "5. TASKS" section and calls `pendingTasksForRecap()` to enumerate running background processes and sub-agent runs (with ID, command/name, and duration). New `Agent.pendingTasksForRecap()` helper. Short recap shows pending count only. (`internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.15 → 0.6.16.
- **Grapheme-Cluster-Aware Line Width Measurement** — Refactored `visualLineWidth` in `internal/tui/selection.go` from per-rune `runewidth.RuneWidth` iteration to `ansi.StringWidth` which correctly handles grapheme clusters (e.g. emoji with variation selectors like "🗺️"). The old naive sum undercounted such sequences by one cell, causing misaligned borders on lines containing them. Tab handling refactored to work cleanly with the new approach.
- **Width-Only Render Cache Fast Path** — Split `msgRenderKey` into width-independent `msgRenderRawKey` + width-baked `msgRenderKey`. On viewport resize, `renderMessageBlock` now skips expensive Chroma/markdown re-rendering and reuses cached `innerContent` (markdown rendered user text / Chroma colored tool output), only redoing the cheap box-layout step. Covers tool blocks (`buildToolOutputBox`), assistant text (re-wrap with `wrapView`), and user message bubbles. New `innerContent`/`rawLineCount` fields on `msgRenderCacheEntry`. Refactored `renderUserTextWithInner`, `renderToolOutputBoxWithInner`, `buildToolOutputBox` from the old monolithic `renderUserText`/`renderToolOutputBox`. Locked in by `TestResizeFastPathMatchesFullRerender` and `BenchmarkResize_Pairs17`/`BenchmarkHugeMessage_500KB`. (`internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/render_cache_bench_test.go`, `internal/tui/render_cache_test.go`)
- **Granular Interpreter Subprocess Permissions** — Replaced flat subprocess rejection in the permission interpreter with granular classification. Safe local tools (ffmpeg, grep, pdf2html, etc.) now auto-allowed when LLM-approved; network-capable subprocesses (curl, wget, nc) require localhost targeting; shell/interpreters (sh, bash, python, node, etc.) always escalated. Python triple-quoted string bodies stripped before analysis to prevent shell-command false positives. New helpers: `stripPythonTripleQuotedBodies`, `pythonIsLikelyPureFileOp`, `extractPythonOpenPaths`, `classifyInterpreterSubprocess`, `isInterpreterSubprocessBinary`, `isNetworkSubprocessBinary`, `subprocessTargetsLocalhost`. Locked in by comprehensive tests. (`internal/agent/permission_interpreter.go`, `internal/agent/permission_interpreter_test.go`)
- **AST Tool Default-On, ast-grep as Opt-In Plugin** — The LSP-backed `ast` semantic navigation tool is now registered by default whenever any language server is available on PATH, requiring no plugin toggle. The `ast-grep` CLI structural search/rewrite tool becomes the new opt-in plugin gated by `plugins.ast` (toggle with `/plugin enable ast`). New `AnyServerInstalled()` in `lsp/manager.go`. New `AstGrepTool` implementation and tests. (`internal/tool/tool.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/lsp/manager.go`, `internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/tui/commands.go`, `internal/tool/ast_grep.go`, `internal/tool/ast_grep_test.go`)
- **Recap Model Feature** — New `RecapModel` and `RecapModelEnabled` config fields for a dedicated summarization model. TUI sidebar toggle (`recap-model`) and `/recap` command with `model`, `status`, `enable`/`disable` subcommands. Agent `recapClient()` prefers recap model, falls back to small/main. Auto-recap runs asynchronously on stream completion when enabled and ≥4 messages exist. Recap context budget limited to 48K chars (~12K tokens). (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`, `internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/picker.go`, `internal/server/tui_status.go`)
- **Short Auto-Recap** — Auto-recap now uses a compact 1-line format (`recap: ...`) in the transcript instead of the full multi-section caveman block. New `RecapAsyncShort()` method extracts shared logic via private `recapAsync()`/`runRecap()` with a `short` parameter, generating a max-100-character one-sentence summary. `RecapResult` and `recapFinishedMsg` carry a `Short` flag so the TUI renders the correct format. Full `/recap` command unchanged. (`internal/agent/agent.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/tui/model_test.go`)
- **Pinned Skills Sync** — New `SyncPinnedSkills()` on Agent rebuilds the discovery session when the pinned set changes, preserving non-pinned `discover_more` attachments. TUI `/skills pin`, `/skills unpin`, `/skills pinned` commands. Pinned skills bypass embedding ranking and are always attached. (`internal/agent/discovery_glue.go`, `internal/agent/discovery_glue_test.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Environment Prompt Enrichment** — Env prompt now shows paths for global opencode/ocode configs, project ocode settings, project opencode config, and all skill search directories. (`internal/agent/prompt.go`)
- **Permission Remote Runner Fix** — `consultPermissionModel` skips interpreter analysis for remote runners (`npx`, `bunx`) whose source cannot be analyzed, falling through to the plain ALLOW/DENY path. (`internal/agent/agent.go`)
- **Permission Shell Control Keyword Exclusion** — Shell control-flow keywords (`if`, `else`, `while`, `for`, `case`, etc.) excluded from the always-allow prefix rule since they are not real commands. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Skill Installer Custom-Modified Detection** — New `statusCustomModified` status using a `.bundled-hash` sidecar to distinguish user-edited skills from outdated ones. Bulk upgrade skips custom-modified skills unless named or forced. `all` keyword for explicit bulk targeting. (`internal/skill/installer.go`, `internal/skill/installer_test.go`, `internal/skill/loader.go`)
- **ExtraAllowedPaths Deduplication** — Duplicate paths removed when writing `extra_allowed_paths` to `ocodeconfig.json`. (`internal/config/ocodeconfig.go`)
- **Theme Dim Colour Fix** — Darkened `Dim` in one built-in theme from `#3A2B1A` to `#6B5A48`. (`internal/theme/theme.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.12 → 0.6.13.
- **Skills as Slash Commands** — All loaded skills are now registered as slash commands (e.g. `/git-commit-push`). The directory name is used as the command key. Built-in and custom commands take priority — if a command with the same name already exists, the skill is not registered. Deduplication follows skill search-path order: `.opencode/skills/` wins over `.claude/skill/`. (`internal/tui/commands.go`)
- **LCARS Theme + Empty-State Art** — New `lcars` theme with Star Trek / LCARS-inspired warm amber panels and five random empty-state ASCII art variants (`internal/tui/startrek_art.go`) selected on session start, `/new`, and `/theme` switch. Locked in by `startrek_art_test.go` and `theme_test.go`.
- **Theme Display Names** — Themes now support an optional `Label` field for human-readable display names (e.g. "lcars (Star Trek OS)"). Theme picker, `/theme` cycle, and theme switch messages use display names. Exposed via `GET /api/theme` with a new `label` response field. (`internal/theme/theme.go`, `internal/tui/theme.go`, `internal/tui/picker.go`, `internal/tui/model.go`, `internal/server/handler_theme.go`)
- **Empty-State Art Abstraction** — Refactored pipboy-only art into a general `m.refreshThemeArt()` and `m.renderEmptyStateBackground()` switch, enabling any theme to provide empty-state art. Selection state, `transcriptLines`, and `rawTranscriptLines` now cleared on `/new` to prevent stale render artifacts. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Permission Interpreter Retry on Parse Failure** — When the permission model returns unparseable JSON, the interpreter now retries once with a corrective prompt instead of immediately failing closed. New `buildPermissionInterpreterRetryPrompt` helper. Locked in by tests. (`internal/agent/permission_interpreter.go`, `internal/agent/permission_interpreter_test.go`)
- **MD Cache Concurrent-Write Safety** — `saveMDCache` now merges entries from a concurrently-written on-disk cache before writing, preventing summary loss when multiple ocode instances share a project. (`internal/agent/md_discovery.go`)
- **Config Model Resolution Preference Fix** — Resolved a regression where ocode's own last-model preference (`GetLastModel()`) was incorrectly skipped when `OPENCODE_MODEL` was set. Preference order is now: env var > `GetLastModel()` > opencode recent list. (`internal/config/config.go`)
- **Orchestrator Pipeline Silent-Halt Report** — The HALT report now surfaces compile output when the last iteration fails at compile (not validation), so users can see why the pipeline stopped. Advisor note and iteration history included in the report. (`internal/orchestrator/pipeline.go`, `internal/orchestrator/report.go`, tests)
- **File Search Hidden Directory Guard** — `scanWorkspaceFiles` now only skips hidden directories at non-root levels, preventing hidden files at the workspace root from being incorrectly filtered when `showHidden` is enabled. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **File Browser Keybinding Hint Fix** — Fixed the file browser tree hint to show `ctrl+d del` instead of `D del`, matching the reworked keybindings. (`internal/tui/files_model_test.go`)
- **Model Picker Case Indentation Fix** — Fixed incorrect indentation in the `modelPickerFullModelsLoadedMsg` handler. (`internal/tui/model.go`)
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.10 → 0.6.12.
- **Pipboy Theme ASCII Art** — Three vault-boy braille art variants (`internal/tui/pipboy_art.go`) randomly selected on session start, `/new`, and `/theme` switch to the pipboy theme. New `RandomPipboyArt()` function. Locked in by `pipboy_art_test.go`.
- **File Browser Keybinding Rework** — All single-letter file operation keybindings migrated to `ctrl+letter` equivalents: `ctrl+n` (new file), `ctrl+b` (new folder), `ctrl+r` (rename), `ctrl+d` (delete), `ctrl+l` (inline edit), `ctrl+y` (copy path), `ctrl+e` (editor), `ctrl+v` (pick editor), `ctrl+o` (reveal in finder), `ctrl+f` (content search), `ctrl+g` (fuzzy find), `ctrl+h` (toggle hidden), `ctrl+u` (refresh tree). Prevents conflicts with typing in filtered views.
- **Theme-Aware UI Styling Consolidation** — Hardcoded colour values replaced with theme-based style globals (`selectedStyle`, `errorStyle`, `userStyle`, `hintStyle`, etc.) across buttons (`component_button.go`), listbox (`component_listbox.go`), git exit button (`git_model.go`), files search bar and exit button (`files_model.go`), slash popup (`slash_popup.go`), and URL links (`urllink.go`). Selection highlight colours in `ApplyThemeColors` set from `SelectedFg`/`SelectedBg`.
- **Model Picker Async Loading** — `openModelPicker()` now shows favourites and recently used models immediately, then loads the full provider model list in a background goroutine and appends it via new `modelPickerFullModelsLoadedMsg`. Picker opening functions (`openModelPicker`, `openAdvisorPicker`, `openPermissionModelPicker`, `openRedactionModelPicker`) return `tea.Cmd`; `/model`, `/small-model`, `/mask model`, `/advisor` commands now propagate the cmd.
- **File Search Hidden Files Toggle** — New `fileSearchShowHidden` field and `ctrl+h` keybind in the file search overlay toggles hidden file visibility. `scanWorkspaceFiles` now accepts a `showHidden` parameter.
- **Theme Save Messages Marked Transient** — Theme switch success/failure messages are now `transient` so they don't persist in conversation history after a `/theme` command.
- **"matrix" Theme Colour Refresh** — Brighter Border, Hint, StatusFg, and Dim values. Added `Thinking` colour field to `ThemeColors`.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.7 → 0.6.10.
- **Bash File-Read Redaction in Scan Chokepoint** — `scanToolResult` now detects sensitive-file reads from bash file-reading commands (`cat`, `head`, `tail`, `bat`, `less`, `more`) in addition to the `read` tool. New `bashSensitiveFilePath` helper parses bash tool args for sensitive file paths, enabling tier-2 redaction on bash-mediated file reads.
- **Theme-Aware Selection Highlight** — Replaced hardcoded reverse-video (`\x1b[7m`) with explicit foreground+background colours from the theme's `SelectedFg`/`SelectedBg`, making text selection reliably visible on syntax-highlighted content. New `selectionHighlightOpen`/`selectionHighlightClose` globals, `SetSelectionHighlightCodes`, `hexColorToANSIBackground`, `hexColorToANSIForeground`, `parseHexColor` helpers in `internal/tui/selection.go`.
- **Permission Dialog Input Preservation** — Removed three `m.input.Reset()` calls from permission dialog close paths (keyboard, enter, mouse), preserving user input across dialog appearance and dismissal instead of silently discarding it.
- **Keyword-Entropy Span Fix** — `addSpansFromKeywordMatches` now emits only the value part (capture group 2) of keyword=value matches instead of the full `keyword=value` span, preventing over-redaction of the keyword prefix.
- **Async Git Diff Loading** — Refactored `loadDiff()` into async `startLoadDiff()` returning a `tea.Cmd`, moving git execution and file reads off the TUI event loop. New `diffReadyMsg` message type with sequence number and workdir for stale-message rejection. New `gitRunInDir()` package-level helper for use by goroutines. New `diffSizeLimit` (256 KB) and `capDiff()` prevent large diffs from blocking rendering. New `diffLoadSeq`/`diffLoading` fields track loading state. Mouse click handlers on the git tab use `startLoadDiff()` instead of synchronous `loadDiff()`. `newGitModel()` returns an initial diff load command fired from `model.Init()` and `runCdCmd`. Locked in by `TestDiffReadyMsgRejectsStaleWorkDir`.
- **Visual-Width Sidebar Path Truncation** — `formatSidebarFilePath` switched from byte `len()` to `ansi.StringWidth`/`ansi.Truncate` so CJK and emoji characters in file paths no longer overflow the sidebar space.
- **Panel Width Guard Against Double-Width Characters** — Inner panel width changed from `-7` to `-8` to prevent terminal double-width character shift from corrupting right-aligned elements in VS Code.
- **`undo_file_change` Tool** — New built-in tool that rolls back a previous `write`, `edit`, `multi_edit`, `multi_file_edit`, `replace_lines`, or `delete` by `tool_call_id`. Backs the rollback with a per-agent `snapshot.Store` keyed by the originating LLM tool call id. Detects and refuses three failure modes: undo expired (>2 agent steps since the write), cross-agent write to the same file after the targeted write, and same-agent newer write to the same file still within the undo window. Refused undo errors include the blocking `tool_call_id` so the LLM can reason about whether to retry. New `ContextualTool` interface in `internal/tool/tool.go` lets the agent pass the per-agent store + `tool_call_id` via `context.Context` to write tools. New `internal/tool/undo.go` implements the tool. `PatchTool` (`apply_patch`) is intentionally not undoable by `tool_call_id` because it has no tool-call-id; its atomic-rollback path continues to use the global snapshot store. Locked in by tests in `internal/snapshot/snapshot_test.go` (round-trip, expired, cross-agent, same-agent, new-file delete, `Reset` unregisters from cross-agent registry, context helpers, advance-step semantics) and `internal/tool/undo_test.go` (write round-trip, delete round-trip, missing `tool_call_id`, unknown `tool_call_id`, `multi_file_edit` atomic rollback uses per-agent store).
- **Per-Agent Snapshot Store Plumbing** — `Agent` now owns a `*snapshot.Store` created with a unique 8-byte hex `agentID`. `Step` calls `AdvanceStep()` at the top of each loop iteration; `executeToolCall` builds a `context.Context` carrying the store + `tool_call_id` and uses it for any `ContextualTool`. `Shutdown` calls `Reset` on the store to unregister the agent from the global cross-agent write registry so surviving agents are not blocked by stale same-agent writes.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.6 → 0.6.7.
- **Project Markdown Discovery (`md_discovery.go`)** — New system that summarizes project `*.md` files (except always-on briefing set) into a cached discovery corpus at `.ocode/md-summaries.json`. Summaries generated by small model (fallback to main client) in blocking first pass with bounded concurrency. Full file content attached to volatile tail on query match only. Editing a doc invalidates its summary on next throttled scan. `DiscoveryStatusInfo` extended with `AttachedMD`, `AllMD`, `MDPending` fields. Locked in by tests in `md_discovery_test.go`.
- **Git Log Full Diff Toggle** — `enter` on the git log section toggles between stat and full diff view. The full diff enables hunk-by-hunk navigation from the log panel. Shows `"enter full diff"` / `"enter stat view"` hints accordingly.
- **Git Branch Merge (`ctrl+m`)** — New `ctrl+m` keybind in the branches section: first press shows confirmation prompt, second press merges the selected branch into the current branch. Uses the same two-press confirmation pattern as `ctrl+x` delete branch.
- **Git Tab Auto-Refresh** — Switching to the git tab now triggers `cmdAutoRefresh()` on tab change via keyboard (`alt+[` / `alt+]`) and mouse click/motion events.
- **Discovery Status Display Rework** — `/discover status` and `/context` now show a unified view of all three discovery categories (skills, MCP tools, project docs) with `●` for attached (full summary injected) and `○` for name-only. Pending doc summarizations shown with a `(N doc summaries still generating…)` note.
- **`/docs` Slash Command & `/doc-mode` Toggle** — New slash command to toggle injection of a documentation-first development prompt into the agent's system prompt. Supports `on|off|status` subcommands. `DocPromptEnabled` config field persisted in `ocodeconfig.json`. Doc prompt instructs the agent to read existing docs before implementing and update them afterward. Locked in by `TestBasePromptMessages_IncludesDocPromptWhenEnabled`, `TestBasePromptMessages_DoesNotIncludeDocPromptWhenDisabled`, and `TestDocPromptEnabledLoadSave`.
- **Goal System (replaces Orchestrator)** — New `goal` CLI subcommand and `/goal` TUI slash command replacing the old `orchestrate` pipeline. Old orchestrator files (`internal/cli/orchestrate.go`, `internal/cli/orchestrate_test.go`, `internal/tui/orchestrate.go`, `internal/tui/orchestrate_test.go`) removed. New implementation in `internal/cli/goal.go`, `internal/cli/goal_test.go`, `internal/tui/goal.go`, `internal/tui/goal_test.go`. `main.go` routes `goal` instead of `orchestrate`.
- **Mouse Motion Throttle** — `newInputFilter` now throttles `MouseMotionMsg` events at 60fps alongside existing mouse-wheel throttling, preventing CPU pegging from VS Code's pixel-resolution mouse motion. New `motionRenderInterval` constant. Locked in by `TestInputFilterThrottlesMotion` and updated `TestInputFilterOnlyThrottlesWheelAndMotion`.
- **Shell Execution Activity Row** — The TUI activity row now tracks the currently running `!shell` command with an elapsed timer using new `shellCmdStart` and `shellCmdText` model fields. Timer cleared on shell completion or `/new`.
- **Permission Dialog Case-Insensitive Keys** — Permission dialog now accepts uppercase `Y`, `N`, `A`, `T` in addition to lowercase, normalized via `strings.ToLower`.
- **Files Panel Scrollbar Fix** — Fixed the files tab preview scrollbar hit-test column alignment: `scrollbarTrackTop()` accounts for `previewHeaderLines()` and scrollbar `X` derived from `treeW + preview.Width()`. New `filesPanelPicker`/`filesPanelPreview` constants for correct scrollbar drag routing.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.1 → 0.6.3.
- **Project Type Signals in Discovery Query** — `discoveryQueryFromMessages` now accepts a `workDir` parameter and appends project type signals detected from marker files (`go.mod`, `pubspec.yaml`, `package.json`, etc.) to the discovery query, providing the embedder with project context for more relevant skill selection. Monorepo support via one-level subdirectory scanning with deduplication. New `projectSignals` helper. Locked in by `TestProjectSignals`.
- **Shell Output `noUserMerge` Guard** — `buildAgentMessagesSnapshot` now uses a `noUserMerge` flag per message to prevent shell-command output (converted to `Role:"user"` messages) from being collapsed into adjacent real user prompts during the consecutive-user-merge pass. Locked in by `TestBuildSnapshotShellOutputDoesNotMergeIntoUserPrompts`.
- **`/doc-sync` Slash Command** — New command that syncs AGENTS.md, rules, and skill docs to reflect current code changes using a small model. Runs on `doc-sync`-eligible small model to keep costs low. Supports `session` (changes since session start) and `all` (full diff) modes. Registered in `handleCommand` as a queued command (waits for current stream to end).
- **Per-MCP-Tool Token Estimates** — `/context` now shows individual tool token counts per MCP server, giving visibility into which tools consume the most context budget.
- **Skill Catalog Token Breakdown** — `/context` displays per-skill token estimates instead of a single compact catalog number, making it easier to identify heavy skills.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.0 → 0.6.1.
- **Plugin Agent Loading** — Agent registry now supports plugin-aware markdown agent loading via `ReloadMarkdownAgents(enabled)`. Orchestrator agents moved from `.opencode/agents/` to `.opencode/plugins/orchestrator/agents/`. Plugin loader supports `agents/` and `commands/` subdirectories. New `LoadPluginCommandDirPaths`, `LoadGlobalPluginAgentsDirPaths`, `LoadProjectPluginAgentsDirPaths`. Locked in by `TestOrchestratorAgentFilesParse`.
- **Discovery Volatility Split** — `injectDiscoveryContext` split into stable (system-role) and volatile (user-role) messages for Anthropic prompt cache stability. New `renderDiscoveryContext` helper with volatility-split contract. Locked in by `TestRenderDiscoveryContext`.
- **Environment Prompt Cache** — `environmentPrompt()` now caches its result keyed by date; cache invalidated on workdir/model change via `clearEnvironmentPromptCache()`. `PrepareMessages` strips stale env block before re-insertion.
- **Recap Timeout Configuration** — New `recap_timeout_seconds` config field (default 120). `recapTimeoutSeconds()` method on Agent reads the configured value.
- **Docker Support** — `.dockerignore`, `Dockerfile`, `docker-compose.yml` for containerized ocode. New Docker Makefile targets (`docker`, `docker-build`, `docker-serve`, `docker-run`) with volume mounts for config, data, and workspace.
- **Config File Atomic Write** — `writeOcodeConfigFile` uses tmp+rename atomic write, preventing corruption on crash.
- **Prompt Cache Stability Guidance** — New `AGENTS.md` section documenting Anthropic prompt cache ordering rules, tool array determinism, stickiness for growing tool sets, and the volatility-split pattern for tail injections.
- **Version Bump** — 0.5.15 → 0.6.0.
- **Sidebar Temperature & Reasoning Display** — The sidebar now shows the effective LLM temperature and thinking/reasoning budget level on a dedicated line above the context window, giving the user at-a-glance visibility of model configuration. New `Agent.EffectiveTemperature()` method exposes the configured temperature from the client or spec. Locked in by `TestEffectiveTemperature`.
- **Model Temperature Defaults Expansion** — `defaultTemperature` extended with support for north-mini-code, claude, gemini, glm-4.5/4.6/4.7/5, deepseek-v4, grok (reasoning & non-reasoning), gemma, mistral/codestral/devstral, cohere/command, llama, nemotron, minimax-m3, mimo, and kimi-k2 (thinking & base) model families. `defaultTopP` extended with qwen and additional kimi-k2 variants. Locked in by comprehensive tests.
- **Read Tool `offset`/`limit` Aliases** — `ReadTool.Execute` now recognizes `offset` and `limit` as aliases for `start_line`/`end_line`, preventing silent reread loops when models emit Claude-Code-style pagination keys. Locked in by `TestReadToolOffsetLimitAliases`.
- **Detail View Text Selection** — Moved text selection initiation before the generic detail click handler, enabling text selection in the agent detail view. Selection survives live transcript refresh ticks. Locked in by `TestDetailViewMouseStartsTextSelection` and `TestDetailSelectionSurvivesLiveRefresh`.
- **Detail Scrollbar Column Alignment** — Fixed `detailScrollbarX()` to derive from layout constants (`detailContentLeftX` + `detailViewportWidth()`) rather than `panelWidth() - 3`, aligning the hit-test column with the rendered scrollbar. Locked in by `TestDetailScrollbarColumnMatchesHitTest` and `TestDetailScrollbarThumbDragScrolls`.
- **Version Bump** — 0.5.13 → 0.5.15.
- **User Embed Server URL** — `DiscoveryConfig.LocalServerURL` lets users point the local discovery backend at an already-running embed server (LM Studio, custom llama-server). `LocalServerOptions{UserBaseURL}` is probed first in `EnsureLocalServer`; if unreachable, falls back to the bundled server. Locked in by `TestDiscoveryConfigRoundTrip`.
- **tar.gz Artifact Extraction** — `EnsureArtifact` now handles `Artifact.Archive="tar.gz"`: downloads the tarball, verifies SHA, extracts to a versioned subdirectory preserving upstream layout (e.g. `llama-b9747/llama-server`), and writes a sidecar marker for cache invalidation. Sibling libraries are extracted alongside the binary so `DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH` resolves them at spawn. Locked in by `TestEnsureArtifactExtractsTarGZ`, `TestEnsureArtifactArchiveInvalidatesOnSHAMismatch`, `TestExtractTarGZRejectsZipSlip`.
- **Artifact Download Retries** — Transient download failures retry up to 3 times with exponential backoff (250ms, 500ms, 1s). Locked in by `TestEnsureArtifactRetriesTransientDownloadError`.
- **BGE-M3 Model + llama.cpp b9747** — Manifest updated from LFM2.5-Embedding-350M to BGE-M3 (bbvch-ai/bge-m3-GGUF:Q4_K_M) with llama.cpp release tarballs (b9747) pinned per platform. Dynamic model ID in the embedding picker reads from `CurrentManifest()` instead of hardcoding.
- **User-Facing Discovery Notices** — `DebugLog.Entry` gains a `UserFacing` flag; `emitUserDiscoveryDebug` tags download/spawn events that the TUI promotes to the chat transcript via `appendDiscoveryNotice` (transient, skips LLM). Locked in by `TestAppendDiscoveryNoticeIsTransientAndSkipsLLM`, `TestDebugLogMsgPromotesOnlyNewUserFacingEntries`.
- **`/discovery` Merged into `/discover`** — The `/discovery` slash command is removed; `/discover` now accepts `enable|disable` as well as `status|model`.
- **Discovery Cache Under Global Data Dir** — `discoveryCacheDir()` uses `paths.GlobalDataDir()` (alongside sessions/auth/usage) instead of `os.UserConfigDir()`, keeping all ocode state in one consistent location.
- **Web UI Tailscale Path Prefix Support** — `web/index.html` injects a runtime `<base>` element derived from the session path prefix, so built asset URLs resolve correctly behind `tailscale serve --set-path`. Vite `base` changed to `"./"` for relative asset references.
- **OnDiscovery Callback** — `Agent.OnDiscovery` field fires with newly attached skill/MCP names after each discovery ranking turn. TUI `streamStep` wires it to the delta channel, surfacing real-time discovery notices in the chat transcript. Locked in by `TestOnDiscoveryCallback`.
- **DiscoveryStatusInfo Split** — `DiscoveryStatusInfo` now exposes `SkillTotal`, `AttachedSkills`, and `AttachedMCP` separately, giving `/discover status` and `/context` clearer per-category visibility into attached skills versus MCP tools.
- **Web UI Session Tab Panels** — `SessionPage` now renders `FileTree`, `GitPanel`, `LogPanel`, and `AssetsPanel` tab content, enabling the web UI to show files, git, logs, and asset management panels inline.
- **Discovery Ignore Paths** — New `IgnorePaths` field on `DiscoveryConfig` (`ocodeconfig.go`) lets users configure path prefixes/glob patterns that the markdown discovery walker skips (default `["skills/", ".opencode/", ".claude/", ".qwen/"]`). New `/discover ignore [add|remove|clear]` slash command to manage ignores at runtime. `walkMarkdownFiles` gained `ignorePaths` parameter; new `mdMatchesIgnorePaths` helper matches against both plain prefixes and glob patterns. `SaveDiscoveryIgnorePaths` persists the list. Locked in by discovery config round-trip tests.
- **OnMDIndexing Callback** — `Agent.OnMDIndexing` field fires with the repo-relative path of each markdown file needing a fresh summary during `mdSummarizePass`. Does NOT send to LLM — purely for TUI progress surfacing. Wired to the `"md-indexing"` delta kind, shown as `"Indexing: <path>"` discovery notices in the transcript.

### Changed
- **Discovery Ignore Paths Merge Semantics** — Custom `discovery.ignore_paths` entries in `ocodeconfig.json` are now *merged with* the built-in default list rather than replacing it. Previously, setting `"ignore_paths": ["docs/"]` would ignore only `docs/`; now the built-in defaults (`skills/`, `.opencode/`, `.claude/`, etc.) are always included alongside user entries. The `/discover ignore clear` command resets to defaults rather than clearing the list entirely. If you relied on `ignore_paths: []` to disable all ignores, your config now receives the defaults.
- **Project Markdown Discovery Prompting** — `/discover` now keeps project `*.md` files out of the stable name-index entirely unless they are attached for the current turn. Attached docs are injected as filename + summary only; full file bodies are no longer pre-injected into the prompt.
- **Discovery Selection Policy** — Added `SelectMin` (0.40) as an absolute minimum similarity threshold; `SelectRankRelative` now requires items to pass BOTH the relative delta (`SelectDelta=0.15`) AND the absolute minimum (`>=SelectMin`). `SelectFloor` reduced from 5 to 0, eliminating forced low-quality skill attachments. Locked in by updated `TestSelectRankRelativeBounds`.
- **Shell Command Output Preservation in Session Restore** — `buildAgentMessagesSnapshot` now converts shell-`*` tool_call+result pairs into `Role:"user"` messages instead of stripping them, preserving `!command` shell output in the LLM history across session restores. This avoids invalid DeepSeek message ordering (synthesized assistant tool_calls before first user message) and prevents orphaned tool results from contaminating the message stream. Locked in by `TestBuildAgentMessagesSnapshotKeepsShellOutputSeparate`.
- **`/context` Discovery-Aware Labels** — `/context` now shows `"Skill catalog (not pre-injected — discovery active)"` when discovery is enabled, and splits attached skills from MCP tools in the Discovery section. Context budget excludes skill catalog tokens when discovery is active, reflecting the net savings from gating.
- **`/discover status` Split Output** — `/discover status` now displays attached skills and MCP tools as separate lists with per-category counts, making it easier to see what's actually attached.
- **Single-width ASCII Status Symbols** — Replaced all double-width emoji used as inline status prefixes (`📄`, `🔧`, `📕`, `✨`, `🌐`, `⚡`, `🧠`, `📦`, `✅`, `📋`, `🔗`, `📎`, `🗑️`, `💬`, `🤖`, `🔖`) with single-width ASCII symbols (`▸`, `⚙`, `✦`, `⊕`, `›`, `◆`, `▣`, `✓`, `≡`, `~`, `+`, `∅`, `»`, `@`, `§`) across `kindIcon()`, CLI summary output (`summary.go`), LSP format helpers (`lsp_format.go`), git model status messages, TUI hints/notices, and the agent detail view. New `AGENTS.md` rule codifies the constraint. Prevents text alignment issues in VS Code terminal on macOS.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.5 → 0.6.6.

### Fixed
- **Discovery Corpus Warm Synchronous + Timeout** — `RunDiscovery` now calls `Warm` synchronously with a 500ms deadline instead of spawning an async background goroutine. This ensures skills are ranked and attached on the very first turn. The timeout bounds first-turn latency on cold embed cache: if warm times out, the call falls through to the allow-all state (avoiding zero-tool attachments) and retries on the next turn. Removed `WarmAsync`, `warmOnce`, and `warming` from `Engine` (dead code after sync migration). Updated `discoveryAllows` comment to clarify that `Ready() == false` now signals a warm failure, not in-progress warming.
- **Shell Command Queuing During Streaming** — `!shell` commands entered while the agent is streaming or compacting are now queued and drained in `drainQueuedCommands` after the stream completes. Extracted the shell-execution setup logic into a reusable `startShellExecution` helper. Prevents loss of user input during concurrent streaming.
- **Bundled Skills FS Registration in `serve` and `web`** — The `serve` and `web` subcommands now register the embedded skills FS and model config FS, matching the registration already present in `skills`, TUI, and other subcommands. Fixes missing skill discovery in the `/api/skills` endpoint and discovery ranking when using the web UI or `serve` subcommand.
- **Version Bump** — 0.6.3 → 0.6.5.
- **Memory Maintenance Client Fallback** — `memoryMaintenanceClient()` now returns the primary LLM client instead of nil when the small model is disabled, empty, or its config is missing. Previously, memory maintenance silently did nothing in these cases.
- **Web UI Base Path Regex** — Fixed regex anchor in `web/index.html` and `web/dist/index.html` (`[^/]+` → `[^/]+$`) for tailscale path prefix detection, preventing partial path matches.
- **Discovery Notice Batch Rendering** — `appendDiscoveryNotice` no longer calls `rerenderTranscriptAndMaybeScroll` internally; the caller now batches notices and re-renders once, eliminating redundant re-renders when multiple log entries arrive simultaneously.
- **Log Promotion Scroll** — Transcript now scrolls correctly after batch-promoting user-facing discovery log entries, using a `promoted` flag to trigger a single scroll pass.
- **Tailscale Cleanup Output Capture** — `removeTailscaleSetPath` now captures stdout/stderr from the tailscale cleanup command, including output in debug logs for easier troubleshooting.
- **Tailscale Stale Sibling Path** — `tailscaleURLWithPathPrefix` now replaces the URL path entirely instead of appending, preventing a stale sibling session's `--set-path` from producing a doubled, unroutable prefix. `stopRCServer` now removes only this session's `--set-path` mount via `removeTailscaleSetPath`. Locked in by `TestTailscaleURLWithPathPrefix`.
- **Local Backend Validation on Discovery Enable** — `setDiscoveryEnabled` skips `discovery.ResolveEmbedder` when the backend is `"local"`, since the local embedder is constructed lazily by the agent (which has process-spawning access). Previously, enabling discovery with the local model would fail at validation time.
- **File Tree Click After Scrolling** — `treeNodeForClick` now accounts for `treeScrollY` when mapping screen row to node index, so clicking on the file tree after scrolling down selects the correct file instead of being off-by-one.
- **Tree Scroll Snap-Back on Wheel/Scrollbar** — `reconcileTreeScroll` now only snaps the view when the cursor actually moved (tracked via `lastScrollCursor`), so wheel/scrollbar scrolling (which leaves the cursor put) never overrides the user's scroll position.
- **Scrollbar Click on Transcript Regions** — Changed `toolOutputForClick`, `thinkingForClick`, and `compactionForClick` to use `m.mainScrollbarX()` instead of `m.panelWidth()`, preventing scrollbar-track/thumb clicks from toggling the transcript region behind the scrollbar. Locked in by `TestClickScrollbarDoesNotToggleToolOutput`.
- **Advisor Over-Exploration** — The advisor system prompt now instructs the model to trust provided context and only explore when genuinely needed, reducing wasteful tool calls during advisory rounds.
- **Read Tool Pagination Hint** — Changed the continue prompt from `limit=50` format to `end_line=` format, matching the tool's declared schema.
- **Context Estimate After Compaction** — `CurrentContextEstimate` now ignores pre-compaction Usage data once a synthetic compaction summary is present, preventing stale large-context values from leaking into the context gauge. New `latestCompactionSummaryIndex` helper. Locked in by `TestCurrentContextEstimateAfterCompaction` and `TestCurrentContextEstimateUsesFreshUsageAfterCompaction`.
- **Scrollbar Drag Stale State** — `handleMouseAction` now defensively clears a stale scrollbar drag on any new press event, preventing viewport drift when the terminal misses the matching release. Also short-circuits the release handler when dragging is (and was) active, avoiding a spurious click fall-through.
- **Context Estimate Non-Cumulative Provider Floor** — `CurrentContextEstimate` now floors per-turn `PromptTokens` against the full message heuristic when the provider reports non-cumulative counts (e.g. minimax-m3 via opencode-go), preventing the context gauge from showing a value below the actual on-disk message cost.
- **Git Files Pane Scroll Offset** — The git files pane now scrolls correctly when the file list exceeds the visible area. New `clampFileListScroll()` method keeps the cursor in view, and `renderFileList()` slices output to the visible window. Applies to both filtered and unfiltered file lists with staged/unstaged headers.
- **Git File List Click — No Staged Files Off-by-One** — Clicking on the first unstaged file when there are no staged files now correctly selects index 0 instead of 1. The row-to-index mapping now handles the single-header layout. Locked in by `TestGitFileListClickNoStagedFiles`.
- **Git File List Click — Filter Bar Offset** — Clicking on file items when the filter is active now accounts for the filter bar row, selecting the correct file instead of being off-by-one. Locked in by `TestGitFileListClickFilterOffset`.
- **Git Log Click — Viewport Offset** — Clicking on the log section now accounts for `commitViewport.YOffset()` when mapping screen row to commit index, so selecting a visible commit after scrolling works correctly. Locked in by `TestGitLogClickAccountsForViewportOffset`.

- **Anthropic Message Merge Fix** — `chatAnthropic` no longer merges consecutive user messages when either contains `tool_result` blocks. Strict Anthropic-compatible providers (e.g. Minimax) reject mixed tool_result + text content in a single user message. New `hasToolResultBlock` helper detects tool_result blocks in content arrays.
- **Heredoc-Aware Bash Parsing** — `parseShellCommandLine` now strips heredoc bodies before tokenizing compound commands, preventing content lines (e.g. Go source inside `cat > file << 'EOF'`) from being incorrectly split as separate shell commands by the newline→';' rule.
- **Patch Tool Error Context** — When `deriveNewContents` fails to find expected lines or context in a file, the error message now includes nearby file content (up to 5 lines around the search position) and the expected patch lines, making it much easier to diagnose patch failures. New `getNearbyLines()` helper formats the snippet.
- **Grep Pattern Misread as Path** — `extractBashCommandPaths` now treats the first positional arg of `grep`/`rg` as the search pattern, not a filesystem path. Previously `grep -r "/review" /path/in/root` triggered a spurious "out of scope path" permission prompt because the leading-slash pattern looked like an absolute path. Locked in by `TestPermissions_GrepSlashPatternNotTreatedAsPath`.
- **Bash Env-Var/Redirection Scope** — `decideSingleCommand` now uses `isWithinAllowedScope` (instead of `isWithinWorkDir` + temp-dir fallback) for env-var and redirection path checks, so a path persisted to `extra_allowed_paths` via "always allow this path" no longer re-prompts on the next bash invocation. Also covers cache roots and the global data dir. Locked in by `TestPermissions_BashEnvVarInExtraAllowedPath_AutoAllows`, `TestPermissions_BashRedirectionInExtraAllowedPath_AutoAllows`, and `TestPermissions_FirstOutOfScopePath_ExtraAllowedPath_ReturnsEmpty`.
- **Read Tool on Extra Allowed Paths** — `read` and `glob` now allow paths inside `extra_allowed_paths` (and the cache/data-dir roots) without prompting, matching the in-bash auto-allow. Locked in by `TestPermissions_ReadToolOnExtraAllowedPath_Allows`.
- **Web Shell `exit status 1` Echo** — `/api/shell` no longer returns `err.Error()` for `*exec.ExitError`; the exit code is already in `exitCode`, and the web UI prepends its own "exit code N" message, so the previous behavior produced a duplicated line. Locked in by `TestRun_NonZeroExit`.
- **Mobile Sidebar Mid-Interaction Close** — The web UI's media-query handler now only auto-closes the sidebar on the *rising* edge (desktop → mobile), not on every breakpoint change. Resizing the window mobile → desktop → mobile no longer snaps a deliberately open sidebar shut.
- **Concurrent Tailscale Sessions** — `/rc` now uses `tailscale serve --set-path /<sessionID>` so multiple ocode instances can expose the same node without overwriting each other's tailscale config. The `tailscaleReset()` global teardown was removed from `stopRCServer` for the same reason. `sanitizeTailscalePath` strips `/`, `.`, and non-ASCII from sessionIDs so embedded separators can't route to a sibling path.

### Added
- **Desktop Bell Fallback (non-macOS)** — Non-macOS systems now fall back to `beeep.Beep(440, 200)` when the terminal doesn't support the OSC 9 desktop bell sequence, providing audible notifications on Linux/Windows. New import `github.com/gen2brain/beeep`.
- **Terminal Detection Expansion** — `supportsDesktopBell()` now recognizes kitty, wezterm, foot, Windows Terminal, contour, and rio via both `TERM_PROGRAM` and `TERM` environment variables, enabling desktop bell notifications on those terminals.
- **`/sound` Command** — New `/sound [on|off|test]` slash command toggles the terminal bell on task completion. Enabled by default. `ringBell()` writes the BEL character safely in alt-screen mode. Wired as an instant (non-queued) command.
- **`/sound` Enhancements** — `/sound test` dispatches a bell notification; no-args shows current status. Desktop bell (OSC 9 escape sequence) for Ghostty and iTerm2. macOS system beep for Apple Terminal. `bellNotifier` field enables test injection. New `defaultBellNotifier`, `bellNotificationPayload`, `supportsDesktopBell`, `macOSSystemBeep` helpers. Locked in by `TestHandleSoundCmdReportsStatusAndToggles` and `TestHandleSoundCmdTestDispatchesBell`.
- **Version Bump** — 0.5.12 → 0.5.13.
- **Version Bump** — 0.5.11 → 0.5.12.
- **In-Chat Search (`/search`)** — New `/search <query>` slash command (alias `/find`) opens an in-chat find bar with Ctrl+F support. Case-insensitive substring matching across all message types (text, tool calls, tool results, reasoning). Highlights matching messages and jumps to them. New `internal/tui/chat_search.go` with full test coverage. New `SearchHit` theme style.
- **Inline URL Links** — New `internal/tui/urllink.go` detects raw URLs and markdown links in LLM output, rendering them as clickable link regions. New `renderMarkdownInLine` helper replaces `renderMarkdownBold` in the agent detail view for URL-aware rendering.
- **`/add-dir` Command** — New `/add-dir <path>` slash command (alias `/add-dirs`) adds a directory to `extra_allowed_paths` so the agent can read and write files there without re-prompting.
- **Reference Catalog in Context** — New `BuildReferenceCatalog` in `internal/agent/context.go` generates a catalog of available slash commands, skills, and agents for the LLM context. Loaded at session start alongside the skill catalog.
- **Session WorkDir Override** — `session.SetWorkDir()` overrides `os.Getwd()` for project slug resolution, so session storage follows the TUI's explicit `workDir` instead of the process-wide CWD. Fixes `/cd` and `-dir` changing the working directory without the session package noticing.
